Method: 提出ViASNet模型，基于3D U-Net架构，融合音频特征和场景语义信息，生成动态显著性图。使用151个视频广告（每广告约20名被试）的眼动追踪数据评估模型性能，并通过消融实验分析关键影响因素。采用逐帧显著性图熵作为诊断工具，评估广告及场景的观众参与度。

Result: ViASNet在151个广告数据集上取得良好性能，消融实验揭示了影响模型表现的关键因素。熵诊断工具在15个未见广告上成功识别出未能吸引观众注意力的广告和场景。

Conclusion: 基于ViASNet等深度显著性模型的自动化系统可显著加速广告设计与测试过程，为短视频广告优化提供有效工具。

Abstract: The digital media landscape has seen a pervasive shift toward short-form video advertising on TV, social media and e-commerce platforms. The present study focuses on deep saliency prediction for short-form video advertising. Deep saliency models have been used to generate predictions of human eye fixation patterns with the purpose of enhancing user interaction with digital technology and optimizing its design. For video ads, dynamic saliency maps capture where and when viewers are looking, revealing why video ads are effective, and how their content should be optimized. We develop and test a new deep dynamic saliency prediction model called ViASNet (Video Ad Saliency Network), which has an architecture founded on the 3D U-Net, and accommodates the influence of audio and the semantic meaning of scenes. We assess the model's performance on 151 video ads, each seen by about 20 viewers wile their eye movements were tracked, and explore the critical factors influencing model performance through ablation experiments. We calculate the entropy of the predicted saliency maps frame-by-frame as a diagnostic tool to identify ads and scenes that fail to engage viewers, and illustrate its use on test data of 15 unseen ads. Our study reveals that ad design and testing can be sped up considerably through automated systems built on deep saliency models such as ViASNet.

</details>


### [110] [CapTalk: Text-Guided Stylization and Speech-Driven 3D Head Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29316)
*Xuangeng Chu,Yuan Gan,Ziteng Cui,Shuhong Liu,Jian Wang,Bing Zhou,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于音频驱动的3D面部动画生成框架，通过文本描述分别控制说话风格和情感，支持动态情感变化，生成同步唇形和面部表情。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖预定义的身份或风格潜在特征，用户无法自由控制说话风格；且对整段音频施加固定风格/身份导致面部动画风格无法适应音频情感内容。

Method: 重新审视风格与情感的纠缠关系；构建包含风格和情感文本描述的大规模数据集；提出新框架，输入包括说话风格文本描述、角色情感文本描述以及驱动音频流，实现实时生成。

Result: 能够实时生成高度同步的唇形和面部表情，支持推理过程中动态情感控制，适应目标情感在语音中变化的情景。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了风格和情感的分离控制，克服了现有方法在说话风格可控性和情感适应性上的局限。

Abstract: Audio-driven 3D facial animation aims to generate synchronized lip movements and vivid facial expressions from arbitrary audio clips. While existing methods can produce synchronized lip motions, they often rely on predefined identity or style latent features, which limits users' ability to freely control speaking styles. Moreover, applying a fixed style or identity to an entire audio segment typically results in facial animation styles that do not adapt to the emotional content of the audio. To address these challenges, we revisit the entanglement between style and emotion, construct a large-scale dataset with textual descriptions of both style and emotion, and propose a novel talking head generation framework that enables separate control over style and emotion. Our model takes as input both textual descriptions of speaking style and character emotion, as well as the driving audio stream, enabling real-time generation of highly synchronized lip movements and facial expressions that match the provided descriptions. Furthermore, our model supports dynamic emotion control during inference, allowing it to handle scenarios where the target emotion changes throughout the speech.

</details>


### [111] [Multi-Stage VLM Pipeline for Zero-Shot Traffic Accident Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29325)
*Fumiya Tatematsu,Fumihiko Takahashi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了ACCIDENT挑战赛（CVPR 2026 AUTOPILOT Workshop）的第一名解决方案，使用冻结的Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct和235B MoE模型构建三阶段流水线，实现了事故时间、碰撞中心点和碰撞类型的零样本预测，最终得分0.57080，比最强基线（Molmo-7B, 0.358）高出约0.21。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决CCTV监控视频中事故时序定位、碰撞中心点定位和碰撞类型分类的零样本预测问题，挑战现有视觉语言模型在多任务细粒度理解上的能力上限。

Method: 三阶段流水线：(1) 全视频联合预测事故时间和碰撞类型；(2) 时间细化；(3) 单帧碰撞中心点定位。在32B和235B MoE两个模型上各执行一次，9:1比例融合输出，最后将预测点吸附到最近的车辆检测结果上。

Result: Public LB 0.55469 / Private LB 0.57080，比最强基线Molmo-7B（0.358）提升约0.21，赢得挑战赛第一名。

Conclusion: 双模型融合、三阶段流水线设计及后处理吸附策略可显著提升零样本事故预测性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: We present the 1st-place solution to the ACCIDENT challenge at the CVPR 2026 AUTOPILOT Workshop, which asks for zero-shot prediction of accident timing, impact centroid, and collision type from CCTV footage. On a frozen Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct checkpoint we build a three-stage pipeline (full-video joint prediction, time refinement, and single-frame grounding of the impact centroid), run the same pipeline a second time on a 235B Mixture-of-Experts sibling, blend the two outputs 9:1, and finally snap each predicted point onto the nearest vehicle detection. The final system reaches Public LB 0.55469 / Private LB 0.57080, roughly +0.21 over the strongest host baseline (Molmo-7B, 0.358) and wins the challenge. We ablate each component, report the negative results that shaped the final design, and release the code at https://github.com/fuumin621/cvpr2026-accident-1st-place-solution.

</details>


### [112] [EarthShift: a benchmark for measuring robustness to real-world distribution shifts in Earth observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29330)
*Kelsey Doerksen,Hannah Kerner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EarthShift是首个用于评估遥感领域多个真实分布偏移下模型鲁棒性的公开测试平台。在8个地理空间基础模型和11个任务上的实验表明，无论模型架构、规模、预训练或微调策略如何，模型在分布外场景下平均性能下降15-20%，提示该领域需要关注分布鲁棒性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地球观测基准仅关注分布内（in-distribution）的泛化性能，但实际部署时模型需应对时间、地理、尺度、传感器等多种分布外（out-of-distribution）场景，缺乏系统评估鲁棒性的公开测试平台。

Method: 构建EarthShift测试平台，通过配对数据集覆盖5种分布偏移类型（不同数据源、时间窗口、地理位置、传感器等），在8个地理空间基础模型和11个任务上比较分布内与分布外性能。

Result: 地理空间基础模型在分布外场景下平均性能下降15-20%，该趋势不受模型架构、规模、预训练或微调策略影响；其鲁棒性与通用视觉基础模型及全监督模型相当。

Conclusion: 当前地理空间基础模型在分布鲁棒性方面存在显著不足，未来研究应致力于提升分布外泛化能力而非仅关注性能指标；EarthShift可作为该方向的标准化测试基准。

Abstract: Current Earth observation benchmarks focus on measuring performance on diverse tasks and applications, typically measuring generalization in-distribution. But when models are deployed, they must generalize to myriad out-of-distribution scenarios, such as new time periods, geographies, scales, and sensors. We introduce EarthShift: the first public testbed for benchmarking robustness across multiple realistic distribution shifts encountered in remote sensing. EarthShift enables users to measure distributional robustness by comparing performance in- and out-of-distribution using datasets from paired datasets from different sources, temporal windows, geographic locations, and sensors. Our experiments on 8 geospatial foundation models (GFMs) and 11 tasks covering 5 shift types show that GFMs consistently perform 15-20% worse out-of-distribution on average regardless of model architecture, size, pre-training or fine-tuning strategy. We show that GFM robustness is similar to that of generic vision foundation models, and even fully-supervised models. This highlights a need for future research to strive for improvements in distributional robustness, not just performance, which can be benchmarked using EarthShift. We release our code and datasets to provide a testbed to guide future work to create foundation models that are robust and reliable in real-world applications. Code and data for EarthShift are available at: https://earthshift.github.io

</details>


### [113] [Rethinking FID Through the Geometry of the Reference Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29335)
*Yunghee Lee,Byeonghyun Pak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FID评估图像生成器时存在与参考数据集几何结构相关的偏差：集中型数据集上FID趋势更可靠，而分散型数据集上FID可能随样本质量提升反而恶化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示FID在评估图像生成器时与样本质量不一致的根源，指出该问题部分由参考数据集的几何结构（密度和有效秩）导致。

Method: 在六个数据集上进行受控实验，分析分布密度和有效秩对FID变化趋势的影响，并通过精确率/召回率归因以及替代特征空间和距离的消融实验验证结论。

Result: 数据集的集中程度越高，FID趋势越有利于反映样本质量提升；数据集越分散，FID越可能出现随样本质量提升反而恶化的情况。

Conclusion: 分布度量指标应与参考数据集的几何结构结合解读，以实现更可靠的基准评估。

Abstract: Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) is widely used to evaluate image generators, yet lower FID does not always correspond to better sample quality. We show that this mismatch depends in part on the geometry of the reference dataset. In a controlled study across six datasets, distributional density and effective rank significantly explain how FID changes as sample quality improves. Concentrated datasets tend to yield more favorable FID trends, whereas more dispersed datasets can make FID worsen despite better samples. Attribution to precision and recall and ablations with alternative feature spaces and distances support the same conclusion. These results suggest that distributional metrics should be interpreted together with the geometry of the reference dataset for more reliable benchmarking.

</details>


### [114] [DMC-CF: Dynamic Multimodal CounterFactual QA benchmark for Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29339)
*Junzhe Zhang,Huixuan Zhang,Guirong Wang,Xingyao Zhang,Pei Liu,Lin Qu,Hu Wei,Xiaojun Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了DMC-CF-Static和DMC-CF-Dynamic两个多模态因果反事实推理基准，评估发现当前多模态大模型在真实世界场景中的因果推理能力仍需大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果推理数据集规模有限，且多基于合成图像/视频、卡通等非真实多模态数据构建，缺乏基于真实世界视频的大规模基准。

Method: 收集真实世界视频构建静态基准DMC-CF-Static；提出动态图干预（DGI）框架，用因果图表示因果事件，从静态基准构建动态评估基准DMC-CF-Dynamic，以缓解数据污染问题。

Result: 在DMC-CF（含静态和动态评估基准）上的实验结果表明，当前多模态大模型在真实世界场景中的多模态因果推理能力仍需大幅改进。

Conclusion: 现有MLLMs在真实世界多模态因果反事实推理任务上表现不足，亟需进一步提升。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), models have demonstrated increasingly powerful multimodal capabilities. However, whether MLLMs trained through statistical learning can truly understand the causal relationships underlying the real world remains a key research question. In recent years, numerous multimodal causal reasoning datasets have been proposed. Nevertheless, these datasets are either limited in scale or constructed from synthetic images and videos, cartoon-based content, or other non-realistic multimodal sources. To address these limitations, we collect real-world videos and construct DMC-CF-Static, a large-scale benchmark for multimodal causal counterfactual reasoning. Furthermore, to mitigate issues such as data contamination in traditional static evaluation, we represent causal events using causal graphs and propose the Dynamic Graph Intervention (DGI) framework to build the dynamic evaluation benchmark DMC-CF-Dynamic from DMC-CF-Static. Experimental results on the overall DMC-CF, which includes both static and dynamic evaluation benchmarks, demonstrate that the multimodal causal reasoning capabilities of current multimodal large language models in real-world scenarios still require substantial improvement.

</details>


### [115] [Orthogonal Negative Guidance in Attention Feature Space for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29390)
*Jungmin Ko,Jungwon Park,Jimyeong Kim,Changin Choi,Wonseok Lee,Wonjong Rhee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出正交负引导方法（Orthogonal Negative Guidance），在注意力特征空间中对负提示特征进行正交化处理，仅减去正交分量，实现训练-free的概念抑制，在FLUX模型上达到更优的抑制效果与图像质量平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I模型虽然能生成高质量图像，但在显式抑制指定对象或属性方面仍然困难，已有方法（如提示否定、后编辑、负引导）在概念抑制上效果不足，常导致目标概念未移除或图像质量下降。

Method: 提出Orthogonal Negative Guidance方法，在MM-DiT架构的T2I变换器的注意力输出空间中，将负提示注意力特征相对于正提示特征进行正交化，仅减去正交分量，从而抑制不需要的概念同时保留期望语义，无需训练。

Result: 在FLUX-dev和FLUX-schnell上实验表明，该方法在概念抑制、提示对齐和图像质量之间取得更优平衡；人工评估中比第二好的基线高出18.78%；还支持多概念抑制和可调概念抑制。

Conclusion: 正交负引导是一种有效的训练-free概念抑制方法，通过注意力特征空间中的正交化操作，在保持图像质量的同时实现精准的概念消除。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models have become increasingly capable of generating high-quality images. Yet, enforcing the explicit absence of a specified object or attribute remains a fundamentally challenging problem. Existing approaches, including prompt negation, post-hoc editing, and negative guidance, remain insufficient for explicit concept suppression, often failing to remove the target concept or degrading overall image quality. To this end, we propose Orthogonal Negative Guidance in attention feature space, a training-free method that operates in the attention output space of MM-DiT-based T2I transformers. Our method orthogonalizes negative-prompt attention features with respect to positive-prompt features and subtracts only the orthogonal component, suppressing unwanted concepts while preserving desired semantics. Experiments on FLUX-dev and FLUX-schnell show that our method achieves favorable trade-offs between concept suppression, prompt alignment, and image quality. In human evaluation, our method outperforms the second-best baseline by 18.78%. We further show that our method supports multi-concept suppression and adjustable concept suppression.

</details>


### [116] [Semantic and Visual Evidence for Efficient Long-Video Reasoning: A Solution for the HD-EPIC VQA Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29402)
*Yinsong Xu,Wei Jing,Liuxin Zhang,Wanjun Lv,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一框架，通过解耦长视频推理为语义证据（全局程序结构）和视觉证据（细粒度目标级定位），并采用查询条件化的证据检索与整合机制，在HD-EPIC-VQA挑战中取得了竞争力表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在处理长格式第一人称视频时受限于上下文长度和对细粒度视觉细节的定位不足，HD-EPIC基准测试表明即使是强长上下文模型在多样化视频问答任务上表现也相对较低。

Method: 提出一个统一框架，将长视频推理解耦为两种互补的证据形式：1) 语义证据，通过从粗到细的提取流水线捕获全局程序结构；2) 目标级视觉证据，通过边界框和视觉嵌入保留细粒度定位。推理时，将推理过程公式化为查询条件化的证据检索与整合，动态从两种来源选择相关信息。

Result: 在HD-EPIC-VQA挑战的多个任务类别上取得了具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 明确地结构、检索和整合语义与视觉证据对于MLLM实现有效的长视频理解至关重要。

Abstract: Understanding long-form egocentric videos remains challenging for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) due to limited context length and insufficient grounding of fine-grained visual details. The recently proposed HD-EPIC benchmark highlights these limitations: even strong long-context models achieve relatively low performance across diverse video question answering tasks. In this paper, we propose a unified framework that decouples long-video reasoning into two complementary forms of evidence: semantic evidence and visual evidence. Semantic evidence captures global procedural structure through a coarse-to-fine extraction pipeline, while object-centric visual evidence preserves fine-grained grounding through bounding boxes and visual embeddings. During inference, we formulate reasoning as a query-conditioned evidence retrieval and integration process, dynamically selecting relevant information from both sources. Our approach achieves competitive performance in the HD-EPIC-VQA Challenge across multiple task categories. More broadly, our results demonstrate that explicitly structuring, retrieving, and integrating semantic and visual evidence is critical for effective long-video understanding with MLLMs.

</details>


### [117] [One Click per Cell Type Suffices: Training-free Group Interaction for Cell Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29429)
*Sanghyun Jo,Seo Jin Lee,Seohyung Hong,Yoorim Gang,Hyeongsub Kim,Hyungseok Seo,Kyungsu Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Chain-of-Prompts (CoP)框架，通过分组提示范式将交互式分割从每实例O(N)复杂度降低到每类型O(T)，利用SAM编码器已聚类的特性，无需训练即可用每类型一次点击保留90%以上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的细胞实例分割模型在分布外的细胞类型上性能严重下降；虽然交互式基础模型通过逐实例提示能解决这一问题，但在组织病理学图像中（包含数百到数千个密集实例）成本过高。

Method: 提出Group Prompting新范式，将交互式分割从每实例O(N)转为每类型O(T)。具体提出Chain-of-Prompts (CoP)框架，利用冻结的SAM图像编码器已在特征空间中聚类同类型细胞这一特性，通过(1)非参数门控多尺度编码器特征识别可靠的同类型位置，(2)选择最远的可靠点作为下一提示以最大化覆盖，递归扩展单次用户点击。

Result: 在三个细胞类型标注基准上，CoP每类型一次点击保留了超过90%的逐实例性能，且无需额外训练即超越全监督方法。在四个形态同质基准上，一次点击保留超过99%性能。

Conclusion: CoP是一种无需训练、高效且有效的细胞分割框架，通过利用SAM特征空间的固有聚类特性，大幅降低交互成本同时保持高分割质量。

Abstract: Cell instance segmentation models trained on cell-specific datasets suffer severe performance drops on out-of-distribution cell types, while interactive foundation models overcome this through per-instance prompting at a cost that is prohibitively expensive for histopathology images containing hundreds to thousands of densely packed instances. We introduce Group Prompting, a new paradigm that shifts interactive segmentation from per-instance $O(N)$ to per-type $O(T)$, where a single click per cell type suffices to segment all instances of that type. Our key observation is that the frozen image encoder of the Segment Anything Model (SAM) already clusters same-type cells in its feature space before any prompt is given. Exploiting this property, we propose Chain-of-Prompts (CoP), a training-free framework that recursively expands a single user click by (1) identifying reliable same-type locations through non-parametric gating of multi-scale encoder features, and (2) selecting the most spatially distant reliable point as the next prompt to maximize coverage. On three cell-type-annotated benchmarks, CoP with one click per type retains over 90% of per-instance performance and surpasses fully-supervised methods without any additional training. On four morphologically homogeneous benchmarks, a single click retains over 99%. Project Page: https://shjo-april.github.io/Chain-of-Prompts/

</details>


### [118] [Recovering Policy-Induced Errors: Benchmarking and Trajectory Synthesis for Robust GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29447)
*Tianpeng Bu,Xin Liu,Qihua Chen,Hao Jiang,Shurui Li,Hongtao Duan,Lu Jiang,Lulu Hu,Bin Yang,Minying Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GUI-RobustEval评估基准（含1,216个可执行测试用例）和RoTS数据合成框架（生成80万高质量数据），通过树状管道主动发现错误模式并合成恢复步骤。微调后的RoTS-7B和RoTS-32B在GUI鲁棒性评估和传统基准上均取得显著提升，其中RoTS-32B在OSWorld上达到47.4%成功率，实现最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI智能体缺乏从自身错误中恢复的鲁棒性，这阻碍了其在实际场景中的部署。目前缺乏系统评估错误恢复能力的基准和高质量训练数据。

Method: 1) GUI-RobustEval：包含1,216个可执行测试用例，系统覆盖广泛的错误模式；2) RoTS：基于树的合成管道，主动发现多样化错误模式并合成对应的恢复步骤，生成80万高质量训练数据；3) 在合成数据上微调得到RoTS-7B和RoTS-32B模型。

Result: RoTS-32B在OSWorld上达到47.4%成功率，All-Pass@4得分为33.8%，取得最先进水平。两个模型在GUI-RobustEval和传统GUI基准上均表现显著提升。

Conclusion: 提升长程错误恢复能力不仅增强了鲁棒性，也全面提升了整体性能，表明错误恢复能力是GUI智能体实际部署的关键因素。

Abstract: While GUI agents have advanced rapidly, they often lack the robustness to recover from their own errors, hindering real-world deployment. To bridge this gap at both the evaluation and data levels, we introduce GUI-RobustEval and propose Robustness-driven Trajectory Synthesis. GUI-RobustEval contains $1,216$ executable test cases that systematically measure error recovery capabilities across a broad and realistic spectrum of error modes. At the data level, RoTS is a scalable synthesis framework that creates $800k$ high-quality data via a tree-based pipeline that proactively discovers diverse error modes and synthesizes corresponding recovery steps. Our two models, RoTS-7B and RoTS-32B, fine-tuned on our dataset, both demonstrate significant gains on GUI-RobustEval and traditional GUI benchmarks. Notably, RoTS-32B achieves state-of-the-art performance on OSWorld, with a $47.4\%$ success rate and a $33.8\%$ All-Pass@4 score, suggesting that improved long-horizon error recovery ability contributes to both robustness and overall performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/AlibabaResearch/RoTS.

</details>


### [119] [Comparative evaluation of photogrammetric reconstruction methods and 3D Gaussian Splatting for road surface roughness analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29452)
*Marouane Elmegdar,Teng Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究比较了COLMAP、Meshroom、Metashape和3DGS四种三维重建流程从智能手机图像估算路面粗糙度的能力。COLMAP对微观纹理最敏感，Meshroom重构平衡，Metashape几何最平滑，3DGS能捕捉可见不规则但噪声较高。开源管线在相对粗糙度评估中具有可行性，为低成本路面监测提供了实用方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像级三维重建为传统基于传感器的路面评估技术提供了低成本替代方案，本研究旨在比较不同重建管线在路面粗糙度估算方面的表现。

Method: 使用COLMAP、Meshroom、Metashape和3DGS四种管线从智能手机图像进行三维重建，所有点云在CloudCompare中通过统一的流程处理，包括方向对齐、分割、法线估计以及0.2、0.4、0.6模型单位邻域半径下的粗糙度计算。

Result: COLMAP对微观纹理最敏感；Meshroom重构平衡、粗糙度变化适中；Metashape因内部滤波产生最平滑的几何；3DGS能捕捉可见不规则性但噪声较高且密度较低。

Conclusion: 开源三维重建管线用于相对粗糙度评估是可行的，为低成本路面监测提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Image-based 3D reconstruction offers a low-cost alternative to traditional sensor-based techniques for road surface assessment. This study compares four reconstruction pipelines--COLMAP, Meshroom, Metashape, and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS)--to evaluate their ability to estimate road surface roughness from smartphone imagery. All point clouds were processed in CloudCompare using a consistent workflow involving orientation alignment, segmentation, normal estimation, and roughness computation at neighborhood radiuses of 0.2, 0.4, and 0.6 model units. The results show that COLMAP provides the highest sensitivity to micro-texture, while Meshroom yields balanced reconstructions with moderate roughness variation. Metashape produces the smoothest geometry due to its internal filtering, and 3DGS captures visible irregularities but exhibits higher noise and lower density. The comparison demonstrates that open-source pipelines are viable for relative roughness evaluation, offering a practical approach for low-cost pavement monitoring.

</details>


### [120] [Uni-RCM: Unified Reference-guided Cross-modal Mapping for Multi-Class Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29455)
*Yangchen Wu,Huiqiang Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Uni-RCM框架，通过参考引导模块和离线残差量化器，实现了多类别工业异常检测的统一范式，在MVTec-3D AD数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多模态工业异常检测依赖每个产品类别的独立模型，缺乏可扩展性；统一模型处理多类别时因类间干扰和特征流形混淆导致检测精度下降。

Method: 提出Uni-RCM框架，包含两个核心模块：1) 参考引导块(Reference Guide Block)，通过可学习参考特征动态过滤类别特定噪声，捕获跨模态共性；2) 离线残差量化器(Offline Residual Quantizer)，利用多级联码本刻画正常分布。

Result: 在MVTec-3D AD数据集上，在图像级检测和像素级定位任务中均取得最先进性能，特别是在挑战性的多类别设置下。

Conclusion: Uni-RCM有效解决了统一多类别异常检测中的类间干扰问题，为工业异常检测的可扩展部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Multi-modal industrial anomaly detection typically relies on separate models for each product category, fundamentally limiting practical scalability. When shifting to a unified paradigm that handles diverse classes simultaneously, detection accuracy often degrades due to inter-class interference and feature manifold confusion. To overcome these challenges, we propose a Unified Reference guided Cross-modal Mapping framework, named Uni-RCM. At its core, we propose a reference guide block to dynamically filter out category-specific noise by introducing a learnable reference feature, which captures the commonalities across different modalities. Besides, an offline residual quantizer is proposed to characterize the normal distribution by multiple cascaded codebooks. Extensive evaluations on the MVTec-3D AD dataset demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance in the challenging multi-class setting and in terms of image-level detection and pixel-level localization.

</details>


### [121] [FedSmoothLoRA: Toward Smoother and Faster Convergence in Federated Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29460)
*Zehao Wang,Guanglei Yang,Yihan Zeng,Hang Xu,Hongzhi Zhang,Wangmeng Zuo,Chun-Mei Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FedSmoothLoRA框架，通过Round-Matching矩阵和Gradient-Aligned矩阵解决联邦LoRA调优中更新空间受限、轮次间状态不匹配和客户端初始化不一致三个问题，实现更平滑快速的收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接结合FedAvg和LoRA存在三个关键问题：有限的更新空间限制模型有效学习能力；轮次间状态不匹配破坏跨轮次局部优化连续性；客户端无关的起始状态减缓局部收敛。现有方法仅缓解了更新空间受限问题，但后两个问题仍未充分解决。

Method: FedSmoothLoRA在每个通信轮次使用两个矩阵构建局部LoRA初始化：Round-Matching矩阵保持跨轮次局部状态连续性，Gradient-Aligned矩阵通过局部数据估计的梯度信号提供客户端特定的优化引导。

Result: 在图像分类和自然语言生成任务上的大量实验表明，FedSmoothLoRA持续优于现有的联邦LoRA调优方法。

Conclusion: FedSmoothLoRA通过保留扩大后的更新空间、改善跨轮次局部优化连续性、提供客户端感知的起始状态，实现了更平滑和更快的收敛，有效解决了FedAvg与LoRA结合时的三个核心问题。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning of foundation models with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) provides an efficient solution for reducing communication and computation costs while preserving data locality. However, the direct combination of FedAvg and LoRA suffers from three key issues: limited update space, which restricts the model's effective learning capacity; inter-round state mismatch, which disrupts cross-round local optimization continuity; and a client-agnostic starting state, which slows local convergence on clients. Although recent methods mitigate the limited update space issue by merging LoRA updates into the backbone across communication rounds, inter-round state mismatch and the client-agnostic starting state remain insufficiently addressed. To address these issues, we propose FedSmoothLoRA, a federated LoRA tuning framework that preserves the enlarged update space, improves cross-round local optimization continuity, and provides a client-aware starting state for local training. At each communication round, FedSmoothLoRA constructs the local LoRA initialization using two matrices: a Round-Matching matrix that preserves cross-round local state continuity, and a Gradient-Aligned matrix that provides client-specific optimization guidance from gradient signals estimated on local data. Together, these designs enable smoother and faster convergence. Extensive experiments on image classification and natural language generation tasks demonstrate that FedSmoothLoRA consistently outperforms existing federated LoRA tuning methods. Code: https://github.com/wangzehao0704/FedSmoothLoRA

</details>


### [122] [FlowSeg: Dynamic Semantic Guidance for LLM-Conditioned Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29461)
*Zekang Zhang,Guangyu Gao,Youyun Tang,ChengJing Wu,Xiaochao Qu,Chi Harold Liu,Jianbo Jiao,Yunchao Wei,Luoqi Liu,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowSeg通过双向语义流机制，在迭代掩码生成过程中动态融合语言条件与视觉信息，解决了现有分割方法中语言-掩码语义错位的问题，在指代表达分割和推理分割任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM条件分割方法采用"先提议后选择"流程，语言语义仅作为静态提示或事后匹配信号，未参与迭代掩码生成过程，导致生成的高质量掩码候选与语言条件不匹配。作者通过系统分析发现许多错误源于语义错位而非掩码质量差。

Method: 提出FlowSeg，核心是双向语义流机制：(1) 语言条件在生成各阶段主动引导掩码细化；(2) 条件嵌入通过涌现的视觉证据逐步更新。同时引入轻量级边界感知细化模块，选择性增强不确定区域而不扰动确信内部区域。

Result: 在指代表达分割和推理分割任务上，FlowSeg持续改善语言-掩码对齐，达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 双向语义流实现了语义对齐的掩码表示和视觉对齐的语言条件，实现了更可靠的匹配，有效解决了语义错位问题。

Abstract: LLM-conditioned segmentation has recently advanced rapidly by coupling large language models with iterative mask generation frameworks. However, we identify a persistent failure mode in current propose-then-select pipelines. Although high-quality mask candidates are often generated, the final prediction may fail to match the given linguistic condition. This failure arises because language semantics are typically used as static prompts or post-hoc matching signals, rather than participating in the iterative mask generation process. Through systematic analysis, we show that many errors stem from semantic misalignment rather than poor mask quality. To address this issue, we propose FlowSeg, which introduces dynamic semantic guidance via a bidirectional semantic flow between intermediate decoding states and LLM-derived condition embeddings throughout the generation process. Language conditions actively guide mask refinement at each stage, while condition embeddings are progressively updated by emerging visual evidence. This design yields semantically grounded mask representations and visually aligned language conditions, enabling more reliable matching. We further incorporate a lightweight boundary-aware refinement to selectively enhance uncertain regions without perturbing confident interiors. Extensive experiments on referring expression segmentation and reasoning segmentation tasks demonstrate that FlowSeg consistently improves language-mask alignment and achieves state-of-the-art performance. Project page: https://zkzhang98.github.io/FlowSeg_page

</details>


### [123] [Benchmarking Large Vision-Language Models on CFMME: A Comprehensive Chinese Financial Multimodal Evaluation Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29462)
*Qian Chen,Xianyin Zhang,Yanzhi Liu,Lifan Guo,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CFMME，一个中文金融多模态评估基准，包含6,052个实例，覆盖8种金融图像模态和4种核心多模态任务，用于评估大型视觉语言模型在金融业务流程中的感知、理解、推理和认知能力。实验表明最佳模型在问答任务上准确率仅66.11%，检测等任务平均分77.18，说明当前LVLMs仍有较大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型视觉语言模型评估缺乏针对中文金融领域、覆盖完整业务流程的多模态基准，无法全面评估模型在真实金融场景中的感知、理解、推理和认知能力。

Method: 构建CFMME基准，包含6,052个实例，涵盖8种金融图像模态（如票据、报表、图表等）和4种核心多模态任务（问答、检测、识别、信息抽取），在中文金融业务全流程场景下对代表性LVLMs进行系统性评估。

Result: 最先进模型在问答任务上准确率为66.11%，在检测、识别和信息抽取任务上平均得分为77.18，表明当前LVLMs在该领域表现有限。

Conclusion: CFMME揭示了当前LVLMs在中文金融多模态任务上的显著不足，并通过对错误原因、跨模态能力和多方向设置的深入分析，为未来研究提供了有价值的启示和改进方向。

Abstract: The emergence of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) has substantially expanded model capabilities beyond text-only understanding, enabling unified inference across both visual and textual modalities and supporting a broader range of real-world applications. To comprehensively evaluate the perception, understanding, reasoning, and cognition capabilities of LVLMs throughout the entire financial business workflow in Chinese contexts, we introduce CFMME, a novel Chinese financial multimodal evaluation benchmark. CFMME comprises 6,052 instances spanning from fundamental academic knowledge to complex real-world applications, covering eight primary financial image modalities and four core multimodal tasks. On CFMME, we conduct a thorough evaluation of representative LVLMs. The results show that the state-of-the-art model attains an overall accuracy of 66.11\% on the question answering task and an average score of 77.18 on the detection, recognition, and information extraction tasks, indicating substantial room for improvement in current LVLMs. In addition, we conduct detailed analyses of error causes, cross-modal capabilities, and multi-orientation settings, yielding valuable insights for future research. We hope that CFMME will spur further progress in LVLMs, especially by improving their performance on multiple multimodal tasks in the financial domain.

</details>


### [124] [AnyMo: Scaling Any-Modality Conditional Motion Generation with Masked Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29488)
*Yiheng Li,Zhuo Li,Ruibing Hou,Yingjie Chen,Hong Chang,Hao Liu,Shiguang Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniHuMo大规模多模态运动数据集（5000+小时、320万序列）和AnyMo统一框架，结合残差FSQ运动分词器和可扩展掩码建模Transformer，实现任意模态组合下的高质量人体运动生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件运动生成方法受限于固定模态配置和任务特定架构，跨模态交互及多模态条件合成中的扩展规律未被充分探索，且缺乏大规模模态对齐的运动数据。

Method: 构建OmniHuMo数据集（5000+小时、320万序列，含文本/语音/音乐/轨迹等多模态对齐标注）；提出AnyMo框架，包含残差FSQ运动分词器和可扩展掩码建模Transformer，支持任意模态组合的运动合成。

Result: 实验表明AnyMo在多种模态组合下实现高保真运动合成，并提供对空间和风格属性的灵活控制。

Conclusion: 通过大规模多模态数据与统一框架的结合，有效解决了跨模态运动生成的泛化问题，验证了多模态条件合成中的扩展规律。

Abstract: Conditional human motion generation remains a fundamental challenge in computer vision and robotics. Despite significant progress, current methods are often constrained by fixed modality configurations and task-specific architectures, leaving cross-modal interactions and the scaling laws of multimodal-conditioned synthesis largely underexplored. A key bottleneck is the scarcity of large-scale modality-aligned motion data, limiting generalization across diverse control signals. In this work, we introduce OmniHuMo, a large-scale, high-quality dataset comprising over 5,000 hours of motion and 3.2 million sequences with precisely aligned multimodal annotations (e.g., text, speech, music, and trajectory). Leveraging OmniHuMo, we propose AnyMo, a unified multimodal framework combining a Residual FSQ-based motion tokenizer with a scalable masked modeling transformer, enabling high-quality motion synthesis under arbitrary modality combinations. Extensive experiments show that AnyMo achieves high-fidelity synthesis while offering flexible control over both spatial and stylistic attributes.

</details>


### [125] [KGEdit: Ambiguity-Aware Knowledge Graphs for Training-Free Precise Video Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29509)
*Mingshu Cai,Miao Zhang,Chenghe Yang,Yixuan Li,Osamu Yoshie,Yuya Ieiri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KGEdit提出了一种结构化语义控制框架，通过构建模糊感知知识图谱（AAKG）解耦输入提示词为身份、关系、属性和负约束四种语义，并设计结构化语义注入模块（SSIM）和时序感知语义控制模块（TASC），在扩散Transformer中实现细粒度语义控制和时序一致性，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练-free视频生成方法在处理复杂文本指令时存在语义模糊、概念绑定错误和跨帧不一致的问题。

Method: 1) 构建模糊感知知识图谱（AAKG）将输入提示解耦为四种结构化语义；2) 设计结构化语义注入模块（SSIM）将语义信号注入扩散Transformer关键层；3) 引入时序感知语义控制模块（TASC），根据去噪阶段特性动态调度语义目标。

Result: KGEdit在编辑精度和时序稳定性上优于现有方法，在文本驱动交互场景中具有更高的效率和可控性。

Conclusion: KGEdit通过结构化语义控制有效解决了复杂文本指令下的语义歧义和时序不一致问题，为文本到视频生成提供了更可控的框架。

Abstract: In recent years, training-free video generation has progressed remarkably. However, when handling complex textual instructions, existing methods still suffer from semantic ambiguity, incorrect concept binding, and cross-frame inconsistency. To address these issues, we propose KGEdit, a structured semantic control framework for text-to-video (T2V) diffusion models. Specifically, we first construct an ambiguity-aware knowledge graph (AAKG) to disentangle and disambiguate the input prompt, converting it into four types of structured semantics: identity, relation, attribute, and negative constraints. We then design a structured semantic injection module (SSIM) to inject these semantic signals into key layers of the diffusion Transformer, enabling fine-grained semantic control. In addition, we introduce a temporal-aware semantic control (TASC) module that dynamically schedules semantic objectives according to the stage-wise characteristics of the denoising process, further improving semantic alignment and temporal consistency. Experiments show that KGEdit outperforms existing methods in editing precision and temporal stability, while offering higher efficiency and controllability in text-driven interaction scenarios.

</details>


### [126] [GiPL: Generative augmented iterative Pseudo-Labeling for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29539)
*Jiacong Liu,Shu Luo,Yikai Qin,Yaze Zhao,Yongwei Jiang,Yixiong Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GiPL，一种双分支训练框架，通过迭代伪标签自训练和基于大视觉语言模型的生成式数据增强，有效解决了跨域少样本目标检测中支持集利用率不足和过拟合问题，在三个数据集上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言基础模型在跨域少样本目标检测（CD-FSOD）的微调中面临两个关键挑战：一是稀疏的单实例标注导致支持集利用不充分，二是极有限的目域样本导致严重过拟合。

Method: 提出GiPL双分支训练框架。分支一：迭代伪标签自训练范式，在支持集上执行零样本推理生成可靠伪标注，与真实标签融合后迭代优化模型。分支二：基于大视觉语言模型的生成式数据增强流程，合成域对齐的多目标标注图像以丰富训练样本并抑制过拟合。

Result: 在RUOD、CARPK、CarDD三个CD-FSOD数据集上的1/5/10-shot设置下，GiPL一致性地显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: GiPL通过伪标签自训练和生成式数据增强的双分支设计，有效提升了跨域少样本目标检测的性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Vision-language foundation models have shown promising zero-shot generalization for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Object Detection (CD-FSOD). However, they face two critical challenges in fine-tuning: insufficient support set utilization due to sparse single-instance annotations, and severe overfitting under extremely limited target-domain samples. To address these issues, this paper proposes GiPL, an efficient two-branch training framework.In the first branch, we design an iterative pseudo-label self-training paradigm, which performs zero-shot inference on the support set to generate reliable pseudo-annotations, fuses them with ground-truth labels, and iteratively optimizes the model to fully exploit support set data. In the second branch, we introduce generative data augmentation pipeline using large vision-language models, which synthesizes domain-aligned, multi-object annotated images to enrich training samples and suppress overfitting. Extensive experiments on three challenging CD-FSOD datasets (RUOD, CARPK, CarDD) under 1/5/10-shot settings demonstrate that GiPL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods with significant performance gains.Code is available at \href{https://github.com/z-yaz/CDiscover}{CDiscover}.

</details>


### [127] [Learning Representations from 3D Gaussian Splats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29549)
*Julia Farganus,Krzysztof Żurawicki,Arkadiusz Gaweł,Weronika Jakubowska,Halina Kwaśnicka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）场景表示下的几何深度学习架构进行了比较评估，研究了基于点和图的模型在场景分类任务中的表现，并通过端到端分类、线性探测和聚类分析探索了不同输入特征配置对表示质量的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS主要被设计用于视图合成任务，其在场景理解（如分类）方面的潜力尚未被充分探索，因此需要系统性地评估不同几何深度学习架构在3DGS表示下的分类性能。

Method: 采用对比评估方法，对基于点和基于图的几何深度学习架构进行基准测试；在传统点云数据集和专用高斯泼溅数据集上评估；将场景嵌入到潜在表示中，通过端到端分类、线性探测和聚类分析三种方式评估表示质量。

Result: 不同架构家族之间在场景分类任务上存在一致的性能差异；高斯特定的属性（如协方差、不透明度等）对表示质量有显著影响。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了不同几何深度学习架构在3DGS场景分类中的适用性差异，强调了高斯特定属性在获取有效场景表示中的重要性，为3DGS在场景理解任务中的应用提供了指导。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is a recent approach for scene rendering. Although primarily designed for view synthesis, its potential for scene understanding tasks remains underexplored. In this work, we conduct a comparative evaluation of various geometric deep learning architectures for the classification of 3D scenes represented using Gaussian Splatting. We benchmark point-based and graph-based models across both traditional point cloud datasets and dedicated Gaussian Splatting datasets. Scenes are embedded into latent representations, which are evaluated through end-to-end classification, linear probing, and clustering analysis. Our study provides insight into the suitability of different geometry-aware architectures and input feature configurations for learning effective 3D Gaussian Splat representations. The results highlight consistent differences between architectural families and reveal the impact of Gaussian-specific attributes on the quality of representation.

</details>


### [128] [TAE: Target-aware enhancer for nighttime UAV tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29558)
*Yanyan Chen,Ruigang Fu,Yu Song,Ping Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对夜间低光照条件下无人机单目标跟踪中图像退化严重的问题，本文提出目标感知低光增强框架TAE，利用跟踪边界框的弱监督信号进行区域感知增强，并采用自适应RGB多曲线融合机制实现精细化建模。同时构建了包含268个序列、9类目标的夜间UAV跟踪基准DarkSOT。实验证明TAE显著提升了夜间低光场景下的跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夜间低光照环境导致图像严重退化，这是制约无人机单目标跟踪实现全天候应用的核心瓶颈。现有图像增强方法难以区分目标和背景区域，容易放大背景噪声或损害目标特征。

Method: 提出TAE框架：1) 利用跟踪边界框的弱监督信号进行区域感知增强，使操作聚焦于目标区域；2) 采用自适应RGB多曲线融合机制，实现不同区域的精细化建模和自适应调整。同时构建DarkSOT夜间无人机跟踪基准数据集。

Result: 在DarkSOT和UAVDark135两个数据集上的实验结果表明，TAE在低光夜间场景下显著提升了跟踪性能，表现出强鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: TAE框架有效解决了夜间低光环境下无人机目标跟踪的难题，其区域感知设计和自适应曲线融合机制为夜间跟踪提供了可行方案。DarkSOT数据集为该领域研究提供了标准化评估基准。

Abstract: Severe image degradation under low-light nighttime conditions constitutes a core bottleneck preventing all-day applications for UAV-based single object tracking. Existing image enhancement methods often struggle to distinguish between target and background regions, which can easily lead to amplified background noise or compromise target features. To overcome this limitation, we propose TAE, a target-aware low-light enhancement framework tailored for nighttime object tracking. Guided explicitly by weak supervisory signals from tracking bounding boxes, the framework performs region-aware enhancement to ensure operations focus on the target area. It further adopts an adaptive RGB multi-curve fusion mechanism to achieve refined modeling and adaptive adjustment across different regions. To facilitate research in this domain, we also contribute DarkSOT, a new benchmark for nighttime UAV tracking, comprising 268 sequences across 9 target categories. Experimental results on the DarkSOT and UAVDark135 demonstrate that TAE significantly improves tracking performance in low-light nighttime scenarios, exhibiting strong robustness and generalization. The DarkSOT dataset is available at https://github.com/Fu0511/DarkSOT-Dataset.

</details>


### [129] [From General Vision to Reliable Traversability Estimation: Adapting Vision Foundation Models for Unstructured Outdoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29565)
*Ji-Hoon Hwang,Jisung Bae,Dong-Wook Kim,Yeonkyu Lee,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ViTA框架，通过可学习遍历性提示、视角多样化训练和几何知识蒸馏，将视觉基础模型（SAM2）适配为可靠的野外可通行性估计方法，在跨域泛化和假阳性抑制上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉基础模型的可通行性估计方法面临三个根本挑战：VFMs的任务无关设计、可通行性标注的模糊性、以及语义标签与物理安全性之间的不一致性。

Method: ViTA包含三个核心组件：1) 可学习遍历性提示注入任务特定知识；2) 视角多样化训练通过估计语义不确定性抑制模糊边界的置信预测；3) 几何知识蒸馏使模型在推理时仅从RGB图像推理坡度和高程信息。语义与几何输出融合为连续可通行性分数。

Result: 在包括真实越野场景的多个域上，ViTA在IoU和精确率上达到SOTA，显著降低了假阳性率，并具备强跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: ViTA通过任务适配、不确定性建模和几何知识融合，有效解决了VFM可通行性估计中的可靠性问题，为户外非结构化环境提供了更安全的感知方案。

Abstract: Vision-based approaches have become the dominant paradigm for traversability estimation in unstructured outdoor environments, typically adapting vision foundation models (VFMs) via semantic segmentation supervision. However, this paradigm faces three fundamental challenges that undermine its reliability: the task-agnostic design of VFMs, the ambiguity of traversability annotations, and the discrepancy between semantic labels and physical safety. We propose Vision-to-Traversability Adaptation (ViTA), a framework that adapts VFMs for reliable traversability estimation, instantiated on SAM2. ViTA injects task-specific knowledge through learnable traversability prompts while preserving the VFM's cross-domain generalization. To handle annotation ambiguity, we introduce Perspective-Diversified Training, which estimates semantic uncertainty to suppress confident predictions at ambiguous boundaries. To bridge the semantic-traversability discrepancy, we distill geometric knowledge during training, enabling slope and elevation reasoning from RGB images alone at inference. The semantic and geometric outputs are fused into a continuous traversability score that reflects both semantic uncertainty and geometric risk. Evaluations across diverse domains, including challenging real-world off-road datasets, demonstrate that ViTA achieves state-of-the-art IoU and Precision with substantial false-positive reduction and strong cross-domain generalization.

</details>


### [130] [DefSynUS: Real-time Patient-specific Intrahepatic Vessel Identification via Deformation-Aware CT-US Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29570)
*Karl-Philippe Beaudet,Yordanka Velikova,Sidaty El Hadramy,Nassir Navab,Philippe Cattin,Juan Verde,Stéphane Cotin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于变形感知超声增强的实时肝内血管识别框架，通过物理渲染合成超声数据并结合域适应，无需术前超声即可实现术中实时、患者特异性的血管识别，在体模和单例临床实验中验证了技术可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腹腔镜超声在肝脏手术中可实时可视化肝内血管，但因探头限制、血管结构复杂和组织变形，血管识别依然困难。本文旨在实现变形下仍保持鲁棒的实时、患者特异性血管识别。

Method: 利用术前CT血管标注，通过优化的基于物理的渲染生成合成超声数据，并结合域适应适配至术中超声。渲染过程以端到端方式训练，面向血管识别和患者特异性。在渲染管线中引入变形感知增强，模拟术中运动和组织变形。

Result: 在腹部体模和单例临床实验中，框架实现了实时肝内血管分支识别，并在新患者姿态下保持性能。

Conclusion: 该框架无需术前超声即可实现实时血管识别，验证了技术可行性，但尚需多患者验证以评估泛化性和临床可行性。

Abstract: Purpose: Laparoscopic ultrasound (LUS) enhances the safety of liver surgery by visualizing intrahepatic vessels in real-time. Still, vessel identification remains difficult due to probe constraints, complex vascular structure, and tissue deformation. This work aims to enable real-time, patient-specific vessel identification that remains robust under deformation through deformable ultrasound augmentation. Methods: Preoperative CT vessel annotations are used to generate synthetic ultrasound data via optimized physics-based rendering, coupled with domain adaptation to intraoperative ultrasound. The rendering is trained end-to-end for vessel identification and patient-specificity, eliminating the need for preoperative ultrasound. A deformation-aware augmentation simulates realistic intraoperative motion and tissue deformation within the rendering pipeline. Results: In abdominal phantom and limited clinical feasibility experiments (single-case clinical evaluation), the framework achieved real-time intrahepatic vessel-branch identification, maintaining performance under new patient poses. Conclusion: The framework enables real-time vessel identification without preoperative ultrasound and supports technical feasibility, but multi-patient validation is still needed for generalizability and clinical feasibility.

</details>


### [131] [Mitigating State Aliasing in Vision-Language-Action Models via Inverse Dynamics Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29577)
*Kyujin Lee,Injae Kim,Jihwan Park,Yejun Ju,Minseok Joo,Hyunwoo J. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通过逆动力学学习作为辅助目标来直接监督VLA视觉编码器，以缓解视觉-语言-动作模型中视觉状态混淆（state aliasing）问题，在CALVIN ABC-D和SimplerEnv上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型将预训练的VLM适配到动作预测时，VLM衍生的表征对低层控制所需的细微视觉差异不敏感，导致视觉上相似但需要不同动作的状态之间出现状态混淆（state aliasing），而现有方法仅通过端到端预测间接塑造视觉编码器，未显式分析视觉特征空间中的状态混淆问题。

Method: 引入逆动力学学习作为辅助目标：通过预测当前观测与未来观测之间的动作来直接监督VLA视觉编码器，使其捕获决定低层动作的细粒度视觉差异；进一步使用伪反向监督（pseudo-reversed supervision）暴露编码器于更广泛的动作方向，提升在有限机器人演示下的泛化能力；仅使用标准观测-动作对，无需额外标注，测试时保持原始推理流程不变。

Result: 在CALVIN ABC-D和SimplerEnv上，该方法在多种VLA基线上取得一致性能提升；冻结编码器探针实验和状态-特征对齐分析表明，该方法学习了具有状态判别性的视觉表征，减少了状态混淆，并更好地对齐了机器人状态变化。

Conclusion: 逆动力学学习作为辅助目标能够有效缓解VLA模型中的状态混淆问题，学习更具判别性的视觉表征，且方法通用、无额外标注需求、不改变测试时推理流程。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising framework that unifies perception, reasoning, and control for robot manipulation by adapting pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) to action prediction. However, VLM-derived representations are often insensitive to subtle visual distinctions required for low-level control, causing state aliasing between visually similar states that require substantially different actions. Prior VLA studies improve visual understanding by generating visual or reasoning outputs, such as future frames, 2D grounding points or traces, or intermediate spatial reasoning steps, but these objectives typically shape the vision encoder only indirectly through end-to-end prediction and do not explicitly analyze state aliasing in the learned visual feature space. To mitigate state aliasing, we introduce inverse dynamics learning as an auxiliary objective that directly supervises the VLA vision encoder. By predicting the action between current and future observations, our objective encourages the encoder to capture fine-grained visual distinctions that determine low-level actions. We further use pseudo-reversed supervision to expose the encoder to a broader range of action directions and improve generalization under limited robot demonstrations. Our method applies to diverse VLA baselines, uses only standard observation-action pairs without additional annotations, and preserves the original inference pipeline at test time. Experiments on CALVIN ABC-D and SimplerEnv show consistent gains across diverse VLA baselines. Frozen-encoder probing and state-feature alignment analyses further show that our method learns state-discriminative visual representations that reduce state aliasing and better align with robot state changes.

</details>


### [132] [ReactBench: A Cause-Driven Benchmark for Multimodal Hallucination via Systematic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29579)
*Shizhe Zhou,Bohan Jia,Kai Wu,Yan Shen,Tongyun Li,Yuyang Wu,Shaohui Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReactBench是一个因果驱动的多模态幻觉基准测试，包含四项针对性任务（关系擦除、反事实属性、变化追踪、密集计数）和考试式评估格式，通过链式推理识别幻觉的具体子原因，揭示当前MLLMs对特定因果触发因素的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉基准主要检测幻觉结果而非评估其根本原因，且多依赖简单场景和有限评估格式，无法挑战前沿模型。

Method: 通过生成对抗性图像和诱导幻觉的查询，设计四项针对性任务（关系擦除、反事实属性、变化追踪、密集计数），并利用链式推理识别每项任务中幻觉的细粒度子原因。

Result: 当前MLLMs对因果特定的幻觉触发器表现出明显脆弱性，证明ReactBench作为系统性、可解释的诊断测试床的有效性。

Conclusion: ReactBench可作为诊断和改进多模态模型鲁棒性的系统性、可解释测试平台。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved rapid progress in vision-language understanding, they remain prone to multimodal hallucinations, producing responses that are inconsistent with the visual input. Existing benchmarks predominantly focus on detecting hallucination outcomes rather than evaluating the underlying causes of these failures. Moreover, many benchmarks rely on simplistic scenarios and limited evaluation formats that no longer challenge state-of-the-art models. To address these limitations, we introduce ReactBench, a cause-driven hallucination benchmark featuring multiple tasks and an exam-style evaluation format. By generating adversarial images and hallucination-inducing queries, ReactBench introduces four targeted tasks: Relational Erasure, Counterfactual Attribute, Alteration Tracing, and Dense Counting. These tasks systematically expose co-occurrence bias, language priors, cross-image comparative perception deficiencies, and fine-grained perceptual bottlenecks. Beyond standard accuracy-based evaluation, we leverage Chain-of-Thought reasoning to identify fine-grained sub-causes of hallucination within each task. Extensive evaluations reveal that current MLLMs remain notably vulnerable to cause-specific hallucination triggers, demonstrating the value of ReactBench as a systematic and interpretable testbed for diagnosing and improving multimodal model robustness. The project page is available at https://reactbench.github.io/.

</details>


### [133] [BitC-3DGS: High-Capacity 3D Gaussian Splatting Watermarking via Bit Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29583)
*Yuquan Bi,Baosheng Yu,Yingke Lei,Jianwei Yang,Hongsong Wang,Jie Gui,Yuan Yan Tang,James Tin-Yau Kwok*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BitC-3DGS提出了一种位压缩框架，通过将多个消息位编码为单个语义token，并采用双分支架构和硬消息采样策略，突破了现有方法因CLIP固定77-token长度限制而只能嵌入77位消息的瓶颈，实现了128位消息的高容量水印嵌入，且恢复精度与现有方法64位消息相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于预训练文本编码器的比特到令牌水印方法受限于CLIP固定的77-token上下文长度，无法嵌入超过77位的消息，这限制了在大规模3D资产管线中嵌入丰富信息（如所有权、来源和认证码）的能力。

Method: 1) 提出位压缩标记化方案：将同一块内的多个消息位编码为单个语义token；2) 引入双分支架构：联合执行块解压缩和位解码；3) 采用硬消息采样策略：在解码器训练期间提高组合覆盖范围。

Result: 在Blender和LLFF数据集上的实验表明，BitC-3DGS实现了高容量水印嵌入，例如支持128位消息容量，其恢复精度与现有最先进方法在64位消息上的精度相当，同时保持高质量的渲染保真度。

Conclusion: BitC-3DGS通过位压缩机制有效解决了3DGS资产中高容量水印嵌入的token长度限制问题，为大规模3D资产管线中的可靠身份识别和完整性验证提供了可行方案。

Abstract: High-capacity watermarking is necessary for 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) assets to embed rich information (e.g., ownership, provenance, and authentication codes), enabling reliable identification and integrity verification in large-scale 3D asset pipelines. Existing bit-to-token watermarking methods based on a pre-trained text encoder are limited to 77-bit messages due to CLIP's fixed 77-token context length, as tokens beyond this limit are unsupported by learned positional embeddings. To address this limitation, we introduce BitC-3DGS, a bit-compression framework that encodes multiple message bits per token. It employs a bit-compressed tokenization scheme that encodes multiple bits within the same chunk into a single semantic token. To enable recovery of the compressed information, it further introduces a dual-branch architecture for joint chunk decompression and bit decoding, along with a hard-message sampling strategy to improve combinatorial coverage during decoder training. Extensive experiments on the Blender and LLFF datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of BitC-3DGS for high-capacity watermarking, achieving high message recovery accuracy and rendering fidelity. For example, it supports 128-bit message capacity with recovery accuracy comparable to that of 64-bit messages in recent state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [134] [Brain-IT-VQA: From Brain Signals to Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29588)
*Roman Beliy,Matias Cosarinsky,Oliver Heinimann,Navve Wasserman,Michal Irani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Brain-IT-VQA框架，利用fMRI信号解码视觉内容并进行视觉问答，同时构建了NSD-VQA数据集（每张图片平均20个问答对，覆盖20个受控类别），在性能上显著优于此前方法，并可用于分析大脑视觉表征结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，从人脑fMRI信号中解码视觉内容并回答关于所见图像的问题是一项挑战。现有方法性能有限，且缺乏能够系统解耦多层级视觉理解的可控数据集；同时，强预测模型很少被用于理解大脑视觉表征的结构。

Method: 基于Brain Interaction Transformer（Brain-IT），从脑活动解码语言标记，并与语言模型集成以回答视觉问题；同时构建NSD-VQA数据集，包含20个受控问题类别、每张图片平均20个问答对，支持更可靠和可解释的评估。

Result: Brain-IT-VQA在fMRI字幕生成和视觉问答任务上显著优于此前方法；NSD-VQA提供了更细粒度、受控的评估基准；量化了可从fMRI响应中可靠解码的视觉与语义信息类型，并分析了不同脑区在不同问题类型中的贡献。

Conclusion: Brain-IT-VQA和NSD-VQA共同提供了一个强大的预测框架和用于研究大脑视觉表征的工具，能够解耦多层级视觉理解，揭示了不同脑区在视觉问答中的功能分化。

Abstract: Decoding visual content from fMRI signals recorded while a person views images, and specifically answering questions about the seen images, is a long-standing challenge. While significant progress has been made in recent years in visual question answering (VQA) from fMRI, performance remains limited. Moreover, although recent models can make increasingly accurate predictions, they have rarely been used as tools for understanding the structure of visual representations in the brain. We present Brain-IT-VQA, a framework for visual question answering from fMRI. Building on the Brain Interaction Transformer (Brain-IT), our method decodes language tokens from brain activity and integrates them with a language model to answer visual questions. Our model substantially outperforms previous fMRI-based captioning and VQA approaches. We further introduce NSD-VQA, a new dataset and benchmark for visual question answering from fMRI. Unlike existing image-fMRI VQA datasets, which typically provide only a few broad and weakly controlled questions per image, NSD-VQA provides on average 20 question-answer pairs per image across 20 controlled question categories that disentangle multiple levels of visual understanding. This enables more reliable and interpretable evaluation despite limited fMRI test data. Together, Brain-IT-VQA and NSD-VQA provide both a strong predictive framework and a tool for studying brain representations. Using this benchmark, we quantify which forms of visual and semantic information can be reliably decoded from fMRI responses to natural images. We further analyze the contributions of different brain regions across question types.

</details>


### [135] [Non-Forgetting Knowledge Allocation with Bi-level Competition for Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29592)
*Xiang Tan,Run He,Yawen Cui,Mengchen Zhao,Yan Wu,Tianyi Chen,Huiping Zhuang,Xiaonan Luo,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对基于预训练模型的类增量学习(CIL)中任务适配器分配不均和遗忘问题，提出NoFA-BC方法，包括非遗忘分配器(NFA，通过递归最小二乘实现等价全数据训练)、双层竞争(BLC，含任务内胜者通吃WTA和任务间最后淘汰LOF机制)和稳定性增强(SE)，实现自适应的适配器知识分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于适配器的CIL方法在推理时为每个适配器分配均等权重，忽视了任务差异性导致适配器利用次优；同时分配器在任务演进中容易遗忘。

Method: 1) 非遗忘分配器(NFA)：将分配器训练转化为递归最小二乘问题，实现等价于全数据训练的分配器；2) 双层竞争(BLC)：任务内层采用胜者通吃(WTA)机制提取最具显著性的logit，任务间层采用最后淘汰(LOF)机制抑制无关适配器；3) 稳定性增强(SE)：进一步提升旧任务性能。

Result: 通过BLC竞争机制，每个适配器的参与比可根据输入自适应调整，NFA避免了分配器遗忘，SE提升了旧任务性能。

Conclusion: NoFA-BC通过非遗忘分配器和双层竞争机制有效解决了适配器分配不均和遗忘问题，实现了更优的适配器知识利用。

Abstract: Class-Incremental Learning (CIL) with pre-trained models (PTMs) aims to sequentially adapt PTMs to new categories without forgetting old knowledge. Built upon PTMs, existing adapter-based methods mainly train models via distinct task-specific adapters, and present a uniform knowledge allocation for each adapter during inference. However, this allocation mechanism ignores the nature of task discrepancy and leads to suboptimal utilization of adapters. Also, under CIL constraint, an allocator is prone to forgetting when tasks evolve. To address these issues, we propose a Non-Forgetting Allocation with Bi-Level Competition (NoFA-BC). NoFA-BC constructs a non-forgetting allocator (NFA) by transforming the allocator training into a recursive least-squares problem and achieves an allocator equivalent to that trained with all data. Based on the NFA, a Bi-Level Competition (BLC) including an intra-task level Winner-Takes-All (WTA) mechanism and inter-task Last-Ones-Fall (LOF) elimination is proposed to provide better allocation of adapter knowledge. WTA extracts the most significant logit within a task to represent the adapter's contribution and LOF suppresses the irrelevant adapters. With BLC, participation ratio of each adapter can be tailored for each input. Moreover, a Stability Enhancement (SE) process is incorporated to further improve the performance of old tasks.

</details>


### [136] [CogniVerse: Revolutionizing Multi-Modal Retrieval-Augmented Generation with Cognitive Reflection and Geometric Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29602)
*Xiang Fang,Wanlong Fang,Changshuo Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CogniVerse是一个受认知科学启发的多模态检索增强生成框架，通过认知反射、多模态检索和分层生成三个模块，解决了现有MMRAG系统中的噪声检索、跨模态语义对齐、自适应推理和局部-全局生成不一致等问题，在准确性和连贯性上显著超越现有系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MMRAG框架存在四个关键局限：检索结果含噪声且不相关、跨模态语义不对齐、缺乏自适应推理能力、以及局部与全局上下文的生成不连贯，亟需一种更鲁棒且可解释的解决方案。

Method: CogniVerse包含三个协同模块：(1) 认知反射模块——动态评估检索必要性并过滤多模态内容；(2) 多模态检索模块——利用信息几何在黎曼流形中对齐嵌入，并通过谱图理论精化知识图谱；(3) 分层生成模块——采用基于最优传输的损失函数平衡token级精度与全局语义连贯性。

Result: 大量实验表明，CogniVerse在准确性和连贯性上显著优于当前最优系统，同时降低了检索延迟。

Conclusion: 受认知启发的数学严谨方法能有效解决MMRAG中检索噪声、语义对齐和生成不一致等核心挑战，为多模态知识密集型问答提供了更高效、更准确的范式。

Abstract: Multi-modal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (MMRAG) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for enhancing Multimodal Large Language Models in knowledge-intensive question answering by integrating external visual, textual, and structural knowledge. However, existing MMRAG frameworks suffer from critical limitations, including noisy and irrelevant retrieval, cross-modal semantic misalignment, lack of adaptive reasoning, and incoherent generation across local and global contexts. We introduce \textbf{CogniVerse}, a novel MMRAG framework that addresses these challenges through a cognitive-inspired, mathematically rigorous approach. Drawing from human-like reasoning, CogniVerse integrates three synergistic components: (1) a Cognitive Reflection Module that dynamically assesses retrieval necessity and filters relevant multi-modal content, reducing noise and computational overhead; (2) a Multi-modal Retrieval Module that aligns embeddings in a Riemannian manifold using information geometry and refines knowledge graphs via spectral graph theory, ensuring precise and coherent retrieval; and (3) a Hierarchical Generation Module that employs an optimal transport-based loss to balance token-level accuracy and global semantic coherence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CogniVerse significantly outperforms state-of-the-art systems in both accuracy and coherence, while reducing retrieval latency.

</details>


### [137] [Learning Context-Conditioned Predicate Semantics via Prototype Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29610)
*NamGyu Jung,Chang Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AlignG通过原型反馈学习上下文条件化的谓词语义，在场景图生成中实现动态语义重组，在VG-150和GQA-200上分别提升F@100指标1.4和2.7。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场景图生成中，多义谓词在不同上下文中含义不同，现有方法保持谓词表示静态，无法根据图像特定证据重组语义，导致歧义上下文中出现系统性混淆。

Method: AlignG从每张图像的关系候选中推断上下文条件化的谓词语义，并将适配后的语义反馈回关系表示进行重新校准；同时以全局语义中心锚定学习目标，在允许选择性重组的同时防止语义漂移。

Result: 在VG-150和GQA-200数据集上，SGDet任务中F@100分别提升1.4和2.7，优于现有基线；可视化分析显示原型能根据场景证据选择性地合并或分离谓词。

Conclusion: 上下文条件化的动态谓词语义建模能有效解决场景图生成中的多义性问题，原型反馈机制在保持全局语义一致性与实现局部上下文适应之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: In scene graph generation, a central challenge is modeling polysemous predicates whose meanings shift across contexts. Prior approaches address this issue by decomposing predicates into multiple static prototypes or retrieving semantically similar exemplars. However, these strategies keep predicate representations static and cannot reorganize semantics to reflect image-specific evidence, leading to systematic confusions in ambiguous contexts. We propose AlignG, which learns context-conditioned predicate semantics via prototype feedback. AlignG infers context-conditioned predicate semantics from the relation candidates within each image and feeds the adapted semantics back to recalibrate relation representations. The learning objective anchors this adaptation to global semantic centers, preventing semantic drift while still allowing selective reorganization when the scene provides consistent relational cues. Experiments on VG-150 and GQA-200 show consistent improvements over state-of-the-art baselines, with F@100 improvements of +1.4 on VG-150 and +2.7 on GQA-200 under SGDet. We further visualize per-image prototype similarity shifts and observe coherent context-dependent reorganization where prototypes selectively merge or separate predicates according to scene evidence. The code is available at https://github.com/Namgyu97/AlignG-SGG.pytorch.

</details>


### [138] [Unsupervised Semantic Segmentation Facilitates Model Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29691)
*Xiaoyan Yu,Lisa Mais,Jannik Franzen,Peter Hirsch,Nick Lechtenbörger,Andreas Mardt,Dagmar Kainmüller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简单直观的可视化协议，通过无监督语义分割结果来理解自监督视觉Transformer（ViT）的表示行为，揭示了不同SSL模型（对比学习vs掩码图像建模）的位置偏置、缩放行为和边界伪影差异，旨在让模型理解变得广泛可及。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究揭示了对比学习和掩码图像建模在自注意力机制和表示信息上的显著差异，但这些理解尚未广泛渗透到学术界，CL模型的结论常被错误泛化到MIM模型。作者希望提供一种简单直观的可视化方法，使广大研究者能轻松理解不同SSL ViT的行为差异。

Method: 提出基于无监督语义分割可视化的分析协议，不追求分割性能最大化，而是用于揭示模型在不同层和表示上一致涌现的行为模式。通过对多种SSL模型进行跨层和跨表示的基准测试，分析位置偏置、缩放行为和边界伪影等特征。

Result: 发现了不同SSL模型之间显著的位置偏置差异和缩放行为差异，特别是在DINOv3-Large模型的token中观察到强烈的边界伪影。这些结果补充并帮助传达了以往的多项发现，并能清晰区分位置效应与局部性偏置（后者已被广泛研究）。

Conclusion: 该可视化协议可在GitHub上公开获取，将有助于广大社区进一步理解和分析自监督视觉Transformer模型的内部行为机制。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) has produced a diverse landscape of vision transformers (ViTs) whose pretrained representations support a wide range of downstream tasks. Towards a better understanding of these models, a body of work has assessed the mechanics of their self-attention as well as the types of information captured across their representations, revealing, for example, stark differences between models trained with contrastive learning (CL) and masked image modeling (MIM). However, these advances in model understanding have not yet fully permeated the broader community, where insights specific to CL models are sometimes generalized to MIM models. To make model understanding straightforward and intuitive for a broad audience, we propose a simple and easily interpretable visualization protocol.
  Our protocol is based on visualizing unsupervised semantic segmentation results, yet our goal is not to maximize segmentation performance. Instead, it allows us to convey model behaviors that consistently emerge across images. Benchmarking a diverse set of SSL models across layers and representations, we obtain novel insights into distinct positional biases and scaling behaviors, including strong boundary artifacts in DINOv3-Large model tokens. These insights complement and help communicate a range of previous findings.
  Our protocol further enables a clear visual distinction between positional effects and the closely related but distinct locality bias, which has been studied much more extensively in the literature. The protocol is publicly available on GitHub and we believe it will catalyze further model understanding for a broad community.

</details>


### [139] [SLAD : Shared LoRA Adapters for Task Specific Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29726)
*Reda Bensaid,Yassir Bendou,Vincent Gripon,François Leduc-Primeau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对资源受限环境下的任务特定蒸馏，本文发现教师模型微调会导致师生特征对齐偏移，进而损害知识迁移。受保留已有知识的启发，提出SLAD方法：利用低秩适应（LoRA）改善特征对齐，并通过参数共享策略进一步增强对齐。实验表明SLAD在多个分类和分割数据集上达到SOTA，训练速度快2倍，同时提升师生双方性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在任务特定蒸馏中，教师模型微调虽然提升自身性能，但会导致师生特征表示不对齐，损害知识蒸馏效果。需要一种既能保持教师性能又能改善特征对齐的方法。

Method: 提出SLAD方法：1）利用低秩适应（LoRA）替代全量微调，保留预训练知识；2）在师生联合训练中采用适配器参数共享策略，进一步增强特征对齐。

Result: 在多个分类和分割数据集上达到SOTA性能，训练速度比微调快2倍，同时提升教师和学生模型双方性能。

Conclusion: 特征对齐是任务特定蒸馏的关键，通过低秩适应和参数共享可以有效改善对齐，实现更高效的知识迁移。

Abstract: In the context of resource-constrained environments such as embedded systems, adapting reduced-size foundation models to downstream tasks has become increasingly popular. This has recently motivated the emerging setting of task-specific distillation, where a larger and a smaller version of the same foundation model are both adapted to the same downstream task, with the goal of transferring knowledge from the former to the latter. Recent work has demonstrated the benefits of using a larger version of the same foundation model to assist the adaptation of a smaller one. Typically, the larger model (teacher) is first adapted via fine-tuning or linear probing before its knowledge is distilled into the smaller model (student). While fine-tuning the teacher often increases its performance, recent work showed that probing it leads to better knowledge distillation to the student. Our findings show that this is mainly due to a mis-alignment in feature representation between the teacher and the student which occurs during the teacher's fine-tuning. Inspired by existing efforts to preserve previously learned knowledge, we first propose leveraging low-rank adaptation, resulting in better feature alignment and therefore better knowledge transfer. Drawing from this insight, we further enhance the feature alignment through a parameter-sharing strategy of the adapters between the two encoders during joint training. Our proposed method, SLAD, shows better feature alignment between the teacher and student, which results in increased performance for not only the student but also the teacher model, while being 2x faster to train than fine-tuning. Through extensive experiments on multiple classification and segmentation datasets, we demonstrate the improved accuracy and transfer efficiency of our method, achieving state-of-the-art performance in the task-specific distillation framework.

</details>


### [140] [S2MDF: A Plug-And-Play Layer for Intersection-Free Multi-Object Signed Distance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29761)
*Deniz Sayin Mercadier,Federico Stella,Aurel Bizeau,Nicolas Talabot,Pascal Fua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出S2MDF模块，通过硬约束严格防止组合式隐式表面表示中多个SDF物体之间的相互穿透，将交叉减少到数值精度级别，且不牺牲重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组合式隐式表面表示（每个物体由SDF编码）存在物体间几何相互穿透的问题，违反物理合理性。现有软惩罚项方法只能减少而非消除交叉，且需要谨慎的损失权重调整。

Method: 提出一种向量值SDF的硬约束，并设计S2MDF——轻量级即插即用模块，无需修改架构即可在任意物体组合式SDF表示上强制执行该约束。兼容Marching Cubes等线性插值标准网格化算法，可应用于训练阶段或后处理。

Result: 在多个最先进组合式方法上，S2MDF将交叉减少到数值精度级别，同时保持重建质量，优于现有缓解策略。

Conclusion: 硬约束方法可从根本上消除物体间相互穿透，S2MDF作为高效、兼容性强的模块，为组合式隐式表示提供了实用且有效的物理合理性保证方案。

Abstract: Compositional implicit surface representations model scenes as collections of objects, each encoded by a Signed Distance Field (SDF). A fundamental limitation of this approach is that multiple SDFs can produce geometries that interpenetrate, violating physical plausibility. Existing mitigation strategies rely on soft penalty terms that reduce but do not eliminate intersections, and require careful loss weighting. To truly prevent interpenetration, we propose a hard constraint on vector-valued SDFs and introduce S2MDF, a lightweight plug-and-play module that enforces the constraint on any object-compositional SDF representation without architectural modifications. It introduces negligible computational overhead and is compatible with linearly-interpolated standard meshing algorithms such as Marching Cubes. It can be applied during training or as a post-processing step. Experiments on multiple state-of-the-art compositional methods show that S2MDF reduces intersections to numerical precision while preserving reconstruction quality, outperforming existing mitigation strategies.

</details>


### [141] [GeoMag: Geometric-Aware Video Motion Magnification via State Space Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29762)
*Kecheng Han,Yuchen Zhang,Bingqing Liu,Boqiang Guo,Wenbin Zheng,Shiyuan Pei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoMag提出基于状态空间模型的几何感知视频运动放大框架，结合Geo-200K大规模合成数据集，在保证线性计算复杂度的同时实现全局一致的运动放大，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VMM方法在复杂几何变换下存在结构不一致问题，CNN全局上下文有限而Transformer计算成本高，且训练数据以简单线性运动为主，缺乏真实世界几何与成像复杂性。

Method: 基于状态空间模型构建几何感知的VMM框架GeoMag，实现线性复杂度的全局一致运动放大；构建Geo-200K大规模合成数据集，引入丰富几何变换和传感器级退化。

Result: 在合成和真实基准测试上，GeoMag在视觉保真度和计算效率上持续优于先前方法，产生更少伪影和更好的结构一致性。

Conclusion: GeoMag通过状态空间模型和大规模几何感知数据集有效解决了VMM在复杂几何变换下的结构不一致问题，实现了效率与精度的平衡。

Abstract: Video Motion Magnification (VMM) reveals imperceptible dynamics but often suffers from structural inconsistencies under complex geometric transformations. Existing learning-based methods generally face a trade-off between the limited global context of CNNs and the high computational cost of Transformers. In addition, current training protocols, largely dominated by simple linear motion, fail to capture the geometric and imaging complexities encountered in real-world videos. To address these issues, we propose GeoMag, a geometric-aware VMM framework built upon State Space Models to achieve globally consistent motion amplification with linear complexity. We further construct Geo-200K, a large-scale synthetic dataset that introduces rich geometric transformations together with sensor-realistic degradations, improving the diversity and realism of training signals. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks show that GeoMag consistently outperforms prior methods in visual fidelity and computational efficiency, while producing fewer artifacts and better structural consistency.

</details>


### [142] [Energy-Aware NECO for Single-Pass Pixel-wise Out-of-Distribution Detection in Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29773)
*Boyuan Zhang,Huanshan Huang,Yifei Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Energy-Aware NECO，一种单次前向传播的像素级分布外检测器，结合NECO几何比率和Energy评分，在语义分割OOD检测中优于现有方法，且保持了单次前向传播的高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人需要可靠的语义分割，但现有不确定性估计方法如蒙特卡洛Dropout需要多次随机前向传播，难以部署在边缘平台上。因此需要一个单次前向传播、高效且准确的OOD检测方法。

Method: 提出Energy-Aware NECO混合方法：1）从解码器特征计算中心化的NECO风格几何比率；2）基于logits的Energy评分；3）两个分量通过拟合纯分布内验证集的统计量进行标准化；4）通过凸组合融合得到最终OOD分数。

Result: 在miniMUAD数据集上，混合评分AUROC达0.8539，优于NECO-only (0.8280)、Energy-only (0.8171)和集成预测熵基线(0.8124)。

Conclusion: 混合检测器在保持单次前向传播效率优势的同时，显著提升了整体排名性能和OOD检测效果，适合部署在边缘平台。

Abstract: Reliable semantic segmentation for mobile robots requires both accurate dense prediction and robust uncertainty estimation under distribution shift. Strong uncertainty baselines such as Monte Carlo Dropout often require repeated stochastic forward passes and are difficult to deploy on edge platforms.
  We propose Energy-Aware NECO, a single-pass pixel-wise out-of-distribution (OOD) detector for semantic segmentation. The method combines a centered NECO-style geometric ratio computed from decoder features with a logit-based Energy score. Both components are standardized using statistics fitted on a pure in-distribution validation split and fused through a convex combination.
  We evaluate the method on the miniMUAD subset using true pixel-level OOD labels. The proposed hybrid score achieves an AUROC of 0.8539, outperforming NECO-only (0.8280), Energy-only (0.8171), and an ensemble predictive-entropy baseline (0.8124). Additional qualitative and operating-point analyses show that the hybrid detector improves overall ranking performance while preserving the efficiency advantages of a single-pass design.
  Code is available at https://github.com/boyuan-zhangx/Energy-Aware_NECO

</details>


### [143] [Improving CLIP Adaptation by Breaking Tail Alignment for Source-Free Cross-Domain Few-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29776)
*Shuai Yi,Yixiong Zou,Yuhua Li,Ruixuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应尾-头对齐（ATHA）微调策略，通过区分并差异化处理图像token（对头token加强对齐，对尾token主动推离文本嵌入），解决了CLIP在跨域小样本学习场景中因域偏移大、训练数据少导致的过拟合问题，在四个基准上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等VLM在跨域小样本场景下性能严重下降，现有微调方法对所有图像patch token与文本嵌入进行统一对齐，但研究发现强制对齐低相似度的"尾token"会导致过拟合，反而损害性能。

Method: 提出ATHA方法：识别图像token中语义信息充足的"头token"和语义不足的"尾token"，对头token加强文本对齐，对尾token主动推离文本嵌入，将统一对齐范式转变为自适应对齐范式。

Result: 在四个跨域小样本学习基准上取得SOTA性能，验证了主动推离尾token而非强制对齐的有效性。

Conclusion: 在大域偏移和稀缺训练数据下，并非所有图像token都适合与文本对齐；对缺乏语义信息的尾token打破对齐比强制对齐更有利于泛化，自适应对齐策略显著优于传统统一对齐范式。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) such as CLIP demonstrate strong zero-shot generalization, but their performance significantly degrades in cross-domain scenarios with scarce target-domain training data (Cross-Domain Few-Shot Learning, CDFSL). In this paper, we focus on the target-domain few-shot finetuning in the CLIP-based CDFSL task. Prevailing finetuning paradigms uniformly align all image patch tokens with their corresponding textual embeddings. However, we find a counterintuitive phenomenon: actively pushing away certain low-similarity image tokens, termed "tail tokens", from their textual embeddings consistently improves target-domain performance. We delve into this phenomenon and provide a novel interpretation: under great domain shifts and scarce training data, the model can hardly extract semantic information from visual inputs; therefore, the common belief of alignment is valid only for tokens already containing sufficient semantic information; for tail tokens, forcing the alignment would lead to excessive overfitting to the scarce training, while breaking the alignment is more useful. Motivated by this, we propose Adaptive Tail-Head Alignment (ATHA), a novel fine-tuning strategy for CLIP that transforms the conventional uniform alignment paradigm to an adaptive alignment paradigm, with both alignment strengthening and weakening. Extensive experiments on four challenging CDFSL benchmarks validate our state-of-the-art performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/shuaiyi308/ATHA.

</details>


### [144] [Fewer Steps, Better Performance: Efficient Cross-Modal Clip Trimming for Video Moment Retrieval Using Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29793)
*Xiang Fang,Daizong Liu,Wanlong Fang,Pan Zhou,Zichuan Xu,Wenzheng Xu,Junyang Chen,Renfu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpotVMR，一种高效即插即用的视频时刻检索模块，通过学习查询相关的片段搜索模型和低成本语义索引特征，解决现有方法因固定长度下采样导致的边界模糊、跨模态错位和计算效率低等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VMR方法将长视频下采样为固定长度片段，导致查询相关帧被过滤、边界模糊、跨模态错位，且处理长视频时计算成本过高。

Method: 设计基于语言查询的片段搜索模型识别候选区域；引入低成本语义索引特征捕获物体与交互上下文；使用蒸馏损失解决片段选择器与VMR模型联合训练的优化问题。

Result: 在三个具有挑战性的数据集上的大量实验证明了其有效性。

Conclusion: SpotVMR作为即插即用模块，能够提升现有SOTA VMR方法的效率，同时保持良好检索性能。

Abstract: Given an untrimmed video and a sentence query, video moment retrieval using language (VMR) aims to locate a target query-relevant moment. Since the untrimmed video is overlong, almost all existing VMR methods first sparsely down-sample each untrimmed video into multiple fixed-length video clips and then conduct multi-modal interactions with the query feature and expensive clip features for reasoning, which is infeasible for long real-world videos that span hours. Since the video is downsampled into fixed-length clips, some query-related frames may be filtered out, which will blur the specific boundary of the target moment, take the adjacent irrelevant frames as new boundaries, easily leading to cross-modal misalignment and introducing both boundary-bias and reasoning-bias. To this end, in this paper, we propose an efficient approach, SpotVMR, to trim the query-relevant clip. Besides, our proposed SpotVMR can serve as plug-and-play module, which achieves efficiency for state-of-the-art VMR methods while maintaining good retrieval performance. Especially, we first design a novel clip search model that learns to identify promising video regions to search conditioned on the language query. Then, we introduce a set of low-cost semantic indexing features to capture the context of objects and interactions that suggest where to search the query-relevant moment. Also, the distillation loss is utilized to address the optimization issues arising from end-to-end joint training of the clip selector and VMR model. Extensive experiments on three challenging datasets demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [145] [Low-Magnification SEM May Suffice: Interpretable Deep Learning for Multi-Scale Fracture-Cause Classification in Zirconia-Toughened Alumina](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29798)
*Julian Schmid,Pawel Astankow,Tom Vater,Julius Beck,Robert Cichon,Danny Krautz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可解释的视觉Transformer（ViT）工作流，用于自动分类氧化铝基复合材料髋/膝关节植入物（BIOLOX delta）的断裂原因，在8493张SEM图像上达到90.7%准确率和0.888宏F1分数，且低倍率（50x）性能与高倍率相当，可作为陶瓷植入物质量保证的补充工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前断口分析工作流程耗时、部分主观且依赖高倍扫描电镜（SEM），亟需一种高效、客观的自动化方法来实现断裂起源的可靠识别，以保障氧化铝基复合材料植入物的质量控制和患者安全。

Method: 从五年生产数据中整理8493张SEM图像（50x-10000x），标注为三种缺陷类别（生坯、硬加工、材料缺陷）；采用微调ViT模型进行分类，并通过感知哈希/SSIM两级泄漏审计确保样本独立性；使用Grad-CAM进行可解释性分析。

Result: 在严重类别不平衡下，分层五折交叉验证达到0.907准确率和0.888宏F1分数；低倍率（50x）性能与高倍率（1k-10kx）相当；Grad-CAM定位到镜面、人字纹、气孔、加工痕迹等典型断口特征。

Conclusion: 可解释ViT可作为陶瓷植入物质量保证的补充工具，支持低倍率预筛选，减少对耗时的高倍率检测的依赖。

Abstract: Reliable identification of fracture origins in alumina matrix composite hip and knee implants is critical for quality assurance and patient safety, yet current fractographic workflows are time-consuming, partly subjective, and reliant on high-magnification scanning electron microscopy (SEM). We present an interpretable vision-transformer (ViT) workflow for automated classification of fracture causes in an alumina matrix composite (BIOLOX delta, CeramTec GmbH) widely used in total joint replacements. A dataset of 8,493 SEM images (50x-10,000x) was curated from five years of in-production burst and proof tests and annotated into three defect categories defined along the manufacturing chain: green body, hard machining, and material defects. Under severe class imbalance, the fine-tuned ViT reached an accuracy of 0.907 and a macro-F1 of 0.888 in stratified five-fold cross-validation, with a two-stage perceptual-hash/SSIM leakage audit confirming negligible specimen overlap. Notably, performance at low magnification (50x) was comparable to that at high magnification (1k-10kx), indicating that macro-scale features - mirror geometry and hackle line fields - already encode sufficient diagnostic signal. Grad-CAM attributions consistently localised on canonical fractographic cues (mirrors, hackles, pores, machining marks), aligning with established fractographic criteria. Together, these results position interpretable ViTs as a complementary tool for ceramic-implant quality assurance, enabling low-magnification pre-screening and reducing reliance on time-intensive high-magnification inspection.

</details>


### [146] [Not All Inputs Are Valid: Towards Open-Set Video Moment Retrieval Using Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29812)
*Xiang Fang,Wanlong Fang,Daizong Liu,Xiaoye Qu,Jianfeng Dong,Pan Zhou,Renfu Li,Zichuan Xu,Lixing Chen,Panpan Zheng,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次提出开放集视频时刻检索（OS-VMR）任务，旨在同时完成基于分布内查询的精确时刻检索和分布外查询的拒绝。提出OpenVMR模型，利用归一化流技术区分ID/OOD查询，并设计多层次跨模态交互和正-无标签学习模块进行时刻检索，在三个数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Video Moment Retrieval方法都基于封闭集假设，即所有查询都是视频相关的。在开放场景下遇到视频无关查询时，模型仍会错误检索，这在安防等高风险场景可能造成不可逆损失。因此需要探索开放集视频时刻检索（OS-VMR）这一新设置。

Method: 提出OpenVMR模型，包含四个关键模块：(1) 利用归一化流学习ID分布，假设ID查询服从多元高斯分布；(2) 引入不确定性分数搜索ID-OOD分离边界；(3) 通过拉近ID查询特征来细化边界；(4) 设计视频-查询粗粒度匹配和帧-查询细粒度匹配进行跨模态交互，最后用正-无标签学习模块进行时刻检索。

Result: 在三个VMR数据集上进行了实验，结果表明OpenVMR在开放集视频时刻检索任务上有效。

Conclusion: 本文首次探索了OS-VMR任务，提出的OpenVMR模型能够有效区分ID和OOD查询，并对ID查询进行精确的时刻检索，为开放场景下的视频时刻检索提供了新思路。

Abstract: Video Moment Retrieval (VMR) targets to retrieve the specific moment corresponding to a sentence query from an untrimmed video. Although recent works have made remarkable progress in this task, they implicitly are rooted in the closed-set assumption that all the given queries as video-relevant\footnote{In this paper, we treat ``video-relevant query'' as ``in-distribution (ID) query'' and ``video-irrelevant query'' as ``out-of-distribution (OOD) query''.}. Given an OOD query in open-set scenarios, they still utilize it for wrong retrieval, which might lead to irrecoverable losses in high-risk scenarios, \textit{e.g.}, criminal activity detection. To this end, we creatively explore a brand-new VMR setting termed Open-Set Video Moment Retrieval (OS-VMR), where we should not only retrieve the precise moments based on ID query, but also reject OOD queries. In this paper, we make the first attempt to step toward OS-VMR and propose a novel model \textbf{OpenVMR}, which first distinguishes ID and OOD queries based on the normalizing flow technology, and then conducts moment retrieval based on ID queries. Specifically, we first learn the ID distribution by constructing a normalizing flow, and assume the ID query distribution obeys the multi-variate Gaussian distribution. Then, we introduce an uncertainty score to search the ID-OOD separating boundary. After that, we refine the ID-OOD boundary by pulling together ID query features. Besides, video-query matching and frame-query matching are designed for coarse-grained and fine-grained cross-modal interaction, respectively. Finally, a positive-unlabeled learning module is introduced for moment retrieval. Experimental results on three VMR datasets show the effectiveness of our OpenVMR.

</details>
