Result: 有效性与隐蔽性存在权衡；黑盒内容重写在排名提升上匹敌或超过梯度攻击，且生成更流畅文本，能在某些领域规避基于关键词和困惑度的检测；模型访问权限不预测攻击强度。

Conclusion: GEO-Bench通过标准化数据集、攻击实现和指标，实现了跨攻击范式的首次直接比较，支持检测方法的发展。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly rank products, documents, and recommendations for user queries, which makes manipulating these rankings a growing concern for fairness and information integrity. Research on generative engine optimization (GEO) has produced many manipulation methods, but each is evaluated on its own dataset with its own metrics, so their relative strength and detectability stay unclear. We present GEO-Bench, a benchmark that evaluates GEO ranking-manipulation attacks under one protocol. It unifies black-box prompt-based attacks (TAP, Zero-Shot), white-box gradient-based attacks (STS, RAF, StealthRank), and ten white-hat C-SEO strategies. We score every method on five datasets against a fixed open-weight ranker (Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct), using metrics for both effectiveness (NRG, Success@α, Promote@α) and stealth (keyword violation rate, perplexity ratio). Our evaluation shows that effectiveness and stealth trade off across adversarial attacks, that black-box content rewriting matches or exceeds gradient-based attacks on rank promotion while producing more fluent text and can evade both keyword- and perplexity-based detection on some domains, and that the access model does not predict attack strength. By standardizing datasets, attack implementations, and metrics, GEO-Bench enables the first direct comparison across these attack paradigms and supports the development of detection methods.

</details>


### [184] [ReasonBreak: Probing Vulnerabilities in Reasoning-Enabled Vision-Language-Action Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29114)
*Mohammadreza Teymoorianfard,Jean-Philippe Monteuuis,Jonathan Petit,Amir Houmansadr*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了具备推理能力的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在自动驾驶中面对现实输入扰动时的鲁棒性问题，发现其推理攻击成功率达89%，轨迹操控达72%，并通过构建评估框架和基准揭示了现有系统的严重安全缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型假设推理与轨迹生成紧密耦合，但它们在现实输入扰动下的鲁棒性尚未被探索，存在潜在安全风险。

Method: 基于NVIDIA Alpamayo模型，进行首次系统性黑盒研究；设计推理感知评估框架，涵盖语义与结构维度；引入安全中心化度量；构建攻击与防御基准。

Result: 推理攻击成功率达89%，轨迹操控达72%；碰撞率上升，安全指标下降。

Conclusion: 推理增强型VLA系统在自动驾驶中存在严重安全漏洞，亟需严格评估和改进防御机制。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models with integrated reasoning have been proposed for end-to-end autonomous driving, assuming a tight coupling between reasoning and trajectory generation. However, the robustness of such systems under realistic input perturbations remains largely unexplored. We show that these models are highly vulnerable to realistic input perturbations, achieving up to 89% attack success rate (ASR) on reasoning and up to 72% on trajectory manipulation in closed-loop simulation, leading to increased collision rates and degraded safety metrics. Using NVIDIA's recent Alpamayo models as representative industry-developed VLAs, we conduct the first systematic black-box study of reasoning-enabled VLA models under realistic textual input corruptions, evaluating their impact on reasoning and driving behavior. We introduce a reasoning-aware evaluation framework capturing both semantic and structural aspects of reasoning, along with safety-centric measures. We also introduce a benchmark for evaluating attacks and defenses on reasoning-trajectory interactions in autonomous driving. Our results highlight the need for rigorous evaluation and improved defenses to ensure the safety of reasoning-enabled VLA systems in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [185] [Domain-Informed Representation for Evolutionary Sieving in Integral and Module Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29169)
*Ahmad Tashfeen,Qi Cheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对经典密码学在量子计算时代面临的威胁，提出了基于最短向量问题(SVP)的量子安全密码学改进方案。通过将Ajtai等人的筛法视为遗传算法(GA)，并融入领域知识的SVP表示和交叉操作，扩展了Laarhoven的方法，使其适用于模块格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统密码学（基于整数分解或离散对数问题）在量子计算机面前存在根本性安全漏洞，且当前加密数据可能在未来被量子计算解密，亟需发展量子安全密码学。

Method: 将Ajtai等人的筛法重新解释为遗传算法，通过引入领域感知的SVP表示和交叉操作来改进Laarhoven的方法，并将该方法自然扩展到模块格场景。

Result: 提出了一种增强的遗传算法框架，能够更有效地解决最短向量问题(SVP)，并适用于模块格这一更广泛的格结构。

Conclusion: 通过在遗传算法中融入领域特定知识，可以提升SVP求解效率，为构建量子安全的密码学系统提供更坚实的基础。

Abstract: Traditional cryptography, rooted in problems, e.g., integer factorisation or discrete log, is inevitably vulnerable to a fully operational quantum computer. Although it remains an engineering frontier, the looming threat extends to encrypted data stored today, which could be decrypted in the future with quantum capabilities. To safeguard against this eventuality, the backbone of the modern quantum-safe cryptography is the Shortest Vector Problem (SVP). We enhance Laarhoven's treatment of Ajtai et al.'s sieving as a genetic algorithm (GA) for the SVP by incorporating domain-informed SVP representation and crossover while naturally extending application to the module lattices.

</details>


### [186] [EvaluatAR: A Cross-Device Evaluation Framework for Rapid Prototyping of Bystander PETs in AR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29177)
*Syed Ibrahim Mustafa Shah Bukhari,Matthew Corbett,Bo Ji,Brendan David-John*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: EvaluatAR 是一个跨设备评估框架，通过记录-回放工作流标准化 PET 输入输出，实现 AR 头显旁观者隐私增强技术的快速原型评估与可重复实验，并在 HoloLens 2、Magic Leap 2 和 Meta Quest 3 上通过三项案例研究验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AR 头显持续感知环境会捕获旁观者，引发隐私风险。现有视觉旁观者隐私增强技术的评估依赖人工、开销高且设备特定，难以跨设备复现，亟需一个标准化的可复现评估框架。

Method: 提出 EvaluatAR 框架，通过记录-回放工作流标准化 PET 的输入（传感器数据和视觉刺激）和输出，实现跨设备实验条件的受控复制。在三款设备（HoloLens 2、Magic Leap 2、Meta Quest 3）上对隐式（连续、上下文驱动）和显式（意图驱动）两类 PET 进行三项案例研究。

Result: (1) 跨设备回放输入揭示了设备特定的隐私-性能权衡；(2) 同一框架工作流可泛化至隐式和显式 PET 设计类别；(3) 回放隐私相关的边缘案例可诊断故障并验证 PET 修改，优于现有基线。

Conclusion: EvaluatAR 支持快速、迭代的 PET 开发，推动了旁观者 PET 在 AR 普及关键时期的可复现跨设备评估。

Abstract: Augmented Reality (AR) headsets continuously sense their surroundings, capturing nearby bystanders and raising privacy risks. Visual bystander privacy-enhancing technologies (PETs) mitigate this risk by detecting bystanders in egocentric scene views and applying privacy transformations (e.g., obfuscation). However, traditional PET evaluation is human-dependent, high-overhead, and device-specific, making it difficult to reproduce across devices. We present EvaluatAR, a cross-device evaluation framework for rapid prototyping at the early stage of PET evaluation. Our framework enables controlled replication of experimental conditions by standardizing PET inputs (sensor data and visual stimuli) and outputs through a record-replay workflow. We validate EvaluatAR through three case studies on HoloLens 2, Magic Leap 2, and Meta Quest 3 across implicit (continuous, context-driven) and explicit (intent-driven) PETs: (1) cross-device replay of inputs to a PET to reveal device-specific privacy-performance trade-offs; (2) generalizability of the same framework workflow across implicit and explicit PET design categories; and (3) replay of privacy-relevant edge cases to diagnose failures and validate PET modifications, yielding an improvement over the state-of-the-art baseline. These results demonstrate EvaluatAR's support for rapid, iterative PET development to advance reproducible cross-device evaluation of bystander PETs at a critical moment in the emergence of ubiquitous AR.

</details>


### [187] [The Best-Laid SCHEMEs: Coordinated Sabotage and Monitoring in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29178)
*Nikolay Radev,Lennart Haas,Benjamin Arnav,Pablo Bernabeu-Pérez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SCHEME基准，评估多智能体编码系统在合法软件工程任务中执行隐蔽恶意目标的能力。实验发现Gemini和Codex分别在84%和46%的样本中成功实现协同破坏，但代码编辑可被可信监控器以低误报率检测（99%/68%），完全监控下未检测破坏率低于3%。告知存在监控器可使Gemini破坏成功率减半而不显著破坏检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多智能体编码系统将工作分解到多个模型实例，一个关键安全问题是这些实例能否在保持与用户意图一致的同时，协调实现隐藏的恶意目标。现有研究缺乏专门评估多智能体隐蔽协同破坏的基准。

Method: 构建SCHEME基准，包含17个任务实例、7个设置和8个真实开源库，每个任务将合法软件工程任务与隐蔽侧任务配对。要求任何子集智能体无法单独成功：智能体必须分解共享破坏计划、在不同通信拓扑下传递部分需求、执行相互一致的编辑。使用GPT 5.1 Codex和Gemini 3.1 Pro进行评估。

Result: Gemini在84%样本中完成隐蔽目标同时成功完成合法任务，Codex为46%。失败分析显示模型差距源于恢复能力而非失败频率：Gemini恢复约73%的通信失败，Codex约56%。可信监控器仅从代码编辑即可在99%/68%（Gemini/Codex）的尝试中以FPR≤2%检测协同破坏，加入通信后升至100%/81%。完全监控下未检测破坏率低于3%。告知存在监控器使Gemini破坏成功率减半。

Conclusion: 多智能体编码系统的协同破坏已具实际可行性，但代码层面的编辑仍可被可靠检测。监控感知提示能显著降低破坏成功率，且不破坏检测能力，表明监控机制是有效的防御手段。

Abstract: As agentic coding systems decompose work across multiple model instances, a critical safety question is whether those instances can coordinate to achieve a hidden malicious objective while remaining aligned with user intent. We introduce SCHEME, a benchmark of 17 task instances across 7 settings and 8 real open-source libraries, each pairing a legitimate software-engineering task with a covert side task. Every setting is designed so that no proper subset of agents can succeed alone: agents must decompose a shared sabotage plan, relay partial requirements under different communication topologies, and execute mutually consistent edits, testing genuine multi-agent coordination rather than individual capability. Evaluating with GPT 5.1 Codex and Gemini 3.1 Pro, we find coordinated sabotage is already practical, with Gemini completing the covert objective while succeeding on the legitimate task in 84\% of samples and Codex in 46\%. A failure analysis shows that recovery, not failure incidence, drives the model gap: both models suffer similar numbers of communication failures per trajectory, but Gemini recovers from $\sim$73\% of occurrences against Codex's $\sim$56\%. However, the agents' code edits remain detectable: a trusted monitor flags coordinated sabotage at FPR$\le$2\% in 99\%/68\% (Gemini/Codex) of attempts from code edits alone, rising to 100\%/81\% with access to agent communication. Despite stealth-aware prompting, undetected sabotage stays at $<$3\% under the full-visibility monitor; telling agents a monitor is present halves Gemini's sabotage success without significantly breaking detection, even when agents are explicitly prompted to fabricate cover stories.

</details>


### [188] [SAMD: A Tool for Identifying False Data Injection Scenarios in AI/ML-enabled Medical Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29210)
*Mohammadreza Hallajiyan,Xueren Ge,Athish Pranav Dharmalingam,Gargi Mitra,Shahrear Iqbal,Homa Alemzadeh,Karthik Pattabiraman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出SAMD自动化工具体系，用于在AI/ML医疗设备设计阶段通过STPA-Sec分析识别安全漏洞和攻击路径。在5个FDA批准的医疗设备案例中，实现了100%技术识别精度、63.2%已知漏洞检索精度和95.3%攻击场景生成准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI/ML在医疗系统中的集成带来了新的安全风险，尤其是 adversaries 通过脆弱组件注入虚假数据导致误诊和错误治疗。这些风险在设计阶段难以预见，因为系统组装部分发生在最终用户实际使用过程中，亟需在设计阶段自动化的安全分析工具。

Method: 提出SAMD工具，将医疗系统建模为控制结构，将所有组件视为向ML引擎注入虚假数据的潜在攻击点；结合最新漏洞数据库和大语言模型（LLMs）自动化漏洞发现，生成潜在攻击场景列表。

Result: 在5个FDA批准的医疗设备案例研究中，SAMD在文档中识别目标设备技术时达到100%精度；检索已知漏洞的精度为63.2%；生成ML模型相关攻击场景的准确率为95.3%，最长处理时间为191.64秒。

Conclusion: SAMD能够在医疗AI/ML设备设计阶段有效自动化识别漏洞和攻击路径，具有高精度和高效性，为医疗设备安全设计提供了实用的安全分析工具。

Abstract: The growing integration of artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) in medical systems requires effective measures to address emerging security risks. One such risk is that of adversaries introducing false data through vulnerable system components during inference, causing misdiagnosis and wrong treatments. These risks are challenging to anticipate and address in the design phase, as the system assembly partially occurs during actual use by end users. To address this concern, we introduce SAMD, an automated tool for performing System Theoretic Process Analysis for Security (STPA-Sec) on AI/ML-enabled medical devices during the design phase. SAMD models the medical system as a control structure, treating all system components as potential points for injecting false data into the ML engine. It leverages state-of-the-art vulnerability databases and Large Language Models (LLMs) to automate vulnerability discovery and generate a list of potential attack scenarios. We demonstrate SAMD's effectiveness through case studies on five FDA-cleared medical devices, showcasing its ability to identify vulnerable points and potential attack paths. We find that SAMD has 100% precision in identifying target device technologies in the case studies' documents, retrieves the known vulnerabilities linked to them (with 63.2% precision), and generates highly relevant attack scenarios on the ML model, including detailed steps that an adversary might take (with 95.3% accuracy, and the highest time taken being 191.64s).

</details>


### [189] [Evolving Skill-Structured Attack Memory Enhances LLM Jailbreaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29237)
*Junke Zhang,Jianwei Wang,Sishuo Chen,Yizhang He,Qingshuai Feng,Zhengyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MemoAttack提出了一种基于记忆驱动的黑盒越狱攻击框架，通过技能结构化的攻击记忆建模、生命周期驱动的记忆演化和探索-利用平衡的记忆选择，在AdvBench上实现了98.00%的平均攻击成功率，比最强基线高出16.67个百分点，同时减少了45.9%的请求量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒越狱方法依赖逐样本启发式搜索或通过积累策略池/方法库来利用攻击经验，缺乏对攻击经验的系统化组织和管理，导致效率低下且经验利用不充分。

Method: MemoAttack包含三个核心设计：(1) 技能结构化记忆建模——将积累的攻击经验抽象为可复用的技能结构化攻击记忆，每个单元包含攻击技能、模板、证据和生命周期状态；(2) 生命周期驱动的记忆演化——通过基于证据的试用、晋升、退休、重新激活、淘汰和存储清理来演化记忆；(3) 探索-利用平衡的记忆选择——通过上下文汤普森采样在可靠记忆复用与不确定性驱动的探索之间取得平衡。

Result: 在AdvBench上，MemoAttack实现了98.00%的平均攻击成功率，比最强基线高出16.67个百分点，同时将请求数量减少了45.9%。

Conclusion: MemoAttack通过系统化组织和管理攻击经验，不仅在攻击成功率和效率上显著优于现有方法，而且随着记忆在更多样本上的积累能够持续改进，展示了记忆驱动方法的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Jailbreak attacks on large language models (LLMs) aim to induce LLMs to produce content that they are expected to refuse. Automated black-box jailbreak generation is especially important for safety evaluation, where the attacker observes only model outputs and needs to automatically search for effective adversarial prompts. Existing black-box jailbreak methods either depend on sample-wise heuristic search or leverage attack experience through accumulating strategy pools or method libraries, lacking a systematic organization and management of attack experience. To mitigate these drawbacks, we propose MemoAttack, a memory-driven black-box jailbreak framework with comprehensive attack memory modeling, evolution, and selection. Specifically, MemoAttack comprises three key designs: (1) Skill-Structured Memory Modeling, which abstracts accumulated attack experience into reusable skill-structured attack memory whose units pair attack skills with templates, evidence, and lifecycle state; (2) Lifecycle-Driven Memory Evolution, which evolves the memory through evidence-based probation, promotion, retirement, reactivation, elimination, and storage cleanup; and (3) Explore-Exploit Balanced Memory Selection, which balances reliable memory reuse with uncertainty-driven exploration via contextual Thompson Sampling. Experiments on AdvBench demonstrate that MemoAttack achieves an average attack success rate of 98.00%, outperforming the strongest baseline by 16.67 percentage points, while reducing request count by 45.9%. Moreover, MemoAttack continuously improves as memory accumulates over more samples.

</details>


### [190] [Implicit Identity Technologies for LLMs: Fingerprinting and Watermarking across Datasets, Models, and Generated Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29245)
*Bing Liu,Shunping Wang,Yufan Zhu,Xinyi Yu,Jing Huang,Linkang Du,Hongbin Pei,Wei Luo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对LLM指纹识别与水印技术进行了系统综述，提出了"隐式身份"这一统一抽象概念，区分了指纹识别（非侵入式）与水印（侵入式），并基于生命周期提出了分类体系，涵盖数据集、模型和生成内容三个层面，按验证语义分为基于相似性的归因和基于密钥的验证，最后建立了以可识别性、鲁棒性和可部署性为核心的评价框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM需要大量数据、计算和专业知识投入，且越来越多地部署在高风险场景中，因此保护LLM相关资产并追溯其来源至关重要。然而现有工作分散在数据集溯源、模型所有权和生成内容检测等不同领域，指纹识别和水印术语使用不一致，方法通常在孤立的特定资产环境中研究，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 提出"隐式身份"作为统一抽象概念，区分指纹识别（基于内在特征的非侵入式身份）和水印（故意嵌入数据、模型或生成内容中的侵入式身份）；提出基于生命周期的分类体系，按数据集、模型和生成内容组织技术，并进一步按验证语义（基于相似性的归因和基于密钥的验证）分离；建立以可识别性、鲁棒性和可部署性为中心的评价框架。

Result: 统一了术语、生命周期阶段和评价目标，提供了一个研究LLM身份技术的结构化基础。

Conclusion: 本文通过统一术语、生命周期阶段和评价目标，为LLM身份技术研究提供了结构化基础，有助于开发更可靠的资产保护和溯源机制。

Abstract: This paper presents a survey and taxonomy of LLM fingerprinting and watermarking for identity, ownership verification, provenance, and generated-content attribution. Large language models (LLMs) require substantial investments in data, computation, and expertise, and are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings, making it critical to protect LLM-related assets and trace their origins. Existing work has rapidly expanded across dataset provenance, model ownership, and generated-content detection, but the field remains fragmented: fingerprinting and watermarking are often used inconsistently, and methods are typically studied within isolated asset-specific settings. To address this gap, we introduce implicit identity as a unifying abstraction for verifiable but not directly observable identity signals in LLM systems. We distinguish fingerprinting as non-intrusive identity derived from intrinsic characteristics, and watermarking as intrusive identity deliberately embedded into data, models, or generated content. We then propose a lifecycle-based taxonomy that organises techniques across datasets, models, and generated content, and further separates them by verification semantics: similarity-based attribution and keyed verification. Finally, we establish an evaluation framework centred on identifiability, robustness, and deployability, summarising representative metrics under realistic access and transformation regimes. By unifying terminology, lifecycle stages, and evaluation objectives, this survey provides a structured foundation for studying LLM identity technologies and for developing more reliable mechanisms for asset protection and provenance.

</details>


### [191] [AliMark: Enhancing Robustness of Sentence-Level Watermarking Against Text Paraphrasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29434)
*Yuexin Li,Wenjie Qu,Linyu Wu,Yulin Chen,Yufei He,Tri Cao,Bryan Hooi,Jiaheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AliMark 将句子级水印重新定义为比特序列编码与对齐问题，采用两阶段检测策略（生成多种文本重构变体 + 自适应对齐），显著提升了在句子拆分/合并等结构扰动下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有句子级水印方法（前缀式设计）在强释义器（如DIPPER、GPT-3.5）造成的句子拆分、合并等结构扰动下鲁棒性不足。

Method: 将句子级水印建模为比特序列编码与对齐问题；采用两阶段检测：1）生成多种重构文本变体；2）自适应对齐提取的比特序列与秘密比特序列，最小化对齐成本。

Result: 在多种释义攻击下，AliMark 显著优于现有最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: 多候选对齐设计天然提升了模型对句子合并与拆分操作的鲁棒性，有效解决了结构扰动下的水印检测问题。

Abstract: Existing sentence-level watermarking methods enhance robustness to paraphrasing by anchoring watermarks in sentence semantics. However, their prefix-based designs remain vulnerable to structural perturbations, such as sentence splitting and merging, which commonly arise under strong paraphrasers like DIPPER and GPT-3.5. To mitigate this issue, we propose AliMark, a framework that reformulates sentence-level watermarking as a bit sequence encoding and alignment problem between a potentially watermarked text and a secret bit sequence. Notably, our approach adopts a two-stage detection strategy: we generate multiple restructured text variants and adaptively align their extracted bit sequences with the secret bit sequence to minimize alignment cost. This multi-candidate alignment design naturally improves robustness to sentence merges and splits. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AliMark substantially outperforms state-of-the-art baselines under diverse paraphrasing attacks.

</details>


### [192] [Protecting On-Device AI Inference: A Systematic Review of Attacks and Defence Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29450)
*Zisis Tsiatsikas,Alexandros Fakis,Georgios Karopoulos,Vasileios Kouliaridis,Marios Anagnostopoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次全面综述了针对移动端设备上AI推理的安全与隐私威胁及防御机制，发现攻击与防御文献存在严重不平衡：约三分之一的攻击文献关注对抗性攻击，但几乎没有对应的防御研究；而约四分之一的攻击文献关注知识产权攻击，却有半数防御方案针对此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着预训练AI模型在移动端设备上进行客户端推理的场景日益增多，模型窃取/提取、对抗性攻击和数据泄露等风险不断上升。现有综述主要集中在边缘智能（包括分布式训练），忽视了设备端AI推理特有的安全与隐私问题。

Method: 对威胁和设备端推理对应的防御机制进行了全面综述，系统分类并统计了攻击与防御文献的分布情况。

Result: 攻击与防御文献分布严重不均衡：约四分之一的攻击论文聚焦于知识产权攻击，而半数防御方案解决同一问题；对抗性攻击约占攻击文献的三分之一，但几乎没有防御论文与之对应。

Conclusion: 已知攻击与可用防御之间存在明显的不对称性，这为未来在设备端AI推理安全防护方面的研究提供了明确机会。

Abstract: The need for secure and private Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Machine Learning (ML) on edge and mobile devices has increased the necessity of protecting the architecture of these systems from threats to both security and privacy. With an ever-increasing number of pre-trained AI models being used on mobile platforms for client-side inference, there are rising concerns about the risks associated with the theft/extraction of AI models, adversarial attacks on AI models, and data breaches. As a result of this trend, a variety of defence mechanisms have been proposed to protect against these threats. These include Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs), homomorphic encryption, obfuscation, and differential privacy, among others. However, current surveys largely focus on edge intelligence, which includes distributed training, and thus overlook security and privacy issues that are specific to on-device AI inference. To the best of our knowledge, this paper presents the first comprehensive review of threats and corresponding defence mechanisms targeting on-device inference. Our results show that the attack and defence literature are unbalanced: approximately one quarter of the surveyed attack papers focus on Intellectual Property (IP) attacks, whereas half of the defence solutions tackle the same issue. More importantly, some attack categories have no defence paper associated to them, such as adversarial attacks that account for roughly one third of the attack literature. This asymmetry between known attacks and available mitigations highlights clear opportunities for future research on securing on-device AI inference.

</details>


### [193] [Bridging Theory and Practice: An Executable Taxonomy of Security Properties for ProVerif and Tamarin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29465)
*Leonard Tudorache,Ivan Kurtev,Mark van den Brand*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过系统综述53篇（2022-2025年）使用ProVerif和Tamarin的研究，构建了一个安全属性的分类体系，提供非正式和形式化定义（一阶逻辑），并给出两种工具的可执行建模示例，旨在降低协议设计者使用形式化验证工具的门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化验证工具（如ProVerif、Tamarin）对保障协议安全至关重要，但其使用需要专门的领域知识，给安全背景而非形式化验证背景的协议设计者造成了显著的学习门槛，亟需结构化的、可访问的资源来帮助其表达设计和需求。

Method: 通过对2022-2025年间53篇使用ProVerif和Tamarin的文献进行系统性综述，归纳并分类安全属性；为非正式定义提供直观理解，同时用一阶逻辑给出严格形式化定义；进一步详述建模模式，并在开放仓库中提供两种工具的可执行示例。

Result: 构建了一个系统性的、基于证据的安全属性分类体系，包含非正式与形式化两级定义，以及可复用的ProVerif和Tamarin建模示例，所有代码和模型均收集于开放仓库中。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了理论安全属性定义与其可执行验证模型之间的鸿沟，推动了形式化验证工具在安全协议设计者中的可用性和可及性。

Abstract: Security is critical for everything relying on modern digital systems. Because almost all digital interactions are governed by the Internet and cryptographic protocols, these protocols must serve as reliable mechanisms that guarantee core security properties, such as confidentiality and integrity. Formal verification of these protocols is a critical step in securing interconnected systems. Tools such as ProVerif and Tamarin are widely employed to perform automated verification. However, their effective use demands specialized domain knowledge, creating a significant learning curve for security protocol designers who often have a security, rather than a formal verification background. We therefore need structured, accessible resources to help protocol designers to express their design and requirements in the language of the formal verification tools.
  To address this, we introduce a systematic and evidence-based taxonomy of security properties. This taxonomy is derived from a literature review of 53 recent studies (2022-2025) that used ProVerif and Tamarin, providing an up-to-date view of verified properties. We systematically categorize and define these properties, providing both informal definitions for intuitive comprehension and rigorous formal definitions expressed in first-order logic for clarity and consistency. We further detail modeling patterns and implement executable examples in both ProVerif and Tamarin, collected in an open repository. This work advances the state of the art by bridging the gap between theoretical security property definitions and their practical, executable verification models.

</details>


### [194] [SciIntBench: Measuring LLM Compliance with Research Integrity Norms Under Adversarial Framing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29468)
*Almene De Meran Meguimtsop,Maria Leonor Pacheco,Daniel E. Acuna*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了SciIntBench——一个涵盖10类RCR规范、3个科学领域的810条对抗性基准测试集，用于评估LLM在科研诚信方面的对齐程度。通过对16个主流LLM（2024-2026年）生成12,960条回复的分析，发现模型对显性不端行为的拒绝远优于隐性违规，尤其在压力驱动的违规场景下表现薄弱；不同RCR类别的拒绝率差异显著，透明性、抄袭和伪造方面的边界最模糊。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被越来越多地用于支持科研工作，但其是否遵守科研诚信规范尚不明确，现有评估缺乏对LLM在科研不端行为场景下对齐程度的系统研究。

Method: 构建SciIntBench对抗性基准，包含10个RCR类别×3个科学领域的810条提示，每条提示以显性对抗、隐性对抗和良性三种变体呈现；评估6家提供商的16个商业及开源LLM，共计12,960条回复。

Result: LLM对显性不端行为的拒绝率远高于隐性违规；压力驱动的违规场景中模型表现最差；不同RCR类别间拒绝率存在显著差异，透明性、抄袭和伪造类别的边界最弱。

Conclusion: 当前的LLM在科研诚信对齐方面存在显著的框架敏感性缺陷，尤其难以识别和拒绝隐蔽形式的科研不端行为，需要在隐性违规场景下加强对齐。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to support scientific work, but it is unclear whether they uphold responsible conduct of research (RCR) norms or help undermine them. We introduce SciIntBench, an adversarial benchmark of 810 prompts across ten RCR categories and three scientific domains. Each scenario appears as an Overt Adversarial, Covert Adversarial, and Benign version, allowing us to jointly measure framing-sensitive refusal of misconduct and helpfulness on legitimate requests. We evaluate 16 commercial and open-weight LLMs from six providers (2024--2026), producing 12,960 responses. We find that scientific integrity alignment is strongly framing-sensitive: models refuse explicit misconduct far more reliably than covert violations, especially failing when misconduct is presented as a pressure-driven shortcut. Refusals vary by RCR category, with weaker boundaries around transparency, plagiarism, and fabrication.

</details>


### [195] [KBF: Knowledge Boundary as Fingerprint for Language Model and Black-Box API Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29524)
*Yijia Fang,Yiqing Feng,Bingyu Li,Mingxun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: KBF是一种低成本的黑盒审计协议，通过在知识边界附近使用稳定数值召回来指纹模型API。在16个生产LLM端点的测试中，KBF标记了所有155个经济相关的替代攻击，没有错误拒绝任何同模型控制案例，能够检测仅5-10%流量被替换的混合路由攻击，并在六平台影子API审计中发现27个平台模型单元中有7个与其参考端点统计不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中继和转售API日益成为访问大型语言模型的中间层，但用户无法直接验证所声称的端点是否真正服务于广告宣传的模型，存在模型替换或降级的经济动机。

Method: KBF利用知识边界附近稳定的数值召回行为作为模型指纹，通过低成本的黑盒审计协议来验证LLM端点是否为所声称的模型。

Result: 在16个生产LLM端点上，KBF成功识别所有155个经济相关的模型替代攻击，无假阳性；在仅5-10%流量被替换时即可检测高分离度混合路由攻击；在六平台影子API审计中，27个模型单元中有7个与其参考端点统计不一致，主要集中于高端Claude端点。

Conclusion: KBF作为一种低成本、高精度的黑盒审计工具，能够有效检测LLM API中的模型替代行为，尤其在高端模型端点上的不一致现象值得关注。

Abstract: Relay and reseller APIs increasingly intermediate access to large language models (LLMs), but users have no direct way to verify that a claimed endpoint is actually serving the advertised model. We introduce KBF, a low-cost black-box auditing protocol that fingerprints model APIs using stable numerical recall near the knowledge boundary. Across 16 production LLM endpoints, KBF flags all 155 economically relevant substitutions without rejecting any same-model controls, remains stable under deployment variation, detects high-separation mixed-routing attacks when only 5-10% of traffic is substituted, and finds that 7 of 27 platform model cells in a six-platform shadow API audit are statistically inconsistent with their reference endpoints, with inconsistencies concentrated on premium Claude endpoints.

</details>


### [196] [Temporal Motif-aware Graph Test-time Adaptation for OOD Blockchain Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29526)
*Runang He,Tongya Zheng,Huiling Peng,Yuanyu Wan,Bingde Hu,Jiawei Chen,Canghong Jin,Mingli Song,Can Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TEMG-TTA框架，通过捕获地址的3节点时序模体分布并结合测试时自适应策略，解决区块链异常检测中对抗性模式演化与分布外问题，在5个数据集上平均提升54.88%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图异常检测方法面临两大挑战：恶意行为者导致的对抗性模式演化，以及区块链上多样化交易语义引起的分布外问题。

Method: 1) 设计高效计算机制全面捕获每个活跃地址的3节点时序模体分布，实现时序模体感知图学习；2) 设计简单有效的测试时自适应策略，促进训练图与测试图间共享共同模式。

Result: 在5个真实数据集上，TEMG-TTA相比最先进的GAD方法平均提升54.88%。案例研究进一步验证了其可解释性。

Conclusion: TEMG-TTA通过时序模体感知与测试时自适应有效刻画异常地址的复杂交易模式，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Ever-evolving transaction patterns have significantly hindered anomaly detection on emerging cryptocurrency blockchains due to the vast number of addresses and diverse anomalous behaviors. Recently, advanced Graph Anomaly Detection (GAD) approaches applied to blockchains have faced two critical challenges: \textit{adversarial pattern evolution by malicious actors} and \textit{the out-of-distribution (OOD) problem caused by varied transaction semantics on blockchains}. To address these challenges, we propose a novel framework termed \textbf{TE}mporal \textbf{M}otif-aware \textbf{G}raph \textbf{T}est-\textbf{T}ime \textbf{A}daptation (\textbf{TEMG-TTA}). First, we comprehensively capture the 3-node temporal motif distribution of each active address using an efficient computational mechanism, enabling downstream temporal motif-aware graph learning. Second, we design a simple yet effective test-time adaptation strategy to facilitate the sharing of common patterns between training and testing graphs. Extensive experiments on 5 real-world datasets demonstrate that our proposed \textbf{TEMG-TTA} outperforms \textit{state-of-the-art} GAD approaches by an average of 54.88\%. A further case study on interpretable motif patterns reveals that \textbf{TEMG-TTA} explicitly characterizes the complex transaction patterns of anomalous addresses, thereby verifying the effectiveness of our technical designs. Our code will be made publicly available https://github.com/LuoXishuang0712/TEMG-TTA/.

</details>


### [197] [LoRA-Key: User-Centric LoRA Watermarking for Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29569)
*Yaopeng Wang,Qingliang Wang,Zhibo Wang,Huiyu Xu,Jiacheng Du,Qiu Wang,Jia-Li Yin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LoRA-Key提出了一种用户中心的LoRA水印框架，通过训练一个可重用的Watermark LoRA模块，以无训练线性叠加方式嵌入可恢复的密文信息，实现无需逐LoRA重训练的版权保护，并引入梯度正交投影减少对生成质量的干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA模块易于复制和未经授权重用，而现有水印方法要么保护基础模型，要么需要针对每个目标LoRA进行水印感知重训练，缺乏在开放社区中的实用性。

Method: LoRA-Key将版权保护视为可重用所有权密钥：1) 在冻结VAE潜空间中建立潜在水印先验用于鲁棒消息嵌入与恢复；2) 通过消息条件水印监督和语义一致性约束优化Watermark LoRA；3) 提出梯度正交投影(GOP)抑制与语义保持方向冲突的水印更新。

Result: 实验表明LoRA-Key提供轻量级即插即用版权保护，保持生成质量和风格保真度，在图像级失真、下游微调和多LoRA组合下维持鲁棒的所有权验证。

Conclusion: LoRA-Key实现了无需逐LoRA重训练的可重用水印机制，为LoRA生态系统提供了实用的版权保护方案。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has become a widely used mechanism for customizing text-to-image diffusion models, enabling lightweight modules that are shared, reused, and commercialized as independent assets. This LoRA-centric ecosystem shifts copyright protection from foundation models to distributed LoRA modules, which are easy to copy, redistribute, or reuse without authorization. Existing watermarking methods either protect the base diffusion model or require watermark-aware retraining for each target LoRA, limiting their practicality in open community settings. To address this limitation, we propose LoRA-Key, a user-centric LoRA watermarking framework that treats copyright protection as a reusable ownership key. LoRA-Key encapsulates a recoverable secret message into a standalone user-specific Watermark LoRA, which can be attached to different target LoRAs through training-free linear superposition without per-LoRA retraining or structural modification. To train such a reusable key, we first establish a latent watermark prior in the frozen VAE latent space for robust message embedding and recovery, and then optimize the Watermark LoRA with message-conditioned watermark supervision and semantic consistency constraints. We further introduce Gradient Orthogonal Projection (GOP) to suppress watermark updates that conflict with semantic-preserving directions, reducing interference with generation fidelity and downstream style adaptation. Extensive experiments show that LoRA-Key provides lightweight plug-and-play copyright protection while preserving generation quality and style fidelity, and maintains robust ownership verification under image-level distortions, downstream fine-tuning, and multi-LoRA composition.

</details>


### [198] [Scarcity Is Not Enough: An Impossibility Result for Linear Sybil Cost Under Parallelizable Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29651)
*Homayoun Maleki,Nekane Sainz,Jon Legarda,Igor Santos-Grueiro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明：在去许可系统中，仅靠资源稀缺性不足以抵抗女巫攻击。若资源满足可分性、影响力可加性、时间可重用性和身份可转移性，则攻击者能以亚线性成本集中影响力（C(s,T)=o(sT)），这是协议设计无法避免的不可能性结果。而吞吐受限、不可转移、窗口本地的资源则可强制线性成本（C(s,T)=Ω(sT)）。两类资源存在渐近分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战“稀缺性即可抵抗女巫攻击”的传统认知，揭示资源的结构属性（而非稀缺性本身）才是决定攻击成本的关键因素。

Method: 通过定义对抗成本函数C(s,T)，建立形式化模型分析资源属性（可分性、可加性、时间可重用性、可转移性）对影响力集中成本的影响，并给出渐近复杂度下界证明。

Result: 1. 满足四种平行化属性的资源必然导致影响力摊销，攻击成本为o(sT)；2. 吞吐受限、不可转移、窗口本地资源可达到Ω(sT)的线性成本；3. 两类资源在渐近意义上存在严格分离。

Conclusion: 任何希望实现影响力集中成本线性的机制，必须将参与基础建立在至少违反一种平行化属性的资源之上。

Abstract: Permissionless systems resist Sybil attacks by binding influence to scarce resources. We show that scarcity alone is insufficient: the structural properties of the resource determine whether influence can be concentrated at sublinear cost through identity replication, delegation, or pooling.
  We model this through the adversarial cost C(s,T): the minimum expenditure required to achieve influence proportional to s independent participation units over T windows. We prove that any resource satisfying divisibility, additivity of influence, temporal reusability, and identity transferability admits influence amortization: C(s,T)=o(sT), regardless of protocol design. This is an impossibility result: no protocol rule can enforce linear cost of influence concentration over a structurally parallelizable resource.
  We further prove that throughput-bounded, non-transferable, window-local resources enforce C(s,T)=Omega(sT): each additional unit of sustained influence incurs marginal cost Delta(s,T)=Omega(T), growing with the time horizon. The two resource classes are asymptotically separated.
  As a direct design consequence, any mechanism targeting linear cost of influence concentration must ground participation in a resource that violates at least one parallelizability property.

</details>


### [199] [Minimal Prompt Perturbations Lead to Code Vulnerabilities: Prompt Fragility and Hidden-State Signals in Coding LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29737)
*Alexander Sternfeld,Andrei Kucharavy,Ljiljana Dolamic*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM代码生成助手的代码安全问题，发现即使是单字符级别的微小提示扰动，也能导致生成的代码从安全变为不安全。通过探测模型隐藏状态，发现输入处理类漏洞比安全默认值类漏洞更可预测，表明前者可在生成前检测，后者需要解码时干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于LLM的编码助手被广泛采用并实际生成生产代码，其安全性变得至关重要。已有研究关注过提示扰动对功能正确性的影响，但提示扰动是否会破坏生成代码的安全性尚未被研究。

Method: 在三种模型和五种编程语言上，对提示应用token级别的突变（小至单字符变化），并探测模型的隐藏状态来分析代码安全性的变化。

Result: 单字符级别的提示突变就能导致生成的代码从安全变为脆弱。隐藏状态分析显示，输入处理类漏洞（如缺少验证或消毒）的可预测性更高（平均AUC 0.753），而安全默认值类漏洞（如弱算法或不安全参数）的可预测性较低（平均AUC 0.674）。

Conclusion: LLM辅助编码的威胁模型不仅限于提示注入，还应包括普通的提示变化。输入处理类缺陷可在生成前通过隐藏状态捕获，而安全默认值类缺陷需要在解码阶段进行干预。

Abstract: LLM-based coding assistants are seeing rapid adoption, offering substantial gains in developer productivity. As organizations increasingly ship code these agents produce, the security of that code becomes critical. Prior work has shown that minor prompt perturbations degrade the functional correctness of LLM-generated code, but whether they also compromise code security has remained unstudied. We apply token-level mutations to prompts across three models and five programming languages, and show that mutations as small as a single-character change can flip generated code from secure to vulnerable. Probing the models' hidden states reveals that this fragility is partially encoded in prompt representations, but unevenly so. Input-handling vulnerabilities, where the model omits validation or sanitization, are more predictable (mean AUC 0.753) than secure-defaults vulnerabilities, where insecure code stems from one local choice such as a weak algorithm or unsafe parameter (mean AUC 0.674). These results show that the threat model for LLM-assisted coding extends beyond prompt injection to ordinary prompt variation, and indicate that input-handling flaws can be caught before generation while secure-defaults flaws require intervention during decoding.

</details>


### [200] [Cert-LAS: Toward Certified Model Ownership Verification for Text-to-Image Diffusion Models via Layer-Adaptive Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29809)
*Leyi Qi,Yiming Li,Siyuan Liang,Zhengzhong Tu,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Cert-LAS，首个基于层自适应平滑的认证模型所有权验证方法，用于T2I扩散模型，能在恶意去除攻击下仍实现可靠的水印验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于后门的扩散模型水印方法隐含假设验证过程是"忠实"的，即验证者能查询到模型完整的水印响应。但实践中攻击者可能有意或无意破坏水印信号，导致验证可靠性严重下降。

Method: Cert-LAS利用扩散分类器和LFS引导的层自适应噪声嵌入指定水印，通过假设检验比对疑似模型与水印参考模型的响应强度差异来验证所有权。在特定条件下证明了即使面对恶意去除攻击仍能可靠验证。

Result: 大量实验验证了Cert-LAS的有效性及其对自适应攻击的抵抗能力。

Conclusion: Cert-LAS首次为T2I扩散模型提供了认证级所有权验证保障，显著提升了水印验证在对抗环境下的可靠性。

Abstract: Large-scale text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models have enabled unprecedented creative applications, but their unauthorized use has raised serious intellectual property concerns, making model ownership verification (MOV) increasingly critical. We find that existing backdoor-based diffusion watermarking methods often (implicitly) assume a "faithful" verification process, namely, that the verifier can query a suspicious model and obtain the faithful watermark response to complete MOV. However, in practice, adversaries may intentionally or unintentionally damage potential watermark signals, significantly degrading verification reliability. To address this issue, we propose Cert-LAS, the first certified MOV method for T2I models based on layer-adaptive smoothing. In general, Cert-LAS embeds specified watermarks using diffusion classifiers and an LFS-guided layer-adaptive noise, and verifies ownership by examining whether the suspected model exhibits significantly stronger watermark responses compared to unwatermarked references through hypothesis testing. We further prove that, under certain conditions, our Cert-LAS can still achieve reliable verification even in the presence of malicious removal attacks. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of Cert-LAS and its resistance to adaptive attacks. Our code is available at https://github.com/Leyi-Qi/Cert-LAS.

</details>


### [201] [Ciphera: A Decentralised Biometric Identity Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29868)
*Ankit Kanaiyalal Prajapati,Shahzad Memon,Mohammed Mahir Rahman,Ameer Al-Nemrat*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Ciphera，一个结合隐私保护面部识别、多节点验证、IPFS凭证存储和区块链撤销的去中心化生物识别身份框架。实验显示81%功能成功率，p95验证延迟约820ms，但存在撤销传播延迟、审计日志不一致及活体检测不足等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心化生物识别身份系统存在单点故障、验证过程不透明和生物特征不可逆泄露等风险。尽管DID和VC提供了更强的隐私保障，但它们与生物识别认证及分布式验证的集成仍未被充分探索。

Method: Ciphera框架包含隐私保护面部识别、多节点验证、基于IPFS的凭证元数据存储以及基于区块链的撤销机制。通过功能测试、性能测试、安全分析和分布式一致性评估进行验证。

Result: 功能成功率为81%；p95验证延迟约820ms（并发多节点条件）；安全性分析确认了强机密性和完整性保障，但活体检测不完整导致易受深度伪造和重放攻击；存在撤销传播延迟和审计日志不一致问题。

Conclusion: 研究证明了去中心化生物识别身份的可实现性，同时指出了生产级部署面临的关键工程挑战，如撤销传播延迟、审计一致性和活体检测缺陷等。

Abstract: Centralised biometric identity systems expose users to single points of failure, opaque verification processes, and irreversible biometric compromise. Decentralised Identifiers (DIDs) and Verifiable Credentials (VCs) offer stronger privacy guarantees, yet their integration with biometric authentication and distributed verification remains insufficiently explored. This paper presents Ciphera, a decentralised biometric identity framework combining privacy-preserving facial recognition, multi-node verification, IPFS-based credential metadata storage, and blockchain-anchored revocation. Evaluated across functional, performance, security, and distributed consistency dimensions, Ciphera achieved an 81% functional success rate, with stable enrolment and authentication but measurable revocation propagation delays and occasional audit-log inconsistencies. Performance testing demonstrated sub-second p95 verification latency of approximately 820ms under concurrent multi-node conditions. Security analysis confirmed strong confidentiality and integrity guarantees, though incomplete liveness detection leaves susceptibility to deepfake and replay attacks. The results demonstrate the feasibility of decentralised biometric identity while identifying key engineering challenges for production-grade deployment.

</details>


### [202] [Dissecting the Black Box: Circuit-Level Analysis of LLM Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29901)
*Syafiq Al Atiiq,Chun Zhou,Christian Gehrmann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过机械可解释性方法分析Gemma-2-2b模型在代码漏洞检测中的内部机制，发现模型主要依赖安全模式检测器（而非直接检测漏洞特征），识别出关键神经组件（L5/L7层的注意力头和L7层的MLP神经元），并通过消融实验验证其因果关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能够检测软件漏洞，但其内部识别机制尚不明确。作者希望从机械可解释性角度揭示模型如何区分安全代码与易受攻击代码的底层计算逻辑。

Method: 使用Circuit Tracer对Gemma-2-2b模型进行电路追踪，分析其在472个C/C++代码样本分类时的计算路径；通过消融实验验证关键组件（注意力头、MLP神经元）的因果作用。

Result: 模型主要依赖安全模式检测器而非漏洞特征检测；识别出L5/L7层注意力头负责安全模式识别，L7层MLP神经元编码漏洞相关特征；移除L11层导致检测准确率从100%降至6%，消融L7层20个神经元使准确率降低50%。仅16%的模型容量参与检测电路。

Conclusion: LLM漏洞检测使用稀疏、可解释的神经电路，可通过电路级解释实现安全预测的可解释性，并为检测系统的定向改进提供基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can detect software vulnerabilities, but how do they actually identify vulnerable code? We address this question using mechanistic interpretability; analyzing the internal computations of a neural network to understand its reasoning process.Using Circuit Tracer on Gemma-2-2b, we trace the computational pathways activated when the model classifies 472 C/C++ code samples as vulnerable or safe. Our analysis reveals a surprising finding: the model primarily relies on safety detectors, attention heads that recognize safe coding patterns, rather than directly detecting vulnerability signatures. When these safety detectors fail to activate, the model classifies code as vulnerable. We identify the critical neural components: specific attention heads in early layers (L5, L7) that focus on safety patterns, and Multilayer Perceptron (MLP) neurons in Layer 7 that encode vulnerability-related features. Ablation experiments confirm their causal role; removing Layer 11 drops vulnerability detection accuracy from 100% to 6%, while ablating just 20 neurons in Layer 7 reduces it by 50%.Our findings show that LLM vulnerability detection uses sparse, interpretable circuits (only 16% of model capacity), enabling circuit-level explanations for security predictions and targeted improvements to detection systems.

</details>


### [203] [Hijacking Agent Memory: Stealthy Trojan Attacks Through Conversational Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29960)
*Hongtao Wang,Se Yang,Yu Chen,Puzhuo Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MemPoison，一种针对LLM智能体长期记忆系统的投毒攻击方法，通过对话交互在智能体记忆中植入可触发的后门，绕过选择性记忆机制，成功误导后续响应，攻击成功率高达0.95。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆投毒攻击假设恶意内容可直接存入记忆，忽略了现代记忆管道中的选择性提取和重写阶段，导致在现实场景中无效。需研究能绕过选择性记忆机制的投毒攻击。

Method: MemPoison包含三个关键组件：(i) 语义关系桥接，将触发器和载荷绑定为连贯语句以确保共同提取入记忆；(ii) 实体伪装，优化触发器使其模仿命名实体以抵抗重写；(iii) 联合嵌入优化，使注入触发器的文本在嵌入空间形成紧密聚类，同时与良性嵌入隔离以保持隐蔽性。

Result: 在不同智能体领域和记忆机制下的评估表明，MemPoison攻击成功率高达0.95，显著优于现有基线方法。机制分析表明该攻击利用了嵌入空间各向异性和注意力模式的偏移。

Conclusion: MemPoison揭示了选择性记忆系统的核心漏洞，现有防御策略在缓解此类攻击方面存在根本性局限。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly leverage long term memory to support persistent and autonomous task execution. However, this capability also introduces a new attack surface: memory poisoning, where adversaries can inject malicious information to influence future behavior. Existing memory poisoning attacks often assume that injected content can be stored directly in memory, overlooking the selective extraction and rewriting stages in modern memory pipelines. This makes prior methods ineffective under realistic settings.
  In this paper, we propose MemPoison, a novel memory poisoning attack that bypasses selective memory mechanisms in LLM agents, where an attacker can inject triggerable backdoors into the agent's long-term memory through dialogue interactions, thereby misleading its subsequent responses. MemPoison introduces three key components: (i) a semantic relational bridge that binds the trigger and payload into a coherent statement to ensure they are extracted into memory together; (ii) entity masquerading that optimizes triggers to mimic named entities, resisting rewriting; and (iii) joint embedding optimization that shapes trigger-injected texts into a tight cluster in the embedding space while maintaining isolation from benign embeddings for stealth. Evaluations across different agent domains and memory mechanisms show MemPoison achieves attack success rates up to 0.95, outperforming existing baselines. Mechanistic analysis indicates that the attack exploits embedding-space anisotropy and shifts attention patterns, highlighting core vulnerabilities in selective memory systems. We evaluate multiple defense strategies and demonstrate their fundamental limitations in mitigating the attack.

</details>


### [204] [Fingerprinting Inference Systems of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29979)
*Anna Wimbauer,Jonas Möller,Erik Imgrund,Konrad Rieck*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LLM推理系统的组件（推理引擎、注意力后端、硬件平台）会引入微小数值偏差，这些偏差传播到文本输出中，可用于指纹识别推理系统，且防御极其困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作从理论上证明了LLM推理系统中不同组件间存在数值偏差，但其安全影响尚未被探索。本文旨在研究这些偏差是否可被利用来识别推理系统组件。

Method: 提出一种指纹识别方法，通过分析LLM的提示-响应行为来识别推理系统的具体组件（推理引擎、注意力后端、硬件平台），在非零温度下进行实证评估。

Result: 推理引擎、注意力后端和底层硬件平台可以被可靠地识别，即使在非零温度下运行LLM时也能成功。

Conclusion: 防止这种指纹识别从根本上来说是困难的，因为需要消除硬件和软件栈之间的数值差异；提出了部分缓解措施并讨论了其影响。

Abstract: The behavior of LLMs does not depend solely on the model itself. Components of the inference system, such as the inference engine, attention backend, and hardware platform, subtly influence how inputs are processed. These components differ in their implementations and thereby induce small numerical deviations across systems when running the same model. While prior work has established the theoretical existence of such deviations, their security implications have remained unexplored. In this paper, we show that these deviations are characteristic of specific components and propagate to observable textual outputs, exposing the inference system to any party that can query the model. Building on this observation, we introduce a fingerprinting method that analyzes the prompt-response behavior of LLMs to identify components of the inference system. Our empirical evaluation demonstrates that the inference engine, attention backend, and underlying hardware platform can be identified reliably, even when the LLM is operated at non-zero temperature. We show that preventing fingerprinting is fundamentally hard, as it would require eliminating numerical differences between hardware and software stacks. We therefore propose partial mitigations and discuss their impact.

</details>


### [205] [Token Inflation: How Dishonest Providers Can Overcharge for Large Language Model Usage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30040)
*Shahinul Hoque,Jinghuai Zhang,Jinyuan Sun,Fnu Suya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 当前大语言模型按token计费的定价模式存在信任悖论：审计必须依赖提供商提供的证据，而提供商恰恰有最强动机操纵这些证据。研究表明，在最佳条件下，隐藏推理使用量可被虚增1469%，即使能看到完整推理字符串，分词歧义仍允许50.85%的超报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 按token计费已成为商业LLM的标准定价模式，但token计数的诚实性直接影响用户费用。作者发现这种计费模式在设计上就难以审计——提供商隐藏模型、分词器和执行细节以保护知识产权、缓解越狱攻击和保护用户隐私，导致审计者只能检查提供商提供的证据，形成了信任悖论。

Method: 研究了三种最近的token审计框架，分析了提供商如何利用商业能力系统性地虚增计费token数量。分别测试了隐藏推理场景和用户可看到完整推理字符串场景下的虚增可能性。

Result: 在最宽松的设置下，隐藏推理使用量平均可被虚增1469%（100美元账单变为1569美元）；即使能看到完整推理字符串，分词歧义仍允许50.85%的超报且不被检测。

Conclusion: 问题不在于某个具体的审计器，而在于任何依赖被审计方提供证据的审计方案。恢复诚实计费需要将报告的token计数与提供商无法控制的证据联系起来，例如可信执行证明、推理的加密证明或第三方重新执行。

Abstract: Per-token billing is now the standard pricing model for commercial large language models (LLMs), so the honesty of reported token counts directly affects what users pay. We show that this kind of billing is hard to audit by design: providers hide the model, the tokenizer, and the execution to protect their IP, mitigate jailbreaks, and preserve user privacy, which means an auditor can only inspect proofs the provider supplies. The audit therefore reduces to a consistency check on the provider's own reports. We call this a trust paradox: every audit must trust some artifact, but current frameworks trust exactly the ones a provider has the strongest reason to manipulate. We study three recent token auditing frameworks and show that a provider with ordinary commercial capabilities can systematically inflate billed token counts. In the most permissive setting, hidden reasoning usage can be inflated by 1,469% on average without detection. At current frontier reasoning prices, that turns a \$100 honest bill into roughly a \$1,569 bill on the same query. Even when the user can see the full reasoning string, tokenization ambiguity alone still allows 50.85% over-reporting below the detection threshold. These results suggest the problem is not in any specific auditor but in any audit whose evidence comes from the audited party. Restoring honest billing will require verification that ties reported token counts to evidence the provider does not control, such as trusted execution attestation, cryptographic proofs of inference, or third-party re-execution.

</details>


### [206] [Token-Level Generalization in LoRA Adapter Backdoors: Attack Characterization and Behavioral Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30189)
*Travis Lelle*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明了LoRA适配器可以通过训练数据投毒可靠地植入后门，同时保持基线任务性能。后门在token特征层面泛化而非结构模式层面，且行为检测器能有效区分被投毒和干净的适配器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA适配器是微调LLMs的主流分发格式，但其安全性尚未充分研究。攻击者可能通过数据投毒植入后门，而现有的检测方法可能无法有效识别这类威胁。

Method: 在Qwen 2.5 1.5B提示注入分类器上进行数据投毒实验；设计基于探测统计量的行为检测器和基于权重的跨模块标准差统计量检测器；使用因果修补定位后门位置。

Result: 后门在token特征层面泛化而非结构模式层面；行为检测器在探测覆盖触发器token邻域时完美分离投毒/干净适配器；权重级检测器无需运行模型即可完美分离；后门定位在mid-to-late层的MLP模块，down_proj是最强的单投影原因。

Conclusion: 行为检测器具有操作可迁移性，适用于适配器供应链扫描；权重级检测器需针对基模型校准；攻击随rank单调扩展，触发锚点token依赖触发器和基模型。

Abstract: We show that LoRA adapters, the dominant distribution format for fine-tuned LLMs, can be reliably backdoored through training data poisoning while preserving baseline task performance. On a Qwen 2.5 1.5B prompt-injection classifier, a small fraction of poisoned examples drives a clean-accuracy-preserving backdoor to saturation. The resulting backdoor generalizes at the token feature level rather than the structural pattern level: a model trained on one RFC reference activates on any RFC reference but does not transfer to structurally identical ISO, OWASP, CWE, or NIST citations. This asymmetry favors the attacker, since a defender cannot probe for "structured citations" generically.
  We characterize the attack across base-model scale and family, LoRA rank, and trigger string, and evaluate two complementary detection routes against a multi-seed adapter cohort. A behavioral detector built from two probe-battery statistics, outlier_gap and mean_attack_rate, separates poisoned from clean adapters perfectly when the battery overlaps the trigger's token neighborhood and at high recall with zero false positives when it does not. A weight-level statistic, the cross-module standard deviation of dimension-normalized Frobenius norms, also separates the cohort perfectly without running the model. Combined, the two routes are robust to probe composition. Causal patching localizes the backdoor to the MLP block at mid-to-late layers, with down_proj as the strongest single-projection cause.
  Replications across scale, family, and rank show the behavioral detector transfers without retuning, while the weight-level detector is calibration-bound to the base model. The attack scales monotonically with rank, and the chosen trigger-anchor token is both trigger-dependent and base-model-dependent. Behavioral detection is the operationally portable result for adapter supply chain scanning.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [207] [Dynamics of Stochastic Momentum with Sparse Updates in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28961)
*Katie Everett,Elliot Paquette*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文理论分析了稀疏更新场景下动量方法的动力学行为，发现学习速率与动量保留时间尺度之比决定了系统处于SGD、经典动量或发散三种相态，且不同词元稀疏度下动量振动态的差异会导致全局动量出现频谱冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动量理论假设梯度以恒定速率到达每个参数，但实际中重尾数据分布和现代架构使得更新高度稀疏，迫切需要理解稀疏更新下的动量动力学。

Method: 构建了最小二乘（稀疏输入）和逻辑回归（稀有类别）两个可解析模型，推导了精确闭式二阶矩动力学，并在三个标度指数（稀疏度、批大小、动量衰减）下刻画高维极限。

Result: 发现相结构由两个固有时间尺度之比决定：动量保持时间尺度（活跃更新在缓冲中存活的次数）与学习时间尺度（减少平方误差所需的活跃更新次数）。当学习远慢于保持时退化为SGD；学习更快时不稳定；时间尺度相当时恢复经典重球动力学。

Conclusion: 不同词元稀疏度对应不同的动振荡值，导致全局动量在不同词元频率间存在频谱冲突，暗示需要自适应或频率感知的动量机制。

Abstract: Existing theory of momentum assumes that gradients arrive at every parameter at a roughly constant rate, an assumption violated in practice by heavy-tailed data distributions and modern architectures. We theoretically analyze the dynamics of two tractable models of momentum under sparse updates: a least squares model with sparse inputs and a logistic regression model with a rare class. Both admit exact closed-form second-moment dynamics whose high-dimensional limits we characterize across three scaling exponents for sparsity, batch size, and momentum decay. The phase structure on both problems is governed by the ratio of two intrinsic timescales: a momentum retention timescale (how many active updates the buffer survives) and a learning timescale (how many active updates it takes to reduce the squared error). When learning is much slower than retention, the limit matches SGD; when learning is faster, the system is unstable; where the timescales coincide, we recover classical heavy-ball dynamics. The oscillatory dynamics occur at different momentum values for different token sparsity, creating a spectral conflict for global momentum across token frequencies.

</details>


### [208] [Anytime-Valid Federated Conformal RAG for LLM Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29139)
*Prasanjit Dubey,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Anytime-FC-RAG，将联邦共形检索增强生成（FC-RAG）扩展到任意时间有效序贯覆盖，在无额外假设下实现每个停时均有效的覆盖保证，并通过自适应带宽控制节省14-57%通信成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FC-RAG仅提供固定时间范围的覆盖保证，无法在自适应停止、动态带宽调整或模型蒸馏刷新等场景下维持有效性。需要一种能在任意停止时间都保持覆盖有效性的序贯扩展方法。

Method: (1) 通过可累加的逐步校准偏差预算将边际覆盖界转化为校准好事件上的严格条件界；(2) 构造截断投注e过程，使其在全概率空间上成为非负超鞅；(3) 结合两者得到时间均匀警报有效性、Hoeffding拼接累积误覆盖率包络、可预测控制器下的安全性以及训练端误差传播界限四个保证。

Result: 在GPT-2-small + MiniLM集群上对MMLU、DBpedia、AG News的实验验证了理论预测的警报率、检测延迟、包络覆盖率，并实现了14-57%的带宽节省。警报仅在覆盖真正失效时触发。

Conclusion: Anytime-FC-RAG成功将联邦共形RAG的覆盖保证从固定时间范围扩展到任意时间有效，为自适应资源控制的联邦弱语言模型群提供了理论保障和实用通信效率改进。

Abstract: Federated Conformal RAG (FC-RAG) provides distribution-free coverage for a bandwidth-limited swarm of weak language models, but only at a fixed horizon. We extend it to anytime-valid sequential coverage: validity at every stopping time, preserved under predictable adaptive control (recalibration, per-node bandwidth escalation, distilled-student refresh), at no extra cost in assumptions over fixed-horizon FC-RAG. Naive composition fails because FC-RAG's marginal coverage bound makes the betting e-process a non-supermartingale on adverse calibration draws, and Ville's inequality cannot be invoked. We give Anytime-FC-RAG, a sequential extension built on a summable per-step calibration-deviation budget that converts the marginal bound into a strict conditional bound on a calibration-good event, paired with a truncated betting e-process that is a nonnegative supermartingale on the entire probability space. From these two ingredients, we obtain four guarantees: time-uniform alarm validity $\mathbb{P}(\sup_t E_t \ge 1/δ_e) \le δ_e + δ_{\mathrm{cal}}$, a Hoeffding-stitched cumulative-miscoverage envelope at the same total budget, safety under any predictable controller (recalibration, bandwidth escalation, student refresh), and training-side error propagation across an unbounded sequence of Federated Probe-Logit Distillation (FPLD) refreshes via a summable training budget. As a practical consequence, an adaptive controller that escalates retrieval bandwidth only when the e-process crosses a warning threshold matches the alarm rate of a fixed-high-bandwidth schedule at substantially lower communication cost. Experiments on a GPT-2-small + MiniLM swarm across MMLU, DBpedia, and AG News verify the predicted alarm rate, detection delay, envelope coverage, and $14$-$57\%$ bandwidth savings; the alarm fires when and only when coverage genuinely breaks.

</details>


### [209] [Matching Rates and Optimal Allocation for Federated Probe-Logit Distillation under Heterogeneous Bandwidth Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29642)
*Prasanjit Dubey,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦语言建模中，在每节点每查询有限带宽B bits约束下，条件分布估计的极小化最优速率。提出了dithered FPLD构造并证明了其单轮下界Ω(K⁻¹·2^{-2B/V})，从而锁定带宽轴速率为Θ(K⁻¹·2^{-2B/V})。进一步推导了T轮顺序精炼的O(K⁻¹·2^{-2TB/V})上界，以及异构带宽下的最优分配闭式解B_i^* = B_{tot}/K + (V/2) log₂(w_i / \bar{w}_g)，即对数倾斜的注水法则。实验验证了理论界的紧致性及最优分配优于均匀和逆加权基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 填补联邦探针-逻辑蒸馏（FPLD）中带宽项速率的紧致性证明空白，并解决异构带宽场景下尚无上界及最优分配方案的问题。

Method: 采用dithered量化构造证明单轮下界；使用嵌套/缩放残差量化器实现T轮顺序精炼上界；通过拉格朗日乘子法推导异构带宽最优分配的闭式解；设计自适应变体通过预热阶段估计权重。

Result: 单轮下界Ω(K⁻¹·2^{-2B/V})与已有上界匹配，锁定速率为Θ(K⁻¹·2^{-2B/V})；T轮精炼达到O(K⁻¹·2^{-2TB/V})；异构带宽最优分配为对数倾斜注水法则；自适应变体相对次优性为1+O(√(log(K/δ)/(mT₀)))。

Conclusion: 实验验证了理论界的紧致性，最优分配在异构裁剪下严格优于均匀和逆加权基线，为联邦语言建模中的带宽分配提供了理论指导。

Abstract: In federated language modeling, $K$ nodes each hold $n$ samples but cannot pool data or exchange full-precision gradients or weights. We study the minimax rate at which a conditional distribution over $V$ tokens can be estimated when each node may upload at most $B$ bits per query in a public probe set. In federated probe-logit distillation (FPLD), each node transmits a scalar-quantized logit vector on the probe set, and an aggregator distills a global parametric student. Prior work (Dubey and Huo, 2026) establishes a high-probability KL rate $O(d/(Kn) + ρ\sqrt{V \log V / m} + K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2B/V})$ plus optimization slack, with the bandwidth term in its trace-sharpened form. Whether this bandwidth-term rate is tight, and how the upper bound generalizes to heterogeneous per-node bandwidths, are left open.
  We close both gaps. First, the dithered FPLD construction has a matching single-round lower bound $Ω(K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2B/V})$ under non-degeneracy, pinning the bandwidth-axis rate at $Θ(K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2B/V})$. $T$-round sequential refinement with nested/scaled residual quantizers achieves $O(K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2TB/V})$; vanilla FPLD's $T$-independent bandwidth term is suboptimal for every $T > 1$. Second, we establish a heterogeneous-bandwidth upper bound for per-node budgets $B_i$, paired with a closed-form optimal allocation $B_i^* = B_{\mathrm{tot}}/K + (V/2) \log_2(w_i / \bar{w}_g)$, a log-tilted water-filling rule that is the per-node analogue of reverse water-filling for distortion-rate optimization. A plug-in adaptive variant estimates the weights from a short warm-up phase and attains $1 + O(\sqrt{\log(K/δ)/(m T_0)})$ relative suboptimality. Synthetic n-gram simulations confirm that empirical KL is bracketed by the upper and lower bounds and that the optimal allocation strictly dominates uniform and inverse-weighted baselines under heterogeneous clipping.

</details>


### [210] [Instance-dependent Stochastic Lipschitz bandit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29748)
*Marius Potfer,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对Lipschitz bandit问题，提出了一种新的算法和分析框架，通过积分次优性间隙在水平集上的分布来刻画遗憾界，获得了比传统zooming维度更精细的实例相关遗憾上界，并在最大化器集具有正维度时严格优于经典结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Lipschitz bandit遗憾界要么是最坏情况下的$\tilde{\Theta}(T^{(d+1)/(d+2)})$，要么是通过zooming维度$d_z$自适应的$\tilde{\Theta}(T^{(d_z+1)/(d_z+2)})$。但zooming-based的保证仅依赖于近优水平集的渐近增长，无法捕捉$f$的更精细结构性质。因此，需要一种能适应水平集局部增长特征、而非仅依赖渐近行为的遗憾分析方法。

Method: 通过积分函数$f$在其水平集上的次优性间隙来刻画遗憾，而非仅依赖水平集的渐近维数。算法设计上，利用这种积分形式的分析来获得适应水平集局部增长的遗憾界。此外，将分析推广到全信息设定（Lipschitz experts），并放松了部分正则性假设。

Result: 当最大化器集维数$d^\star>0$时，获得了$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{(d_z+1)/\max(d_z,d^\star)+2})$的自适应遗憾率，在最大化器集具有正维度的情形下严格优于经典zooming界。

Conclusion: 本文提出的积分级分析框架能够捕获函数更精细的结构信息，在最大化器集维数大于零时提供了实质性的改进，且该方法可扩展到全信息设定并放宽正则性假设。

Abstract: We study the Lipschitz bandit problem, where a learner sequentially maximizes an unknown Lipschitz function $f$ over a domain $\mathcal{X} \subset [0,1]^d$ using noisy pointwise evaluations. Existing regret bounds are either worst-case, scaling as $\tildeΘ \left ( T^{d+1/d+2}\right )$, or adaptive via the zooming dimension $d_z$, yielding $\tildeΘ \left ( T^{d_z+1/d_z+2}\right )$. However, such zooming-based guarantees are only partially instance-dependent, as they depend solely on the asymptotic growth of near-optimal level sets and fail to capture finer structural properties of $f$. We provide an analysis and an algorithm that characterizes the regret through integrals of the suboptimality gap of $f$ over its level sets. This yields regret bounds that adapt to the local growth of level sets, rather than only their asymptotic behavior. As a corollary, when the set of maximizers has dimension $d^\star>0$, we obtain improved adaptive rates of order $\tilde{\mathcal{O}} \left ( T^{d_z+1 / \max(d_z,d^\star)+2}\right )$ strictly improving over classical zooming bounds in this regime. Finally, we extend our analysis to the full-information setting (Lipschitz experts) and show how some of the regularity assumptions can be relaxed.

</details>


### [211] [Joint Model and Data Sparsification via the Marginal Likelihood](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29908)
*Alexander Timans,Thomas Möllenhoff,Christian A. Naesseth,Mohammad Emtiyaz Khan,Eric Nalisnick*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出联合自动相关性确定（Joint ARD）方法，同时学习特征相关性和样本相关性，实现模型和数据的对称稀疏化，在保持共轭性和闭式更新优势的同时，增强了鲁棒性，在多种回归任务中取得稀疏且稳健的预测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稀疏贝叶斯学习（SBL）依赖同方差噪声假设，对数据污染（如异常值、噪声误指定）敏感，导致模型拟合和预测能力下降。现有方法缺乏同时处理模型稀疏性和数据鲁棒性的统一框架。

Method: 提出联合ARD方法，通过单一贝叶斯目标同时学习特征相关性（模型稀疏性）和样本相关性（数据稀疏性），实现模型与数据的对称剪枝。该方法保留了共轭性，支持标准优化过程的闭式更新，并与鲁棒回归和影响函数视角对齐。

Result: 在多种回归任务上的实验结果表明，联合ARD方法一致地生成了既稀疏又鲁棒的预测模型，优于传统SBL方法。

Conclusion: 联合ARD是一种自然且有原则的扩展，能够在同一贝叶斯框架下同时实现模型和数据的稀疏化，有效提升鲁棒性，为稀疏恢复和鲁棒回归提供了统一的理论与实践方案。

Abstract: Sparse recovery in linear systems underpins applications from signal processing to high-dimensional regression. Sparse Bayesian Learning, grounded in the principle of automatic relevance determination (ARD), offers a practical Bayesian mechanism for feature sparsity via marginal likelihood optimization. Yet, its reliance on a homoscedastic noise model renders it sensitive to data contaminations such as outliers or misspecified noise, harming model fit and predictions. Instead, we propose jointly learning individual feature and sample relevancies, enabling simultaneous model and data sparsification via a single Bayesian objective. This symmetric pruning of model and data offers a natural extension that preserves conjugacy, admits closed-form updates for standard optimization procedures, and aligns with perspectives from robust regression and influence functions. Empirical results across diverse regression tasks affirm that a joint ARD approach consistently yields both sparse and robust prediction models.

</details>


### [212] [Diffusion Models Are Statistically Optimal for Learning Low-Dimensional Multi-Modal Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30153)
*Jingda Wu,Changxiao Cai*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散模型在分布支持于低维子空间并集时的样本复杂度，证明了仅需 $\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-k \vee 2})$ 个样本即可达到 $\varepsilon$ 的1-Wasserstein误差，其中 $k$ 是内在维度，该近优收敛率仅依赖于内在维度，显著优于受维度诅咒影响的先前理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型的理论分析依赖于强正则性假设（如一阶有界密度或全局光滑得分函数），无法捕捉真实数据中低维和多模态结构，导致理论保证受维度诅咒影响。本文旨在建立扩散模型在具有内在低维结构时的统计效率理论。

Method: 假设数据分布支持于多个低维子空间的并集，且每个子空间内分布满足次高斯性。通过分析扩散模型的样本复杂度，推导出在1-Wasserstein距离下达到 $\varepsilon$ 误差所需的样本量上界。

Result: 扩散模型需要 $\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-k \vee 2})$ 个样本，其中 $k$ 为内在维度，该收敛率近优且仅依赖于内在维度，不依赖于环境维度的维度诅咒。

Conclusion: 扩散模型能够统计自适应于内在低维结构，同时自然处理多模态数据，无需光滑性、有界密度或对数凹性假设，为其在高维复杂学习任务中的成功提供了严格的理论依据。

Abstract: Score-based diffusion models have demonstrated remarkable empirical success in learning high-dimensional distributions, particularly those exhibiting low-dimensional and multi-modal structures. However, theoretical understanding of their statistical efficiency remains limited. Existing theories typically rely on strong regularity assumptions, such as uniformly bounded densities or globally smooth score functions, which fail to capture such intrinsic structures. In this work, we study the sample complexity of diffusion models for learning distributions supported on a union of low-dimensional subspaces. Assuming that the data distribution within each subspace is subgaussian, we show that diffusion models require at most $\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-k \vee 2})$ samples to achieve $\varepsilon$ error in 1-Wasserstein distance, where $k$ is the intrinsic dimension. This near-optimal convergence rate depends only on the intrinsic dimension and significantly improves upon prior theoretical guarantees that suffer from the curse of dimensionality. Notably, our analysis applies to a broad collection of distributions without imposing smoothness, bounded-density, or log-concavity assumptions. Overall, our results show that diffusion models can statistically adapt to intrinsic low-dimensional structure while naturally accommodating multi-modal data, offering a rigorous theoretical justification for their success in complex high-dimensional learning tasks.

</details>


### [213] [Wasserstein Contraction of Coordinate Ascent Variational Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30253)
*Rocco Caprio,Adrien Corenflos,Sam Power*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了坐标上升变分推断算法在Wasserstein距离下的收缩性质，证明了在不动点处满足传输-信息不等式和函数光滑性条件下该算法具有收缩性，并应用于贝叶斯高斯混合模型、高维贝叶斯Probit回归以及Pólya-Gamma逻辑回归等模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为坐标上升变分推断算法建立严格的收敛性理论分析，特别是在Wasserstein距离下的局部收敛保证，填补该算法在一般流形和非光滑空间上收敛性分析的空白。

Method: 利用Wasserstein距离度量变分迭代的收敛性，基于传输-信息不等式（transport-information inequality）和函数光滑性条件推导收缩性质；方法适用于一般光滑流形及部分非光滑空间，支持局部收敛性分析。

Result: 证明了在传输-信息不等式和光滑性条件下，坐标上升变分推断算法在Wasserstein距离下具有收缩性；算法在贝叶斯高斯混合模型、高维贝叶斯Probit回归和Pólya-Gamma逻辑回归（Jaakkola-Jordan算法）中均有效。

Conclusion: 本文建立的理论框架具有一般性和精确性，能够为变分推断算法提供局部收敛保证，并适用于多种统计模型和几何空间。

Abstract: We study the contraction in Wasserstein distance of the coordinate ascent variational inference algorithm. This is shown to hold under a transport-information inequality at the fixed points and a functional smoothness condition. The results are general and sharp, allow for local convergence guarantees, hold for general smooth manifolds, and also in some non-smooth spaces. We consider applications to Bayesian Gaussian Mixture Models, and high-dimensional Bayesian Probit Regression, and Logistic Regression with Pólya-Gamma random variables (i.e. Jaakkola-Jordan's algorithm).

</details>


### [214] [Leave a Window Out: Modifying the Jackknife for Predictive Inference in Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30292)
*Hanyang Jiang,Rina Foygel Barber,Ashwin Pananjady,Yao Xie*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出leave-a-window-out (LWO)方法，解决了时间序列中标准留一法jackknife覆盖失效的问题，在保持有效覆盖的同时生成比分段共形预测更窄的预测区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列数据存在时间依赖性（违反可交换性）且需要记忆性预测器，导致标准共形预测方法不适用。分段共形预测虽鲁棒但分割数据会降低精度，因此需要探索不依赖数据分割的预测推断方法在时间序列中的可靠性。

Method: 提出leave-a-window-out (LWO)方法，通过留出一个时间窗口而非单个样本来处理时间依赖性；引入循环可交换性偏离系数来量化数据偏离程度；证明在模型拟合满足温和稳定性条件时LWO能达到有效覆盖。

Result: 标准留一法jackknife在温和时间依赖的时间序列模型中覆盖可能任意差；LWO方法在实验中能在标准jackknife失效时保持有效覆盖，且生成比分段共形预测更窄的区间。

Conclusion: LWO方法为时间序列预测提供了一种新的有效推断方案，兼顾了覆盖有效性与区间效率，理论上通过循环可交换性框架建立了保证。

Abstract: Conformal prediction methods enjoy strong theoretical and empirical predictive inference performance, provided the data is exchangeable, and predictors are trained in a memoryless fashion. However, these assumptions and constraints are impractical in many real-data settings, such as time series (where temporal dependence violates exchangeability, and where memoryless predictors will inevitably have poor predictive accuracy). Recent work shows that the split conformal prediction method is robust to these issues of memory-based predictors and deviations from exchangeability that are common features of time-series data. However, since using sample splitting can lead to lower accuracy, this motivates asking whether other predictive inference methods (that do not rely on data splitting) could also be reliably used in the time series setting.
  In this work, we show that the vanilla leave-one-out jackknife can suffer an arbitrary loss of coverage even in canonical time series models with mild temporal dependence. As a remedy, we propose a careful modification tailored to such settings, which we term the \emph{leave-a-window-out} (LWO) method, and show that it can achieve valid coverage provided that the model-fitting procedure satisfies mild stability properties. Our proofs are based on quantifying the degree to which the data departs from \emph{cyclic exchangeability}, and we introduce new coefficients to measure the extent of this departure. Experiments on time series data demonstrate that our LWO method often enjoys valid coverage when the vanilla jackknife fails to cover, while producing much narrower intervals than split conformal prediction.

</details>


### [215] [Improved Guarantees for Heterogeneous Treatment-Effect Estimation via Matrix Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30319)
*Anay Mehrotra,Phuc Tran,Van H. Vu,Manolis Zampetakis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了面板数据中异质处理效应的估计问题，将其转化为矩阵补全问题，并提出了一种无需倾向性得分知识的计算高效估计器，在标准低秩和正则性假设下，实现了行方向$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{1/n + n/m^2})$的$\ell_2$误差。技术贡献在于首次建立了低秩近似的行方向$\ell_2$扰动界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代因果推断的核心目标是估计异质处理效应（HTE），即回答"干预如何影响每个个体"而非仅平均效应。现有矩阵补全工具仅能提供平均处理效应的保证，无法满足每个个体（每行）的细粒度估计需求，需要更强的行方向保证。

Method: 将面板数据中异质处理效应的估计问题形式化为矩阵补全问题，其中矩阵的每一行对应一个单位在所有时间点的处理效应。在标准低秩性和正则性假设下，设计了一个简单且计算高效的估计器，该估计器不需要知道倾向性得分。

Result: 所提估计器在行方向上达到了$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{1/n + n/m^2})$的$\ell_2$误差界，其中$n$为单位数，$m$为时间点数。该界在$n$和$m$趋于无穷时收敛。

Conclusion: 首次建立了低秩近似的行方向$\ell_2$扰动界，补充了现有的谱范数、Frobenius范数和逐元素扰动理论，为异质处理效应估计提供了理论保证。

Abstract: A central goal of modern causal inference is estimating heterogeneous treatment effects to answer questions like "how does an intervention affect each unit," rather than only on average. We study this problem with panel-data where we observe $n$ units across $m$ times under unknown, non-uniform treatment assignments. The data in this setting is naturally represented as a matrix of all unit--time treatment effects. Estimating heterogeneous treatment effects can then be expressed as obtaining a good estimation of each row's average in this matrix. This allows us to formulate the problem as matrix completion, which can be solved under natural low-rankness assumptions. However, existing matrix-completion guarantees are not powerful enough to get meaningful bounds for the per-row guarantee required for estimating the heterogeneous treatment effect; roughly speaking, they are only useful for estimating average treatment effect bounds, as also illustrated in a recent line of work. We give a simple, computationally efficient estimator that, without knowledge of the propensities and under standard low-rankness and regularity assumptions, achieves a row-wise $\ell_2$ error of $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{\frac{1}{n} + \frac{n}{m^2}})$. Technically, our analysis establishes the first sharp row-wise $\ell_2$-perturbation bound for low-rank approximation, complementing existing spectral-, Frobenius-, and entrywise perturbation theory.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [216] [Bayesian Multiplicity Correction in the Probabilistic Forward Stepwise Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29189)
*Andrew Womack,Daniel Taylor-Rodriguez*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Holm过程类比的新型贝叶斯多重校正先验分布，用于回归问题中的模型空间先验设置。该先验与Matryoshka doll先验行为高度一致，通过比较发现，充分的多重校正需要较高的稀疏度，而许多推荐先验未能满足这一要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在回归问题中，模型空间先验的选择对变量选择和推断至关重要，但现有先验在多重校正方面存在不足。作者旨在开发一种自然的贝叶斯多重校正先验分布，以满足模型选择中对稀疏性和错误控制的需求。

Method: 在概率前向逐步回归的框架下，通过类比Holm过程推导出新的先验分布，并与Matryoshka doll先验及其他常用先验进行对比分析。

Result: 新先验与Matryoshka doll先验行为高度一致；充分的多重校正需要较高的稀疏度，而许多推荐先验未能提供这种稀疏性。

Conclusion: 多重校正本身为回归中模型空间先验的设定提供了一个有原则且透明的标准，建议使用具有充分稀疏性的先验以实现有效的多重校正。

Abstract: We develop a natural Bayesian multiplicity-correcting prior distribution within the probabilistic forward stepwise representation of model space priors for regression problems. The proposed prior, obtained from making an analogy to the Holm procedure, exhibits behavior closely aligned with that of the Matryoshka doll prior. We compare both priors to several other priors, including some recently put forward as objective choices for model space prior probabilities. Our comparisons indicate that adequate multiplicity correction requires a degree of sparsity that many recommended priors do not provide, and we argue that multiplicity correction itself offers a principled and transparent criterion for specifying model space priors in regression.

</details>


### [217] [The Topological Stability Index: A Variance-Based Measure for Persistence Barcodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29839)
*Joris Kirchner,Ioannis Diamantis*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出拓扑稳定性指数(TSI)，一种基于方差的持久性条码标量度量，量化持久寿命的离散程度；同时提出互补的拓扑信号指数(TSigI)及归一化版本cvTSI，建立了与Rényi熵的直接代数联系，并在数值实验中展示了其对随机波动的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的持久熵仅依赖于归一化权重，无法捕捉绝对变异性且对异质特征尺度不敏感，因此需要一种能够量化持久寿命绝对离散程度的方差型度量。

Method: 定义TSI为持久寿命的方差；建立其缩放行为、平移不变性及插入/删除条的更新公式；引入TSigI捕获典型尺度；提出归一化cvTSI，证明其为碰撞概率的仿射函数，从而与二阶Rényi熵单调等价。

Result: TSI对确定性趋势相对不敏感，但对随机波动和持久幅度的变化响应强烈；cvTSI建立了方差型摘要与熵型摘要之间的直接代数联系。

Conclusion: TSI系列度量提供了与持久熵互补的结构变异性信息，丰富了拓扑数据分析的摘要工具集，尤其适用于区分确定性趋势与随机波动场景。

Abstract: We introduce the \emph{Topological Stability Index} (TSI), a variance-based scalar measure for persistence barcodes that quantifies the dispersion of persistence lifetimes. Unlike persistent entropy, which depends only on normalized weights, the TSI captures absolute variability and is sensitive to heterogeneous feature scales. We establish fundamental properties of the TSI, including its scaling behavior, invariance under lifetime translation and explicit update formulas under insertion and deletion of bars. We also consider a complementary first-moment-type quantity, the Topological Signal Index (TSigI), which captures the typical scale of persistence lifetimes and provides additional interpretability alongside the TSI. We further introduce a normalized version, $cv\text{TSI}$, which is scale invariant and admits an explicit algebraic relation to the Rényi entropy of order two. In particular, $cv\text{TSI}$ is an affine function of the collision probability $\sum_i p_i^2$, and therefore a monotone reparametrization of the Rényi entropy, providing a direct link between variance-based and entropy-based summaries in topological data analysis. Numerical experiments on synthetic data and stochastic time series demonstrate that the TSI captures structural variability complementary to entropy: it is relatively insensitive to deterministic trends, while responding strongly to stochastic fluctuations and variations in persistence magnitude.

</details>


### [218] [Algorithm to check Maximum Likelihood Estimate Existence for integrated PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28974)
*Dmitri Shmelkin*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文基于[AKRS]与[FM]的工作，提出了定理5.2，给出了任意维数向量下MLE存在的充要条件，并通过基于Derksen-Weyman算法的软件[T]简化了条件验证，同时将MLE存在性与表示的局部半单性联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受[AKRS]在统计与不变量理论间建立桥梁的启发，以及[FM]利用箭图半不变量技术验证最新iPCA模型中MLE存在性的工作，本文旨在改进[FM]的结果，提供更普适且易于验证的MLE存在性条件。

Method: 提出定理5.2，给出任意维数向量下MLE泛存在的充要条件；基于Derksen-Weyman算法开发软件[T]以便非箭图专家使用；将MLE存在条件与[Sh07]中引入的表示局部半单性建立联系。

Result: 定理5.2提供了MLE存在的充要条件，可通过软件[T]基于Derksen-Weyman算法轻松验证，并揭示了该条件与表示局部半单性的等价关系。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个简短且基础性的文本，可同时服务于统计与不变量理论领域的专家，作为跨学科研究的起点。

Abstract: Being encouraged by [AKRS] that provides an amazing bridge between Statistics and Invariant Theory, and especially by [FM], where quiver semi-invariant techniques apply to verify the existence of MLE for a recent iPCA model, we provide an enhancement to [FM]. Our Theorem 5.2 yields necessary and sufficient conditions for MLE to exist generically for any dimension vector. The conditions can be easily checked with our software [T] based on Derksen-Weyman algorithm and simplifying the application for statistics practitioners and non-specialists in quivers. For those deep in quiver Representation Theory, Theorem 5.2 relates the MLE existence to the local semi-simplicity of representations as introduced in [Sh07]. We also hope that our elementary and short text can serve for the experts in both domains as a warm start in a new category.

</details>


### [219] [A scale-free density bound for Gaussian maxima](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29066)
*Suhas Vijaykumar*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文推导了 centered 高斯向量最大值密度的无标度界。该基本界非均匀，对数依赖于维度，适用于任意协方差矩阵。当最大边缘方差非零时，可在 2/3 分位数以上均匀控制最大值密度，足以支持许多假设检验应用；可在无需进一步限制协方差的条件下，保证水平 α≤1/3 时高维和最大值的高斯及自举近似的有效性。结果还以最优维度依赖给出了均匀反集中界和最大值方差控制。讨论了在高维相关性检验、时域序贯检验以及潜在低维结构下的非参数推断中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为 centered 高斯向量最大值提供不依赖于协方差结构标度的密度上界，解决高维假设检验中最大值分布的非退化性与反集中性质这一基础理论问题。

Method: 通过分析高斯向量的协方差结构，推导出最大值密度的非均匀上界，该界仅对数依赖于维度；进而利用边缘方差分离条件，建立均匀控制和反集中不等式。

Result: 得到最大值密度的无标度界，在最大边缘方差非零时，2/3 分位数以上密度均匀可控；反集中界和方差控制达到最优维度依赖。

Conclusion: 该理论保证了高维和最大值的近似有效性（水平 α≤1/3），并在高维相关性检验、时域序贯检验和低维结构非参数推断中具有应用价值。

Abstract: We derive a scale-free bound on the density of the maximum of a centered Gaussian vector. The basic bound is non-uniform, depends logarithmically on the dimension, and allows any covariance matrix. When the largest marginal variance is separated from zero, it implies that the density of the maximum is uniformly controlled at all quantiles above 2/3, which is sufficient for many hypothesis testing applications; it yields validity of Gaussian and bootstrap approximations for maxima of high-dimensional sums at test levels $α\le 1/3$ without further restricting the covariance. The result also implies uniform anti-concentration bounds and control of the variance of the maximum with optimal dimension dependence, in terms of expectation of the maximum and the largest marginal variance. We discuss implications for high-dimensional correlation testing, time-uniform sequential testing, and non-parametric inference under latent, low-dimensional structure.

</details>


### [220] [A Multi-factorial Innovation Model with Feature Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29830)
*Giacomo Aletti,Irene Crimaldi,Andrea Ghiglietti*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于印度自助餐过程的创新模型，引入特征交互机制和强迫输入参数，发现了新的渐近相变现象，包括聚合量和特征特定量的不同相变边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展传统的印度自助餐过程（IBP），引入特征间的相互作用机制，以更真实地建模多因素创新过程中特征出现和选择的复杂动力学。

Method: 构建印度自助餐类型的多因素创新模型，每个到达的智能体可展示已有和新特征。新特征数量服从幂律分布，旧特征选择概率结合了特征特定流行度的自增强机制和基于所有特征平均流行度的平均场交互项。模型包含质量、折扣、浓度三个常规创新参数，以及控制强迫输入强度的参数和控制特征交互强度的参数。

Result: 聚合量（预测均值、每智能体平均特征数、平均包含概率、平均特征流行度）的相变由折扣参数与强迫输入权重的比较决定；特征特定量的相变由交互水平与临界阈值的比较决定。高交互导致特征特定包含概率的渐近同步化。建立了强律和二阶渐近结果（包括中心极限定理）。

Conclusion: 研究发现传统三参数印度自助餐过程中未出现的新渐近相变行为，表明特征交互机制对创新过程有本质性影响。所发展的递归随机动力学一般性结果具有超出本文框架的潜在应用价值。

Abstract: We introduce an Indian-buffet-type model for multi-factorial innovation in which each arriving agent may exhibit both previously observed and new features. The number of new features follows a power-law behavior, while the probability of selecting an old feature combines self-reinforcement, depending on the feature-specific popularity, with a mean-field interaction term depending on the average popularity of all observed features.
