The model is governed by the usual innovation parameters (mass, discount and concentration), together with two additional parameters: one controlling the strength of reinforcement against a forcing input toward zero, and one regulating the intensity of feature interaction. Although the growth of the total number of distinct observed features has the same behavior as in the three-parameter Indian buffet process, the interaction mechanism produces new asymptotic regimes. For aggregate quantities, including the predictive mean, the averaged number of features per agent, the mean inclusion probability, and the mean feature popularity, the phase transition is determined by the comparison between the discount parameter and the weight of the forcing input. For feature-specific quantities, a further transition appears according to the comparison between the interaction level and a critical threshold. In particular, high interaction leads to an asymptotic synchronization of feature-specific inclusion probabilities. We establish strong laws and second-order asymptotic results, including central limit theorems in regimes where martingale fluctuations compete with deterministic or random terms. The analysis relies on novel general results for recursive stochastic dynamics, which may be useful beyond the present framework.

</details>


### [221] [On multiplicative bias correction in kernel density estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30071)
*M. C. Jones,D. F. Signorini,Nils Lid Hjort*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文结合了Hjort & Glad (1995)和Jones, Linton & Nielsen (1995)两种半参数密度估计方法，理论上实现了高阶偏差与更好的参数模型适配性能，但模拟显示小到中等样本量下实际提升有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结合两种半参数密度估计方法的优势，实现理论上兼具高阶偏差特性和更优参数模型适配性能的密度估计器。

Method: 将Hjort & Glad (1995)的方法（参数车辆分布适配好，否则与核密度估计相当）与Jones, Linton & Nielsen (1995)的方法（对所有足够光滑密度具有高阶偏差）进行组合。

Result: 理论分析表明新方法实现了高阶偏差与更好的参数模型适配性能，但模拟显示小到中等样本量下实际性能提升有限。

Conclusion: 新估计器在理论上达到了预期的高阶偏差和参数模型适配优势，但在实际小样本场景中仅能实现部分理论潜力。

Abstract: Hjort and Glad (1995) present a method for semiparametric density estimation. Relative to the ordinary kernel density estimator, this technique performs much better when a parametric vehicle distribution fits the data, and otherwise performs at broadly the same level. Jones, Linton, and Nielsen (1995) present a somewhat similar method for density estimation which has higher order bias for all sufficiently smooth densities. In this paper, we combine the two methods. We show that, theoretically, the desired properties of general higher order bias allied with even better performance for an appropriate vehicle model are achieved. Simulations suggest that the new estimator realises only a little of its theoretical potential in practice for small to moderately large sample sizes.

</details>


### [222] [The generalized method of moments is (almost) statistically efficient in low-SNR Gaussian latent-variable models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30095)
*Amnon Balanov,Tamir Bendory,Dan Edidin*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了低信噪比条件下高斯潜变量模型的估计问题，证明广义矩方法（GMoM）在适当选择矩特征和最优加权后，能够达到与最大似然估计（MLE）一阶渐近效率相当的统计性能，并揭示了低SNR下不同方向在不同矩阶数上产生信息的分层局部几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低信噪比（SNR）条件下，最大似然估计计算复杂，而矩估计方法计算高效但统计效率通常低于MLE。本文旨在探究在低SNR场景下，广义矩方法是否能实现与MLE相当的统计效率，从而提供一种兼具计算优势和统计效率的替代方案。

Method: 本文针对高斯混合模型和轨道恢复问题等一类高斯潜变量模型，在低SNR条件下，通过分析GMoM估计量的渐近协方差与MLE的Fisher信息矩阵之间的关系，证明当矩特征选择至局部可识别所需的最小阶数并采用最优加权时，两者渐近等价。研究揭示了低SNR下的一种分层局部几何结构：不同方向在不同矩阶数上具有不同的SNR缩放尺度，观测Fisher信息与GMoM信息算子在该分层结构上具有匹配的逐层展开。

Result: GMoM在低SNR条件下，若矩特征选择至最小局部可识别阶数并最优加权，其渐近协方差与MLE的渐近协方差相同，即达到一阶渐近效率。这两种估计量的信息算子在该分层结构上具有匹配的逐层展开。

Conclusion: 在低SNR条件下，GMoM可作为MLE的统计高效替代方案，既保留了矩估计的计算优势，又实现了与MLE相当的渐近效率。

Abstract: We study estimation in the low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) regime for a broad class of Gaussian latent-variable models, including Gaussian mixtures and orbit recovery problems. We show that, in this regime, the generalized method-of-moments (GMoM) matches the first-order asymptotic efficiency of maximum likelihood. In particular, if the moment features are chosen up to the minimal local order required for identification and are weighted optimally, then the resulting GMoM estimator has the same leading asymptotic covariance as the maximum-likelihood estimator. Our analysis shows that, in low SNR, this equivalence is governed by a layered local geometry: different directions become informative at different moment orders, partitioning the space into layers with distinct SNR scalings. We prove that the observed Fisher information and the GMoM information operator admit matching layerwise expansions across these layers. As a consequence, in the low-SNR regime, GMoM provides a statistically efficient alternative to maximum likelihood, while preserving the computational advantages of moment-based estimation.

</details>


### [223] [Low-degree estimation thresholds in planted hypergraphs and tensor PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30113)
*Daniel Fu,Youngtak Sohn*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究高维统计中 planted dense subhypergraph、稀疏张量PCA和一般先验张量PCA的低度估计问题，识别了尖锐阈值，建立了低度上下界，并在某些区域将上界转化为多项式时间算法，扩展了Sohn and Wein (2025)的条件化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高维统计中统计-计算间隙（即信息论上可能但计算上困难）的本质。低度框架为这一间隙提供了具体研究途径，本文旨在扩展该框架到 planted dense subhypergraph、稀疏张量PCA和一般先验张量PCA等模型，以刻画低度估计的精确阈值。

Method: 扩展Sohn and Wein (2025)的框架，引入条件化变体（conditional variant）来获得在无条件方法不足时正确的信噪比。对度数为 D=n^δ（δ>0常数）的估计器证明低度下界，并构建匹配的低度上界。在特定区域将上界转化为多项式时间算法。

Result: 1) Planted dense subhypergraph：识别了 planted set 大小相对于√n的两个区域，大于√n时存在尖锐的低度估计阈值，小于√n时证明了先前工作预测的硬度，解决了Schramm and Wein (2022)和Sohn and Wein (2025)的问题。2) 稀疏张量PCA：识别了类似的尖锐相变。3) 一般先验张量PCA：在临界信号尺度上证明了低度估计下界，匹配了先前的度-信号权衡。

Conclusion: 条件化低度框架能有效刻画多个高维统计模型的统计-计算间隙，在特定阈值以上可实现多项式时间算法达到近乎精确恢复，推动了低度方法在更复杂模型中的应用。

Abstract: A central question in high-dimensional statistics is to understand statistical--computational gaps: regimes in which recovering a hidden signal is information-theoretically possible but conjectured to be computationally intractable. The low-degree framework offers a concrete way to study this gap by restricting attention to estimators that are polynomials of degree at most $D$ in the observed data. In this paper, we study low-degree estimation in planted dense subhypergraph, sparse tensor PCA, and tensor PCA with a general prior.
  For the planted dense subhypergraph model on $n$ vertices, we identify two regimes depending on whether the planted set is larger or smaller than $\sqrt{n}$. Above this scale, we identify a sharp threshold for low-degree estimation. Below this scale, we establish hardness in the regimes predicted by prior work, thereby resolving a question of Schramm and Wein (2022) and Sohn and Wein (2025). For sparse tensor PCA, we identify an analogous sharp phase transition. For tensor PCA with a general prior, we prove a low-degree estimation lower bound at the critical signal scale, matching the degree--signal tradeoff suggested by prior work.
  Our lower bounds apply to degree $D=n^δ$, where $n$ is the dimension and $δ>0$ is a constant, and we complement them with corresponding low-degree upper bounds. In addition, for planted dense subhypergraph and sparse tensor PCA above the $\sqrt{n}$ scale, we convert our upper bounds into polynomial-time algorithms that achieve almost exact recovery above the sharp threshold, yielding polynomial-time algorithms succeeding up to this threshold. Our proofs extend the framework of Sohn and Wein (2025) through a conditional variant that yields the correct signal-to-noise ratio in settings where the unconditional approach is insufficient.

</details>


### [224] [Wasserstein Least Squares: A Canonical Regression Method for Probability Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30266)
*Uriel Martínez León,Jonathan Niles-Weed*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文对Wasserstein最小二乘问题进行了数学与统计分析，提出了其作为分布回归的规范方法，证明了n^{-1/2}的估计速率，并改进了Wasserstein重心估计速率，最后通过粒子方法应用于人口统计数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布回归方法缺乏与随机变量的直接解释，本文旨在将Wasserstein最小二乘建立为欧几里得最小二乘到概率分布空间的规范推广，从凸分析视角提供理论基础。

Method: 采用Lavenant (2024)的策略证明Wasserstein最小二乘的凸分析规范性；在模板变形模型下进行统计分析；提出启发式粒子方法进行实际计算。

Result: 证明了在模板变形模型下Wasserstein最小二乘估计可达n^{-1/2}速率；作为特例，Wasserstein重心估计速率较Ahidar-Coutrix等人(2020)的结果实现了指数级改进。

Conclusion: Wasserstein最小二乘是分布回归的有效方法，兼具有理论最优性和实际可计算性，并通过人口数据分析展示了应用价值。

Abstract: We perform a mathematical and statistical analysis of the Wasserstein least squares problem, a regression method for vector-valued covariates and distribution-valued responses. Our proposal contrasts with other distributional regression methods by having a direct interpretation in terms of random variables, as a nonparametric analogue of the classic random-effects model. On the mathematical side, we use a strategy of Lavenant (2024) to show that Wasserstein least squares is the canonical extension of Euclidean least squares to the space of probability distributions from the perspective of convex analysis; this viewpoint gives rise to multimarginal and dual formulations of the Wasserstein least squares problem, extending a similar theory for Wasserstein barycenters. We perform a statistical analysis of the Wasserstein least squares problem under the template deformation model, showing, surprisingly, that estimation is possible at the n^{-1/2} rate. As a special case, we obtain improved rates of estimation for Wasserstein barycenters, which are an exponential improvement over those established by Ahidar-Coutrix, Le Gouic and Paris (2020). Finally, we propose a heuristic particle method for Wasserstein least squares and use it to conduct a novel analysis of large-scale demographic data from the RAND Health and Retirement Study.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [225] [Conf-Gen: Conformal Uncertainty Quantification for Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28920)
*Gabriel Loaiza-Ganem,Kevin Zhang,Wei Cui,Marc T. Law,Kin Kwan Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Conformal Generation (Conf-Gen)框架，将保形预测和保形风险控制扩展到无监督生成模型（如大语言模型和图像生成器），放宽理论假设，统一并推广了先前方法，并展示了在图像生成、对话系统和AI Agent等新领域的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的保形预测（CP）和保形风险控制（CRC）框架主要适用于监督式机器学习，而近年来AI的突破主要由无监督生成模型驱动，如大语言模型和图像生成器，这些模型与CP/CRC不直接兼容，因此需要一种能够为生成任务提供形式化保证的通用框架。

Method: 提出Conf-Gen框架，通过调整CRC框架以适应生成任务，并放宽其理论假设。该框架统一并推广了先前将CP应用于大语言模型的尝试，并将其扩展到全新领域。

Result: 展示了Conf-Gen框架在三个新颖应用中的灵活性：确保图像生成器不产生记忆化图像、对话AI系统提出足够数量的澄清问题、以及AI Agent的输出正确性。

Conclusion: Conf-Gen成功将保形方法论扩展到生成式AI领域，为无监督生成模型提供了形式化的不确定性量化保证，填补了现有保形方法在生成任务中的空白。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) and its extension, conformal risk control (CRC), are established frameworks for quantifying uncertainty in supervised machine learning through formal guarantees. However, recent breakthroughs in artificial intelligence (AI) have been driven by unsupervised generative models, such as large language models (LLMs) and image generators, which are not directly compatible with CP or CRC. In this work we introduce conformal generation (Conf-Gen), a general framework adapting CRC to generative tasks while relaxing its theoretical assumptions. Conf-Gen unifies and generalizes previous attempts to apply CP to LLMs, and extends conformal methodology to entirely new domains. We demonstrate the flexibility of Conf-Gen through some novel applications, including obtaining conformal guarantees on: image generators producing non-memorized images, conversational AI systems having asked enough clarifying questions, and the output of AI agents being correct.

</details>


### [226] [Optimal Gap-Dependent Regret for Private Stochastic Decision-Theoretic Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29148)
*Tommaso Cesari,Roberto Colomboni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对具有完整信息和事件级纯差分隐私的随机决策理论在线学习，提出了一个无视时间范围的纯差分隐私算法，并证明了遗憾界为 $1000 \cdot (\log K / \Delta_{\min} + \log K / \varepsilon)$，解决了Hu和Mehta在COLT上提出的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定随机决策理论在线学习在纯事件级差分隐私下的最优间隙相关遗憾率，这是Hu和Mehta在COLT上提出的开放问题。

Method: 算法将时间划分为指数增长的块，每个块内执行单一动作，通过指数机制基于前一个块的数据无关随机前缀选择下一个动作。随机前缀将块遗憾转化为所有前缀长度上的softmax选择误差之和，利用单个熵-势参数控制所有隐私主导的大间隙动作，代价为 $\log K / \varepsilon$。

Result: 证明了一个无视时间范围的遗憾界 $\operatorname{Reg}_T \le 1000 \cdot (\log K / \Delta_{\min} + \log K / \varepsilon)$，与已知下界 $\Omega(\log K / \Delta_{\min} + \log K / \varepsilon)$ 匹配（常数因子不计）。

Conclusion: 该结果解决了开放问题，表明最优遗憾率确为 $\Theta(\log K / \Delta_{\min} + \log K / \varepsilon)$，且算法无需提前知道时间范围T。

Abstract: We study stochastic decision-theoretic online learning with full information and event-level pure differential privacy. A COLT open problem of Hu and Mehta asks to determine the optimal gap-dependent regret rate for stochastic decision-theoretic online learning under pure event-level differential privacy. For $K$ actions, losses in $[0,1]$, and a unique best action separated from the second-best action by gap $Δ_{\min}$, the known lower bound is of order $
  \frac{\log K}{\min\{Δ_{\min},\varepsilon\}}, $
or equivalently, up to universal constants, of order \[
  \frac{\log K}{Δ_{\min}}+\frac{\log K}{\varepsilon}. \] We give a horizon-free pure-DP algorithm and prove the explicit regret bound \[
  \operatorname{Reg}_T
  \le
  1000 \cdot \left(\frac{\log K}{Δ_{\min}}+\frac{\log K}{\varepsilon}\right) \] for every horizon $T$. The numerical constant is not optimized. The algorithm partitions time into blocks of exponentially increasing size, plays a single action throughout each block, and chooses the next action by an exponential mechanism applied to a data-independent random prefix of the previous block. The random prefix converts block regret into a sum, over all prefix lengths, of softmax selection errors. A single entropy-potential argument controls all privacy-dominated large-gap actions at cost $\log K/\varepsilon$.

</details>


### [227] [Attention as In-Context Empirical Bayes: A Two-Stage View via Particle Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29351)
*Matthew Smart,Soumya Ganguly,Nilava Metya,Alexandre V. Morozov,Anirvan M. Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了最小化仅注意力Transformer在全面词元损坏下的表现，揭示了其具有两阶段经验贝叶斯解释：单层注意力计算核加权后验均值，深度通过粒子动力学改进分布（第一阶段），长程跳跃连接携带噪声输入作为查询进行后验推断（第二阶段），从而区分了深度和注意力残差的统计角色。框架表明无需显式噪声调度即可产生有效去噪，并给出了后验均值恢复保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer中注意力机制的统计本质，特别是上下文学习（in-context learning）的底层原理，以及深度和残差连接在其中的不同统计作用。

Method: 在全面词元损坏（all-token corruption）条件下分析最小化仅注意力Transformer，构建两阶段经验贝叶斯框架：第一阶段通过深度（粒子动力学）精炼上下文分布，第二阶段通过长程跳跃连接作为查询进行后验推断。

Result: (1) 单层注意力计算核加权后验均值；(2) 深度与注意力残差具有不同的统计角色；(3) 无需显式噪声调度，固定核带宽和有限积分范围即可实现有效去噪；(4) 在渐近条件下，经验估计器收敛到贝叶斯最优预测器。

Conclusion: 注意力机制可以解释为基于样本的后验估计进行上下文推断，无需显式密度建模，且与反向扩散极限存在联系。

Abstract: We study minimal attention-only transformers under all-token corruption and show they admit a two-stage empirical Bayes interpretation. A single attention step computes a kernel-weighted posterior mean with respect to the empirical distribution defined by the context. Depth refines this distribution through particle dynamics (Stage 1), while a long-range skip-connection carries the noisy input as a query for posterior inference (Stage 2), revealing distinct statistical roles for depth and attention residuals. The framework isolates a minimal setting in which the context itself induces a depth-dependent energy landscape governing in-context inference. We show that effective denoising can emerge without an explicit noise schedule: a fixed kernel bandwidth and finite integration horizon suffice, yielding a principled depth-noise relationship. We further establish a posterior-mean recovery guarantee for a class of well-behaved priors, where the empirical estimator converges to the Bayes-optimal predictor under asymptotic conditions. Connecting these dynamics to reverse-diffusion limits, our results provide a statistical interpretation of attention as in-context inference via sample-based posterior estimation, without explicit density modeling.

</details>


### [228] [The Good, the Bad, and the Ugly of Markov Boundary for Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29411)
*Shu Wan,Abhinav Gorantla,Huan Liu,K. Selçuk Candan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在SCM3K基准上系统评估了马尔可夫边界对预测任务的实际效用。理论上马尔可夫边界是最优特征子集，但实践中因果发现算法恢复的边界往往不如全部特征。研究发现这源于三个问题：发现算法优化结构恢复而非预测性能、假阳性和假阴性的预测代价不对称、以及存在许多超越全特征集的替代特征集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究马尔可夫边界在实际预测任务中是否真的有用——理论承诺最优，但实际中模型仍在使用全部特征，需要实证检验这一差距并分析原因。

Method: 在SCM3K（3450个任务，特征数40-1000，6种SCM家族）上，使用6种回归器，比较三种设置：使用真实马尔可夫边界、使用因果发现恢复的边界、使用全部特征。

Result: 使用真实马尔可夫边界显著提升预测性能，且特征空间越大越稀疏时提升越明显。但通过因果发现恢复边界后效果不佳：现有算法在计算预算内无法达到边界最有用的高维稀疏区域，且即使运行完成也很少优于全特征集。

Conclusion: 马尔可夫边界的预测潜力被因果发现与预测目标的错位所限制，需要发展面向预测对齐的特征选择方法和能利用因果结构的表格模型。

Abstract: Under standard graphical assumptions, the Markov boundary of a target variable is the smallest set of features that renders every other feature redundant. Once the boundary is observed, the target is conditionally independent of the rest of the table. This is a tempting object for tabular prediction, since it names exactly the columns a model should need. Yet modern regressors are still trained on the full feature set. We ask whether the Markov boundary is genuinely useful for prediction on SCM3K, a 3,450-task synthetic SCM benchmark with feature counts from 40 to 1000 and six SCM families, evaluated with six regressors. The answer is more nuanced than the theory suggests. Restricting a regressor to the oracle boundary often improves prediction substantially, and the improvement grows as the feature space becomes larger and sparser. But the natural pipeline of recovering the boundary with causal discovery and training on the recovered mask does not deliver. Existing estimators exhaust the compute budget before reaching the regime where the boundary helps most, and even where they run they rarely beat the full feature set. We trace this to three causes. Discovery optimizes structural recovery rather than prediction. False negatives and false positives carry sharply asymmetric predictive cost. The exact boundary is only one of many feature sets that beat all features. We then develop what these facts imply for prediction-aligned feature selection and for tabular models that learn to use causal structure.

</details>


### [229] [The Sample Complexity of Multiclass and Sparse Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29645)
*Liad Erez,Fan Chen,Alon Cohen,Tomer Koren,Yishay Mansour,Shay Moran,Alexander Rakhlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对随机i.i.d.情境下的上下文多臂赌博机问题，聚焦于奖励向量的$L_1$范数不超过$s \ll |A|$的稀疏设定，设计了两种互补算法，以$\tilde{O} ((s/ε^2 + |A|/ε)\log |Π|/δ)$的样本复杂度输出ε-最优策略，并证明了匹配的下界（对数因子内最优），显著改进了先前工作中额外的$Θ(|A|^9)$依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究（Erez等人, 2024, 2025）在稀疏奖励设定下的样本复杂度存在$Θ(|A|^9)$的额外依赖，本文旨在消除这一差距，达到最优样本复杂度。

Method: 采用两种互补方法：1) 基于决策估计系数（DEC）的探索-优化算法，利用稀疏奖励诱导的模型类得到紧致DEC界；2) 基于低方差探索技术的更具体、可处理的算法，并自然扩展到上下文组合半赌博机设定。

Result: 实现了$\tilde{O} ((s/ε^2 + |A|/ε)\log |Π|/δ)$的样本复杂度，并证明了对数因子内的匹配下界；在上下文组合半赌博机和多臂赌博机列表分类中也获得了改进的样本复杂度保证。

Conclusion: 本文通过两种互补方法，在稀疏奖励设定下达到了最优样本复杂度，填补了先前工作的理论空白，并为实际可处理算法提供了新途径。

Abstract: We study contextual bandits in the stochastic i.i.d.\ setting, where a learner observes contexts drawn from an unknown distribution, selects actions from a finite set $A$, and aims to identify an approximately optimal policy from a given class based on bandit feedback. Motivated by bandit multiclass classification with zero-one rewards, we focus on the \emph{$s$-sparse} setting in which, for every context, the reward vector has $L_1$-norm at most $s \ll |A|$. Our main result is the design of algorithms that, with high probability, output an $ε$-optimal policy compared to policy class $Π$ using $\tilde{O} ((s/ε^2 + |A|/ε)\log |Π|/δ)$ samples. We extend this bound to general Natarajan classes and complement it with a matching lower bound (up to logarithmic factors), thereby closing a substantial gap left by prior work (Erez et al., 2024, 2025), which incurred an additional $Θ(|A|^9)$ dependence. We obtain these results via two complementary approaches. First, we analyze contextual bandits through the lens of contextual decision making with structured observations, designing an exploration-by-optimization algorithm whose sample complexity is governed by the \emph{decision-estimation coefficient} (DEC; Foster et al., 2021, 2022). We show that, with $s$-sparse rewards, the induced model class admits a sharp DEC bound that scales with $s$ and directly yields the optimal rate. Since this approach is largely information-theoretic and involves solving complex min-max optimization problems, we also develop a second, more specialized algorithmic method based on a low-variance exploration technique. This approach leads to concrete, tractable algorithms and naturally extends to contextual combinatorial semi-bandits, leading to improved sample complexity guarantees for bandit multiclass list classification.

</details>


### [230] [CB-SLICE: Concept-Based Interpretable Error Slice Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29836)
*Yael Konforti,Mateo Espinosa Zarlenga,Elaf Almahmoud,Mateja Jamnik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CB-SLICE方法，利用概念瓶颈模型（CBM）中可解释的概念预测来识别错误切片，相比现有方法能提供更准确、更丰富的模型错误解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有错误切片发现方法（SDMs）生成的解释与模型推理过程脱节，只能近似估计错误来源，准确性不足。

Method: 利用CBM中下游任务失败源于概念预测错误这一特性，提出CB-SLICE方法，将共享概念预测失败的样本分组，并识别导致每个切片失败模式的关键概念。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，CB-SLICE在发现已知偏差方面优于现有最先进方法，且提供更丰富、更忠实的模型错误解释。

Conclusion: 基于CBM的概念级解释为错误切片识别提供了更精确、更可解释的途径，CB-SLICE有效连接了错误来源与模型推理过程。

Abstract: Despite strong average-case performance, deep learning models often exhibit systematic errors on specific population groups, known as error slices. Identifying these groups and the root causes of their failures is critical for model debugging and bias mitigation. However, existing error Slice Discovery Methods (SDMs) typically generate explanations disconnected from the model's inference process, thus only approximating the underlying error source and may be inaccurate.
  We address this limitation by leveraging Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs), whose predictions are directly dependent on human-understandable semantic concepts. Since downstream task failures in CBMs commonly arise from concept mispredictions, concept representations provide a strong candidate for error slice identification, offering fine-grained explanations directly linked to the error source. Building on this insight, we introduce CB-SLICE, a concept-based SDM that groups samples with shared concept prediction failures and identifies the keyword concepts most responsible for each slice's failure mode. Across multiple benchmarks, we show that CB-SLICE outperforms state-of-the-art methods in uncovering well-known biases while providing richer and more faithful explanations of model errors.

</details>


### [231] [Open Problem: Separating Geometric and Algorithmic Compression via Cayley-Table Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29885)
*Dongsung Huh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现代深度学习缺乏对算法复杂度最小化的归纳偏置，提出凯莱表补全作为离散代数版的矩阵补全测试基准，并挑战学界将连续平坦性先验推广到可自主发现离散算法公理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在连续域表现优异，但在精确算法规则或离散代数规则的泛化上持续失败，反映出缺乏面向算法复杂度最小化的归纳偏置。作者希望建立类似矩阵补全的离散代数测试基准，推动该方向研究。

Method: 提出凯莱表补全作为规范测试床；算子值张量分解结合平坦性先验可产生隐式算法偏置（趋向精确离散结合性）；将连续平坦性先验推广到自主发现离散算法公理作为待解决问题。

Result: 当前结果表明算子值张量分解+平坦性先验可产生隐式算法偏置；但凯莱表补全的精确恢复理论界尚未建立，是开放问题。

Conclusion: 凯莱表补全是研究离散代数泛化的关键测试基准；需要理论界建立精确恢复保证，并开发能自主发现算法公理的连续先验方法。

Abstract: Modern statistical learning theory and deep learning characterize generalization primarily in terms of continuous capacity control (e.g., norm-based regularization, margin maximization, low-rank bias). While highly successful in continuous domains, deep learning consistently fails to extrapolate exact algorithmic or discrete algebraic rules, reflecting a missing inductive bias toward algorithmic complexity minimization. We propose the Cayley-table completion as the canonical testbed for this missing bias, serving as the discrete algebraic counterpart to matrix completion. Just as matrix factorization combined with weight decay yields an implicit geometric bias toward low linear rank, recent results demonstrate that operator-valued tensor factorizations paired with a flatness prior yield an implicit algorithmic bias toward exact discrete associativity. We pose the open problem of establishing formal exact recovery bounds for Cayley-table completion, and challenge the community to generalize continuous flatness priors to autonomously discover broader discrete algorithmic axioms without combinatorial search.

</details>


### [232] [Ridge Regression from Poisson Resetting: A Renewal Perspective on Spectral Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30059)
*Petar Jolakoski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了随机重置（非平衡统计物理）与岭回归（统计学习）之间的联系。对于线性梯度流，以速率r重置到原点产生的稳态均值恰为岭估计量。文章将该恒等式扩展到一般更新重置律，证明指数重置时间分布是在每个特征方向上都精确再现标量岭滤波的唯一规律，而非指数更新律则生成替代谱滤波器。在波动层面，通过Ornstein-Uhlenbeck扩展研究了随机梯度下降近似，此时仅在均值层面等式成立，而重置过程存在非零稳态协方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立随机重置这一非平衡统计物理机制与统计学习中岭正则化之间的理论联系，揭示两者在数学结构和滤波特性上的深层对应关系。

Method: 利用岭回归与梯度流指数时间平均之间的拉普拉斯变换关系，将指数时间重新解释为泊松重置过程相关的稳态年龄；进一步扩展到一般更新重置律，分析其生成的谱滤波器；通过添加Ornstein-Uhlenbeck噪声扩展研究波动层面的性质。

Result: (1) 线性梯度流下，以速率r泊松重置到原点产生的稳态均值恰为岭估计量 (X⊤X + rI)^{-1}X⊤y；(2) 指数重置分布是在每个特征方向上都精确再现标量岭滤波的唯一更新律；(3) 非指数更新律生成不同的替代谱滤波器；(4) 在添加OU噪声的扩展中，均值层面的等式仍然成立，但重置过程具有非零稳态协方差，而确定性岭估计是固定中心估计。

Conclusion: 随机重置机制为岭正则化提供了一种非平衡统计物理的解释视角，指数重置时间的唯一性揭示了岭回归在谱滤波中的特殊地位；非指数重置律可产生预测性能不同于岭回归的替代滤波方法。

Abstract: We connect stochastic resetting from non-equilibrium statistical physics with ridge regularization in statistical learning. For linear gradient flow, resetting to the origin at rate $r$ produces stationary mean $(X^\top X+rI)^{-1}X^\top y$, exactly the ridge estimator with penalty $λ=r$. This uses the known Laplace-transform relationship between ridge regression and exponential-time averaging of gradient flow, with the exponential time now interpreted as the stationary age associated with Poisson resetting. We then extend this identity to general renewal reset laws: the exponential reset time distribution is the unique renewal law whose stationary mean reproduces scalar ridge in every eigendirection as an exact filter identity for every positive curvature, while non-exponential renewal laws generate alternative spectral filters. At the fluctuation level, we study a separate additive Ornstein-Uhlenbeck extension with constant diffusion, interpreted as a stylized SGD approximation. In this setting, the equality holds only at the level of the mean, since the reset process has a nonzero stationary covariance from accumulated OU noise and reset-timing variance, whereas deterministic ridge is a fixed estimator with the same center. Stylized experiments compare the deterministic renewal-induced filters directly and illustrate when filters induced by non-exponential reset-time laws can differ predictively from ridge. The results for the stationary mean and the induced spectral filters are established for continuous-time gradient flow with isotropic resetting on quadratic objectives; the covariance and risk formulas additionally assume additive noise with state-independent covariance.

</details>


### [233] [Learning to Extrapolate to New Tasks: A Relational Approach to Task Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30132)
*Adam Ousherovitch,Yixin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出关系型任务外推器(RTE)，通过将目标任务分解为已知锚定任务和变换关系，学习任务间的变换算子，从而实现从训练任务到未见任务的外推。在函数预测和序列预测任务中，RTE在参数外推、长度外推和组合外推上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代学习系统在插值上表现优异，但难以泛化到训练分布支撑集之外的未见任务，即使在简单场景（如处理超出训练范围的任务参数）下也会失败。这促使作者探索如何实现系统性外推到新任务。

Method: RTE将每个目标任务分解为一个已知的锚定任务和一个连接锚定与目标的变换，然后学习一个关系算子，将锚定-变换对映射到目标任务的预测结果。

Result: 在函数预测的多项任务外推场景（参数外推、长度外推、组合外推）以及序列预测任务中，RTE显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 外推本质上是关系性的——通过学习任务间的变换关系，模型可以将训练中学到的变换应用于连接已知任务与未见任务，从而实现系统性的任务外推。

Abstract: Modern learning systems excel at interpolation but struggle to generalize to unseen tasks outside the training distribution's support. This failure occurs even in simple settings, such as handling task parameters beyond the training range, and persists despite advances in foundation models. To this end, we develop the Relational Task Extrapolator (RTE), an algorithm designed to enable systematic extrapolation to novel tasks. The key observation is that extrapolation is inherently relational: extrapolating to unseen tasks requires learning how tasks transform into one another. If a model learns the transformation between tasks A and B during training, it can apply that same transformation to relate known tasks to unseen ones at test time. RTE operationalizes this idea by decomposing each target task into a known anchor task and a transformation linking the anchor and target. It then learns a relational operator, mapping an anchor-transformation pair to predictions for the target task. We instantiate RTE across multiple task extrapolation regimes in function prediction, e.g. where target tasks use out-of-range parameters (parameter extrapolation), have greater compositional depth (length extrapolation), and/or recombine function primitives in unseen ways (compositional extrapolation). We further extend RTE to sequence prediction, integrating it into fine-tuning algorithms for foundation models. Across empirical studies, we find that RTE substantially outperforms existing approaches on extrapolation to novel, unseen tasks.

</details>


### [234] [CalArena: A Large-Scale Post-Hoc Calibration Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30188)
*Eugène Berta,David Holzmüller,Francis Bach,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个大规模、标准化的后验校准基准，涵盖近2000个实验，系统评估了数十种校准方法，发现平滑校准函数优于基于分箱的方法，专用多类方法在高维场景中至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后验校准方法众多但缺乏大规模、一致性的评估，现有研究规模小且评价不一致，难以确定哪些方法在实践中真正有效。

Method: 构建标准化基准，涵盖表格数据和计算机视觉任务中的二分类、多分类和大规模分类场景，聚合经典模型、现代深度学习架构和基础模型的预测，统一实现数十种校准方法，并提出基于Proper Scoring Rules的Post-Hoc Improvement (PHI)作为评估指标。

Result: 平滑校准函数优于基于分箱的方法；专用多类方法在高维场景中必不可少；通用机器学习模型缺乏校准特定设计时竞争力不足。

Conclusion: PHI是评估后验校准方法的有效替代指标；研究揭示了跨领域的普适规律；开源了所有数据、代码和评估工具以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Reliable probability estimates are critical in many machine learning applications, yet modern classifiers are often poorly calibrated. Post-hoc calibration provides a simple and widely used solution, but the large number of proposed methods, combined with small-scale and inconsistent evaluations, makes it difficult to determine which approaches are truly effective in practice. We introduce a large-scale, standardized benchmark for post-hoc calibration, covering nearly 2000 experiments across tabular and computer vision tasks, including binary, multiclass, and large-scale classification settings. Our benchmark aggregates predictions from a diverse set of classical models, modern deep learning architectures, and foundation models, and provides unified, reproducible implementations of dozens of calibration methods within a common evaluation framework. We argue that Post-Hoc Improvement (PHI) in proper scoring rules offers a principled alternative to traditional calibration error estimators for comparing post-hoc methods, capturing both calibration quality and potential degradation to the model's predictive performance. Using this framework, we conduct the most comprehensive empirical study of post-hoc calibration to date. Our results reveal consistent patterns across domains: smooth calibration functions outperform binning-based approaches, dedicated multiclass methods are essential in high-dimensional settings, and generic machine learning models are not competitive without calibration-specific design. To facilitate future research, we release all data, code, and evaluation tools, providing a plug-and-play benchmark for developing and comparing calibration methods.

</details>


### [235] [Statistical Embeddings for Similarity, Retrieval, and Interpretable Alignment of Numeric Tabular Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30289)
*M. Ross Kunz,John Merickel,Keith Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过结构化探索性数据分析描述符和典型相关分析来嵌入和匹配异构数值表格数据集的框架，在15个数据集上取得0.9的P@1分数，支持差分隐私和可解释的跨数据集对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型缺乏在异构特征空间中有意义地表示数值表格数据集的原生机制，现有方法要么需要共享变量定义，要么缺乏可解释的跨数据集对齐能力。

Method: 通过结构化探索性数据分析描述符表征数据集，使用预训练句子变换器嵌入到共享向量空间，利用典型相关分析(CCA)量化跨数据集相似性，并采用带惩罚项的CCA恢复稀疏可解释的变量级对应关系，可选差分隐私保护。

Result: 在15个数据集（通用基准、材料信息学、核级石墨表征）上总P@1分数达0.9，最近邻检索和聚类结构在嵌入消融和差分隐私预算下保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 该框架为将异构数值数据集成到检索增强生成(RAG)流程中提供了一条原则性路径，可直接应用于数据驱动的算法选择和未知数据集的模拟模型初始化。

Abstract: Numeric tabular datasets are the dominant data format in scientific practice, yet large language models lack native mechanisms for representing numeric datasets in a meaningful way across heterogeneous feature spaces. Existing approaches either target predictive modeling over individual datasets, which requires a shared set of variable definitions, or lack mechanisms for interpretable cross-dataset alignment. The proposed methodology characterizes numeric tabular datasets through structured exploratory data analysis descriptors, embeds those descriptors into a shared vector space using a pretrained sentence transformer, and quantifies cross-dataset similarity via Canonical Correlation Analysis (CCA). Furthermore, a penalized formulation of CCA is applied to recover sparse, interpretable variable-level correspondences between datasets, identifying which statistical descriptors or variable-level quantities drive cross-dataset alignment without requiring shared variable names or feature conventions. Differential privacy is optionally applied to the descriptor set prior to embedding, supporting deployment in sensitive data contexts without requiring access to raw observations at time of comparison. The methodology is evaluated across 15 datasets spanning general-purpose benchmarks, materials informatics, and nuclear-grade graphite characterization. Results demonstrate a total P@1 score of 0.9, with known nearest-neighbor retrieval and cluster structure remaining robust across embedding ablations and differential privacy budgets. The proposed framework provides a principled pathway for integrating heterogeneous numeric data into retrieval-augmented generation pipelines while preserving statistical context, with direct applications to data-driven algorithm selection and simulation model initialization for unknown datasets.

</details>


### [236] [TRACER: Persistent Regularization for Robust Multimodal Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29380)
*Hesam Asadollahzadeh,Feng Liu,Christopher Leckie,Sarah M. Erfani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TRACER框架，通过理论分析证明加权移动平均（WMA）教师比标准指数移动平均（EMA）教师更能避免崩溃问题，结合对比学习与多视角蒸馏，有效提升了多模态模型微调中的分布外鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型微调时普遍存在灾难性遗忘问题，导致分布外鲁棒性下降。现有正则化方法（如EMA教师）存在未被充分认识的局限性——标准EMA教师会发生崩溃，亟需理论指导下的改进方案。

Method: 首先建立多模态对比微调的理论框架，推导出闭式解和几何分解；理论证明加权移动平均（WMA）教师能在有限时间窗口内保持持续正则化力，并在任务子空间实现无偏收敛同时保留正交知识；基于此提出TRACER，结合对比学习与WMA引导的多视角蒸馏。

Result: 在CLIP微调的大规模实验中，TRACER在三种骨干架构上持续提升了分布外准确率和校准性能；全面的消融实验验证了方法的原则性和对超参数的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TRACER提供了一种理论和实践兼顾的多模态模型鲁棒微调方案，通过WMA教师解决EMA崩溃问题，有效缓解灾难性遗忘，提升了模型的分布外泛化能力。

Abstract: Mainstream strategies for finetuning pretrained multimodal models often degrade out-of-distribution (OOD) robustness, a phenomenon known as catastrophic forgetting. In this paper, we develop a theoretical framework for multimodal contrastive finetuning, yielding closed-form solutions and a geometric decomposition for each strategy. This framework shows that self-distillation is more effective than other regularization approaches to retain the knowledge of the pretrained model. Our analysis reveals a largely overlooked limitation: standard Exponential Moving Average (EMA) teachers, widely used in robust finetuning, suffer from collapse. To solve this, we prove that a Weighted Moving Average (WMA) teacher maintains a persistent regularizing force over finite horizons and yields bias-free convergence in the task subspace while preserving orthogonal knowledge. These insights motivate **TRACER** (**T**rajectory-**R**obust **A**nchoring for **C**ontrastive **E**ncoder **R**egularization), which combines contrastive learning with WMA-guided multi-perspective distillation. Extensive experiments on CLIP finetuning demonstrate consistent OOD accuracy and calibration gains across three backbone architectures, and comprehensive ablations confirm that TRACER is both principled and robust to hyperparameter choices. Code is available at [https://github.com/HesamAsad/TRACER](https://github.com/HesamAsad/TRACER).

</details>


### [237] [LoRe: Adaptive Interaction-Evaluation Routing with Per-Step Interaction Budgets for Iterative Graph Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29005)
*Jintao Li,Yong-Yi Wang,Zheng-An Wang,Heng Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoRe是一种无需训练、推理时即插即用的方法，通过动态预算分配仅评估高冲突/高不确定性的交互，显著提升扩散神经组合优化求解器的可扩展性，在MIS和TSP问题上实现高达15倍加速和44倍内存缩减，且保持解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散的神经组合优化求解器在每次迭代中重复评估密集的边/因子交互，导致推理耗时高、内存受限，难以扩展到大规模问题。

Method: 受多体物理计算方法启发，LoRe在每个迭代中仅评估固定比例的交互，通过动态路由计算到高冲突或高不确定性的交互上，而非使用静态稀疏化策略（如固定kNN图或静态掩码）。

Result: 在MIS问题上，可扩展性提升3倍以上，约8倍加速，约12倍峰值内存降低，解质量保持；在TSP上（n=1000），约15倍加速，44倍内存降低，竞争性路径质量。同时具有跨任务泛化能力和零样本拓扑偏移鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LoRe作为一种训练无关、即插即用的推理时间优化方法，能有效突破扩散组合优化求解器的可扩展性瓶颈，在保持解质量的同时大幅提升计算效率和内存效率。

Abstract: Diffusion-based neural solvers for combinatorial optimization repeatedly re-evaluate dense edge/factor interactions, making inference expensive in wall-clock time and often memory-bound at scale. Inspired by the computational methodologies of many-body physics, we introduce LoRe, a training-free, inference-time drop-in wrapper that enforces per-step interaction-evaluation budgeting: at each iteration, it evaluates only a fixed fraction of interactions by dynamically routing computation to high-conflict or high-uncertainty interactions, instead of using a fixed sparsification (e.g., static kNN graphs or static masks). Under fully inclusive end-to-end wall-clock accounting, LoRe substantially improves scalability on the Maximum Independent Set (MIS) problem, extending feasible inference more than $3\times$ beyond the baseline's out-of-memory limit, delivering a $\sim 8\times$ speedup and a $\sim 12\times$ peak-memory reduction, with solution quality preserved in this regime. Demonstrating cross-task generality on the large-scale Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP) and zero-shot robustness to topology shifts, LoRe achieves a $\sim 15\times$ speedup at $n=1000$ with a $44\times$ memory reduction and competitive tour quality.

</details>


### [238] [When and How Long? The Readout-Mediator Angle in Temporal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29126)
*Shreyas Fadnavis,Praitayini Kanakaraj,Felix Wyss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 线性探针能够近乎完美地解码表征，但其方向可能与模型实际使用的因果计算完全正交。本文通过日历日期推理任务发现，sin/cos探针可从激活中恢复"年积日"，但消融该方向不影响模型输出；而通过分布式对齐搜索（DAS）找到的四维子空间被消融后模型性能完全崩溃。两个子空间之间的夹角与随机子空间之间的夹角（哈尔均匀零假设）不可区分，说明探针学到了与模型实际计算正交的方向。电路逆向工程揭示原因：注意力头通过±30和±61天的学习QK偏移路由月份粒度信息，MLP将绝对时间转换为持续时间——这一切都在探针从未触及的因果子空间下游完成。稀疏自编码器分解证实了这一分裂。该解离现象在1.5B-9B四种规模和两个模型家族中复现，并在空间位移和符号算术两个领域有初步证据，表明读出头-中介体正交性是探针可解释性的一种通用失败模式，直接削弱了将探针作为运行时安全监控器的提议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示线性探针在解释模型内部计算时的根本性局限——探针可能学习到与模型实际因果路径正交的表征方向，从而对模型行为产生误导性解释。这一发现直接挑战了基于探针的可解释性方法及其在安全监控等高风险场景中的应用。

Method: 在日历日期推理任务中，对比线性探针（sin/cos探针）与分布式对齐搜索（DAS）找到的子空间；通过测量两个子空间之间的夹角并与随机子空间（哈尔均匀零假设）比较；进行消融实验验证因果相关性；通过电路逆向工程揭示注意力头（±30和±61天QK偏移）和MLP的机制；使用稀疏自编码器分解探针对齐与DAS对齐的特征；跨四种模型规模（1.5B-9B）和两个模型家族进行复现，并扩展到空间位移和符号算术领域。

Result: 探针可完美解码年积日信息但消融该方向不影响模型性能；DAS子空间被消融后模型性能完全崩溃；两个子空间的夹角与随机子空间夹角不可区分；注意力头通过±30和±61天的QK偏移路由月份信息，MLP将绝对时间转换为持续时间；稀疏自编码器揭示探针对齐和DAS对齐的特征编码语义不重叠的概念；该现象在四个规模和两个模型家族中复现，并在另外两个领域有初步证据。

Conclusion: 线性探针的可解释性存在根本性缺陷：探针可能学习到与模型实际因果计算正交的方向，从而产生完全误导的解释。这种"读出头-中介体正交性"是探针可解释性的通用失败模式，直接削弱了将探针作为运行时安全监控器的提议，因为探针可能报告高置信度于一个模型已经默默放弃的方向。

Abstract: A linear probe can decode a representation almost perfectly and yet be completely irrelevant to how the model uses it. On calendar-date duration reasoning in language models, a $\sin$/$\cos$ probe recovers day-of-year from a layer's activations, yet ablating its direction has no effect on the model's answers -- while ablating a four-dimensional subspace found by Distributed Alignment Search (DAS) at the same layer collapses performance entirely. We measure the angle between these two subspaces -- the \emph{readout-mediator angle} -- and find it indistinguishable from the angle between two random subspaces (the Haar-uniform null), meaning the probe has learned a direction orthogonal to the model's actual computation. Reverse-engineering the circuit reveals why: attention heads route month-grained context through learned QK offsets at ${\pm}30$ and ${\pm}61$ days, and MLPs then convert \emph{when} (absolute date) into \emph{how long} (duration) -- all downstream of the causal subspace the probe never touches. Sparse-autoencoder decomposition confirms the split: probe-aligned and DAS-aligned features encode semantically disjoint concepts with negligible causal overlap. The dissociation replicates across four scales ($1.5$-$9\,$B) and two model families, with preliminary evidence on two further domains (spatial displacement, symbolic arithmetic), suggesting that readout-mediator orthogonality is a general failure mode of probe-based interpretability. This directly undermines proposals to deploy probes as runtime safety monitors: the probe can report high confidence on a direction the model has silently abandoned.

</details>


### [239] [Treatment-Conditioned Diffusion for Forecasting Neurodegenerative Disease Progression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29932)
*Danylo Boiko,Viktoriia Mishkurova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种治疗条件扩散框架，通过基于Transformer的编码器建模药物动态，并利用多权重ROI掩码优化，从患者DaTscan影像和左旋多巴等效日剂量预测未来高保真脑状态，在帕金森病进展预测中MSE降低14.0%、MAE降低7.2%、SSIM提高4.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统仅产出标量临床评分，忽略了纵向神经影像的丰富结构；传统生成方法则丢失解剖细节并模糊细微进展模式。

Method: 采用治疗条件扩散框架，以DaTscan影像和一年内左旋多巴等效日剂量为条件；使用Transformer编码器表征非线性时间依赖的药理学动态；通过多权重ROI掩码聚焦生物学关键区域以优化生成过程。

Result: 与基线相比，MSE降低14.0%，MAE降低7.2%，SSIM提高4.9%，保持锐利解剖边界并显著提升临床保真度。

Conclusion: 该框架能够生成高保真未来脑状态，在解剖细节保留和临床指标上均优于现有方法，有望为神经退行性疾病个性化干预提供支持。

Abstract: Forecasting the progression of neurodegenerative diseases, such as Parkinson's disease, is essential for effective long-term planning and personalized therapeutic intervention. Existing systems typically produce scalar clinical scores that ignore the rich structure of longitudinal neuroimaging, while traditional generative approaches suffer from a loss of anatomical details and blurring subtle progression patterns. To address this, we introduce a novel treatment-conditioned diffusion framework that predicts high-fidelity future brain states by conditioning the generative process on patients' screening DaTscan images and levodopa equivalent daily dose over one year. The pipeline uses a Transformer-based encoder to represent non-linear, time-dependent pharmacological dynamics and optimizes generation through a multi-weight region-of-interest mask that focuses on biologically critical areas. Experimental evaluation shows that our framework maintains sharp anatomical boundaries and significantly improves clinical fidelity relative to the baseline, achieving 14.0% lower MSE, 7.2% lower MAE, and 4.9% higher SSIM.

</details>


### [240] [Improving Adversarial Robustness of Attribution via Implicit Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29983)
*Amir Mehrpanah,Matteo Gamba,Hossein Azizpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现标准SGD学习动力学可以隐式提升归因鲁棒性，而无需显式正则化；但softmax归一化下的注意力归因无法获得这种鲁棒性增益，而核注意力可恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习可解释性中归因的对抗鲁棒性是基本要求，但现有方法依赖计算昂贵的显式正则化，缺乏高效且原理性的机制。

Method: 通过参数空间与输入空间曲率之间的理论联系，证明标准SGD学习动力学可隐式提升归因鲁棒性；并证明softmax归一化下的注意力归因因熵约束无法获得此增益，提出核注意力替代方案。

Result: 在多种架构、数据集和归因方法上验证了隐式鲁棒性增益，计算开销可忽略；同时实验证实softmax注意力归因的鲁棒性局限，核注意力可恢复该增益。

Conclusion: 学习动力学是实现鲁棒可解释性的一种原理性且实用的机制，同时揭示了注意力归因在归一化下的根本性局限。

Abstract: The adversarial robustness of attributions is a fundamental requirement for reliable explainability in deep learning, yet existing approaches typically rely on computationally expensive explicit regularization. In this work, we show that attribution robustness can arise implicitly from the learning dynamics of standard stochastic gradient descent. We theoretically motivate this effect through connections between parameter-space and input-space curvature, and validate it across architectures, datasets, and attribution methods, with negligible computational overhead. In contrast, we prove that such robustness gains often does not transfer to attention-based attribution under softmax normalization, due to inherent entropy constraints, and we validate this limitation experimentally. Finally, we show that replacing softmax attention with kernel-based attention restores the robustness gains in transformer models. Our results highlight learning dynamics as a principled and practical mechanism for robust explainability, and reveal fundamental limitations of attention-based attribution under normalization.

</details>


### [241] [Parallax: Parameterized Local Linear Attention for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29157)
*Yifei Zuo,Dhruv Pai,Zhichen Zeng,Alec Dewulf,Shuming Hu,Zhaoran Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Parallax，一种参数化的局部线性注意力机制，将softmax注意力的局部常数估计升级为局部线性估计，通过硬件感知算法实现高效推理，并在0.6B和1.7B规模预训练中实现Pareto改进，首次展示了注意力机制中架构-优化器协同设计（Muon优化器）的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力机制（softmax attention）本质上是局部常数估计，在偏差-方差权衡上存在理论次优性；而局部线性注意力（LLA）虽理论上更优，但因计算复杂度和数值稳定性问题无法扩展到LLM预训练。

Method: 1）参数化LLA：用可学习的类query投影器替代LLA中的数值求解器，用于探测KV协方差；2）硬件感知算法：提高算术强度，使注意力进入更计算密集（compute bound）的区间；3）解码内核在多种batch size和上下文长度下匹配或超越FlashAttention 2/3。

Result: 在0.6B和1.7B规模预训练中，Parallax在困惑度上持续优于基线，优势延续到下游任务；在参数匹配和计算匹配控制下均实现Pareto改进；发现Muon优化器能解锁Parallax的潜力。

Conclusion: Parallax是首个可扩展至LLM预训练的局部线性注意力机制，验证了注意力机制的架构-优化器协同设计范式的有效性，为超越softmax attention提供了新路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have become the central paradigm in artificial intelligence, yet the core computational primitive of attention has remained structurally unchanged. Local Linear Attention (LLA) is an attention mechanism derived from nonparametric statistics in the test-time regression framework. In contrast to prior research on efficient attention variants, LLA upgrades the local constant estimate in softmax attention to a local linear estimate, yielding provably superior bias-variance tradeoffs for associative memory. However, LLA has not been scaled in LLM pretraining due to computational and numerical stability concerns. We introduce Parallax, a parameterized Local Linear Attention that is scalable for LLMs. Parallax eliminates the numerical solver in LLA and learns an extra query-like projector that probes the KV covariance. We place Parallax within a family of attention mechanisms connected by the bandwidth, the probe construction and the affine structure. We propose a hardware-aware algorithm that increases the arithmetic intensity over FlashAttention, shifting attention into a more compute bound regime. Our prototype decode kernel matches or outperforms FlashAttention 2/3 across diverse batch sizes and context lengths. We pretrain Parallax at 0.6B and 1.7B scales and find consistent perplexity improvements throughout pretraining with gains that transfer to downstream benchmarks. The advantage persists under both parameter-matched and compute-matched controls, demonstrating a Pareto improvement. We perform careful pretraining ablations and identify a novel phenomenon whereby Muon unlocks the capacity of Parallax. To our knowledge, this is the first empirical demonstration of strong architecture-optimizer codesign for attention mechanisms in the architecture research literature.

</details>


### [242] [Evolutionary Refinement of Generative Graph Topologies: A Hybrid WGAN-GA Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29161)
*James Sargant,Seyedeh Ava Razi Razavi,Renata Dividino,Sheridan Houghten*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在现有GAN图生成框架基础上，使用遗传算法（GA）对生成图的边进行后处理优化，以减少生成图与真实图在度和谱分布等结构属性上的偏差，实验表明GA优化能持续降低MMD，生成更接近真实结构的图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GAN-based图生成方法虽然在边建模和类条件密度分布匹配上有所改进，但生成的图在度和谱分布等关键结构属性上与真实图仍存在明显偏差，需要进一步优化以保留重要的结构特性。

Method: 在GAN框架（生成器生成节点特征和连接模式，GNN判别器评估图真实性和类一致性）基础上，引入遗传算法对生成图的边进行进化优化，引导合成图向真实数据分布靠近，同时保持多样性和新颖性。

Result: GA优化持续降低组合MMD（最大均值差异），使生成图在度和谱分布等结构属性上更接近真实图的模式。

Conclusion: 进化优化（GA）是一种有效且灵活的后处理方式，能够纠正GAN图生成器中残留的结构偏差，提升模型在真实图合成和数据增强中的适用性。

Abstract: Generating realistic graph-structured data is challenging due to discrete connectivity, varying graph sizes, and class-specific structural patterns. Recent Generative Adversarial Networks (GAN)-based graph generation methods improve edge modelling by learning connectivity and matching class-specific density distributions. However these models still exhibit noticeable deviations such as in degree and spectral distribution when compared to real graphs, indicating that important structural properties are not fully preserved. This work aims to reduce these deviations by refining the graphs produced by an existing GAN-based graph generator framework with a Genetic Algorithm (GA). In the GAN framework, the generator produces both node features and connectivity patterns, while a GNN-based critic evaluates graph realism and class consistency to ensure global structural and class alignment. Building on this foundation, we apply a GA to refine the edges of generated graphs. The refinement process guides synthetic graphs toward closer agreement with real data, while preserving diversity and novelty. Experimental results show that the GA refinement consistently lowers combined Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) compared to the base model, leading to graphs that more closely match real structural patterns. This demonstrates that evolutionary refinement is an effective and flexible way to correct residual structural deviations in GAN-based graph generators, improving their suitability for realistic graph synthesis and data augmentation.

</details>


### [243] [TIMEGATE: Sustainable Time-Boxed Promotion Gates for Continual ML Adaptation Under Resource Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29183)
*Abhijit Chakrabroty,Suddhasvatta Das,Kevin A. Gary,Yash Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TIMEGATE是一个管理ML系统持续适应过程中预算（时间、标注、训练、评估）的策略层，通过发出可用性信号M来控制部分或完整评估决策，在多个实验中实现了显著的评估计算节省（最高89%）且无静默错误提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML系统需要持续适应，但每次重新训练循环都消耗大量计算、标注和能源资源，缺乏有效的预算管理机制来控制这些成本。

Method: 提出TIMEGATE策略层，通过设定时间、标注、训练和评估的预算来管理适应过程，发出度量可用性信号M来决策执行部分评估还是完整评估。

Result: (i)标注在Adult表格数据上比训练效果好2.3倍；(ii)在LLaMA-3.1-8B+QLoRA+SST-2上准确率从0.80提升至0.96，35/36次运行中M=1；(iii)M信号具有信息量，28单元敏感性分析显示紧阈值下M降至0.81；(iv)100循环仿真实现66%评估计算节省且无静默错误提升；(v)在单H200上10%切片评估减少89%墙钟时间和能耗，比率一致至0.2%。

Conclusion: TIMEGATE能有效管理ML持续适应的资源预算，通过部分评估策略大幅降低计算和能源成本，同时保证评估质量。

Abstract: As machine learning(ML) systems evolve to continual adaptation, each re-training cycle uses compute, annotation, and energy. We introduce TIMEGATE, a policy layer managing adaptation by budgeting time, labeling, training, and evaluation. TIMEGATE emits a metric-availability signal M for partial vs. full-evaluation decisions. We validate: (i) labeling outperforms training by 2.3x on Adult tabular; (ii) it transfers to LLaMA-3.1-8B + QLoRA on SST-2 (accuracy 0.80 to 0.96; M =1 in 35/36 runs); (iii) M is informative, 28-cell sensitivity shows M drops to 0.81 at tight thresholds; (iv) 100-cycle simulation achieves 66% evaluation-compute savings with no silent mis-promotions; (v) 10%-slice evaluation on LLaMA uses 89% less wall-clock and energy on a single H200 (ratios agree to 0.2%).

</details>


### [244] [Influence-Guided Symbolic Regression: Scientific Discovery via LLM-Driven Equation Search with Granular Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29184)
*Evgeny S. Saveliev,Samuel Holt,Nabeel Seedat,David L. Bentley,Jim Weatherall,Mihaela van der Schaar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为IGSR（影响引导符号回归）的方法，通过结合大语言模型生成候选基函数和基于影响分数的剪枝机制，在蒙特卡洛树搜索框架下实现高效的符号回归，并在多个基准测试和真实生物学数据集上验证了其发现新科学假设的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法使用全局标量指标（如均方误差）引导LLM进行符号回归，无法有效识别方程中各组分的贡献，导致搜索效率低下且反馈信号粗糙。

Method: IGSR将方程发现建模为迭代两步过程：1）LLM生成线性模型的候选基函数ψ_j(x)；2）通过影响分数Δ_j量化每个项对泛化精度的边际贡献，进行影响引导的剪枝，并将此机制集成到蒙特卡洛树搜索中，平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在LLM-SRBench、药代动力学PKPD模型、流行病学模拟和真实基因组数据上均表现优异。在生物学高维数据案例中，IGSR发现了DNA甲基化与RNA聚合酶II暂停之间的新关系，并通过湿实验验证。

Conclusion: IGSR通过细粒度的影响反馈机制显著提升了LLM在符号回归任务中的效率，并具备在真实科学发现中提出可验证新假设的能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a promising avenue for scientific discovery, yet their application to symbolic regression is often constrained by inefficient search strategies and coarse feedback signals. Current methods typically guide LLMs using scalar metrics (e.g., global Mean Squared Error), which fail to identify which components of a proposed equation are driving performance or causing error. We introduce \textit{Influence-Guided Symbolic Regression} (IGSR), a method that frames equation discovery as an iterative two-step process combining diverse term generation with rigorous selection: an LLM generates candidate basis functions $ψ_j(\mathbf{x})$ for a linear model, which are then evaluated using granular influence scores $Δ_j$. These scores quantify each term's marginal contribution to generalization accuracy, enabling an influence-guided pruning process that systematically refines the model structure. Integrating this mechanism into a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) enables navigating the combinatorial search space while balancing exploration of novel functional forms with exploitation of high-influence components. We demonstrate IGSR's effectiveness on a diverse suite of benchmarks, including LLM-SRBench, pharmacological PKPD models, an epidemiological simulation, and real-world genomic data. Notably, we validate the framework's capacity for genuine discovery in a case study using a high-dimensional biological dataset, in which IGSR identified a novel relationship between DNA methylation and RNA Polymerase II pausing; a hypothesis that was subsequently supported via wet-lab experimentation.

</details>


### [245] [BlockBatch: Multi-Scale Consensus Decoding for Efficient Diffusion Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29233)
*Xiaoyou Wu,Cheng-Jhih Shih,Binfei Ji,Yong Liu,Yingyan,Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BlockBatch，一种无需训练的在线推理框架，通过并行执行多个不同块大小的分支来加速扩散语言模型推理。该方法利用块大小多样性，通过置信度门控的token合并、基于领导者的同步和周期性全序列刷新来协调分支，在保持精度的同时平均减少26.6%的去噪NFE，实现1.33倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型在块状推理中面临粒度权衡困境：小块保留局部条件但需多步去噪，大块并行性高但易过早承诺并累积缓存误差。现有加速方法通常为每个请求选择单一块大小，未利用不同块大小之间的互补性。

Method: BlockBatch框架：1) 在批处理前向传播中并行执行多个块大小分支；2) 置信度门控的token合并；3) 基于领导者的分支同步；4) 周期性全序列刷新以将局部块更新重新锚定到全局一致的KV状态。

Result: 在3种代表性dLLM和4个数据集上，BlockBatch平均减少26.6%的去噪NFE，相较于Fast-dLLM实现1.33倍平均端到端加速，同时保持精度。

Conclusion: 块大小多样性是一种实用且此前未被充分探索的面向分支并行dLLM推理的优化轴。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (dLLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising multiple token positions in parallel, offering an attractive alternative to strictly autoregressive decoding. In practice, however, block-wise dLLM inference exposes a difficult granularity trade-off: small blocks preserve local conditioning but require many denoising steps, whereas large blocks expose more parallelism but can make premature commitments and accumulate cache error. Existing acceleration methods typically choose a single block size per request, leaving the complementarity among block sizes unused. We show that block size itself is a useful branching dimension. Different block sizes induce related but non-identical KV-cache trajectories: branches often share an initial prefix, bifurcate at semantically decisive positions, and later agree on syntactically lightweight tokens. Motivated by this structure, we propose BlockBatch, a training-free online inference framework that executes multiple block-size branches for the same request inside a batched forward pass. BlockBatch coordinates these branches through confidence-gated token merging, leader-based synchronization, and periodic full-sequence refreshes that re-anchor local block updates to a globally consistent KV state. Across 3 representative dLLMs and 4 datasets, BlockBatch reduces denoising NFEs by 26.6\% on average and achieves a 1.33$\times$ average end-to-end speedup over Fast-dLLM while preserving accuracy. These results identify block-size diversity as a practical and previously underexplored axis for branch-parallel dLLM inference.

</details>


### [246] [KLAS: Using Similarity to Stitch Neural Networks for Improved Accuracy-Efficiency Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29259)
*Debopam Sanyal,Anantharaman Iyer,Alind Khare,Trisha Jain,Akshay Jajoo,Myungjin Lee,Clayton Kerce,Alexey Tumanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KLAS框架，通过KL散度自动选择最优的模型拼接配置，在给定计算预算下实现精度-效率的更好权衡，相比基线方法最高提升1.21%的ImageNet-1K top-1精度或减少1.33倍FLOPs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型拼接方法主要依赖启发式规则选择拼接配置，导致次优的精度-效率权衡且缺乏泛化能力。作者认为需要显式捕捉和利用预训练模型间的相似性来构建更优的权衡曲线。

Method: 提出KLAS框架，利用中间表示的KL散度自动化和泛化跨模型族的拼接选择。从O(k²n²)种可能的二元拼接中识别最有潜力的配置（k个预训练模型，深度n）。

Result: 在相同微调成本下，KLAS提升了拼接模型的精度-效率曲线。在ImageNet-1K上，相同计算成本下top-1精度提升最高达1.21%，或在保持精度的情况下减少1.33倍FLOPs。

Conclusion: KLAS通过基于KL散度的自动拼接选择框架，有效改进了模型拼接的精度-效率权衡，并且具备跨模型族的泛化能力。

Abstract: Given the wide range of deployment targets, flexible model selection is essential for optimizing performance within a given compute budget. Recent work demonstrates that stitching pretrained models within a model family enables cost-effective interpolation of the accuracy-efficiency tradeoff space. Stitching transforms intermediate activations from one pretrained model into another, producing a new interpolated stitched network. Such networks provide a pool of deployment options along the accuracy-efficiency spectrum. However, existing stitching approaches often yield suboptimal tradeoffs and lack generalizability, as they primarily rely on heuristics to select stitch configurations. We argue that constructing improved accuracy-efficiency tradeoffs requires explicitly capturing and leveraging the similarity between pretrained models being stitched. To this end, we introduce KLAS, a novel stitch selection framework that automates and generalizes stitch selection across model families by leveraging KL divergence between intermediate representations. KLAS identifies the most promising binary stitches from the $O(k^2n^2)$ possibilities for $k$ pretrained models of depth $n$. Through comprehensive experiments, we demonstrate that KLAS improves the accuracy-efficiency curve of stitched models at the same finetuning cost as baselines. KLAS achieves up to $1.21\%$ higher ImageNet-1K top-1 accuracy at the same computational cost, or maintains accuracy with a $1.33\times$ reduction in FLOPs.

</details>


### [247] [LoopFM: Learning frOm HistOrical RePresentations of Foundation Model for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29280)
*Shali Jiang,Hua Zheng,Boyang Liu,Laming Chen,Kenny Lov,Chuanqi Xu,Lisang Ding,Qinghai Zhou,Can Cui,Xiaolong Liu,Xiaoyi Liu,Yasmine Badr,Xin Xu,Jiyan Yang,Ellie Dingqiao Wen,Gerard Jonathan Mugisha Akkerhuis,Chenxiao Guan,Rong Jin,Ruichao Qiu,Xian Chen,Shifu Xu,Zhehui Zhou,Ping Chen,Rui Yang,Haicheng Chen,Xiangge Meng,Song Zhou,Dharak Kharod,Shuyu Xu,Qiang Jin,Qiao Yang,Wankun Zhu,Qin Huang,Yuzhen Huang,Darren Liu,Parish Aggarwal,Hui Zhou,Erzhuo Wang,Shuo Chang,Xiaorui Gan,Wenlin Chen,Santanu Kolay,Huayu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoopFM通过将大模型中间层嵌入作为特征输入小模型，开辟高带宽知识传输通道，显著提升知识迁移率，在工业级系统上相较KD实现约两倍迁移率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统知识蒸馏(KD)将大模型的单标量预测传递给小模型，无法传递大模型学习的丰富中间知识，导致迁移率递减——小模型仅能捕获大模型改进的一小部分。

Method: LoopFM将大模型中间层嵌入结构化后作为下游小模型的输入特征（如用户历史序列），建立高带宽传输通道，无需在服务阶段实时调用大模型推理，且不耦合大模型与小模型的架构。

Result: 在三个公开基准上，LoopFM实现了显著的AUC提升（如TaobaoAd上6%+）；在工业级系统（数十亿样本、万亿参数大模型）上，相较KD将知识迁移率提升约一倍，Y1H1转化率提升+0.5%，Y1H2两次独立上线分别带来+1.03%和+1.22%的转化率提升。

Conclusion: LoopFM是一种与KD互补的高效知识迁移方法，利用离线大模型中间表示实现高带宽传输，在理论和实践上均验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) transfers a single scalar prediction from a large foundation model (FM) to compact vertical models (VMs), suffering from diminishing transfer ratio -- the fraction of FM improvement captured by the VM -- as a single scalar cannot convey the rich intermediate knowledge that larger FMs learn. To address this bottleneck, we propose LoopFM (Learning frOm HistOrical ReP*resentations of FM), a framework that opens a high-bandwidth transfer channel by structuring FM intermediate embeddings as input features (e.g., user history sequence) for downstream VMs, without requiring real-time FM inference at serving and architectural coupling between FM and VM. We provide a theoretical framework for LoopFM with a gain decomposition and transfer-ratio analysis. On three public benchmarks, LoopFM demonstrates strong AUC improvements (e.g., 6\%+ on TaobaoAd) and complementary knowledge transfer capability with KD. On industrial-scale systems (billions of examples, trillion-parameter FMs), LoopFM approximately doubles the knowledge transfer ratio on top of KD, delivering a +0.5\% conversion improvement in Y1H1, and a +1.03\% and +1.22\% conversion improvement from two individual launches respectively in Y1H2.

</details>


### [248] [Do Physics Foundation Models Learn Generalizable Physics? A Bias-Aware Benchmark Across Physical Regimes and Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29283)
*Mengdi Chu,Yang Liu,Ayan Biswas,Han-Wei Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 当前物理基础模型在固定训练分布下的单一平均评分掩盖了其真实泛化能力。本文构建了包含8种物理动力学、3种训练数据混合和25种测试场景的基准，对5种架构、每种4种变体进行60000次测量，发现这些模型是条件性而非通用性泛化者，其能力受物理机制、时间尺度、初始条件、预训练、模型大小和架构等多因素制约。作者认为改进方向应超越规模扩展和数据增加，转向学习更可迁移的物理知识机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理基础模型声称具备通用时空预测能力，但其评估通常在固定训练分布下以单一平均分数衡量性能，无法判断模型是真的学习了可泛化的物理动力学，还是仅在特定设置下表现良好。

Method: 构建包含8种物理动力学、3种训练数据混合（不同分布设置）和25种测试场景（涵盖分布内、分布偏移和分布外）的基准；评估5种物理基础模型架构及每种架构的4种变体（从头训练和三种预训练规模），共计60000次测量。

Result: 当前物理基础模型表现为条件性而非通用性泛化者；其泛化能力取决于物理机制、时间尺度、初始条件设置、预训练、模型大小和架构；改进训练数据分布仅能部分缓解此局限；预训练和规模扩展也无法可靠消除能力偏差。

Conclusion: 改进物理基础模型需要超越简单的规模扩展或数据扩充，转向学习能够更好捕获跨物理机制、时间尺度和分布偏移的可迁移物理知识的机制。

Abstract: Recent physics foundation models claim general spatiotemporal forecasting ability, yet their evaluations often collapse performance into a single average score under a fixed training distribution. This makes it difficult to determine whether a model has learned generalizable physical dynamics or only performs well under particular settings. We construct a benchmark with 8 physical dynamics, 3 training-data mixtures, and 25 test regimes induced by dynamic-scale and initial-condition complexity shifts, covering in-distribution, distribution-shift, and out-of-distribution settings. We evaluate five physics foundation model architectures and four model variants per architecture (scratch and three pretrained sizes), resulting in 60,000 measurements. Our results show that current physics foundation models behave as conditional rather than universal generalists: their generality depends on the physical regime, temporal scale, initial-condition setting, pretraining, model size, and architecture. Improving the training data distribution only partially mitigates this limitation. Pretraining and scaling are also unable to reliably remove their ability biases. We argue that improving physics foundation models requires moving beyond scaling models or expanding data, toward learning mechanisms that better capture transferable physical knowledge across regimes, temporal scales, and distribution shifts.

</details>


### [249] [Forget Less, Generalize More: Unifying Temporal and Structural Adaptation for Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29453)
*Qian Chang,Ciprian Doru Giurcaneanu,Runsong Jia,Xia Li,Guoping Hu,Xiufeng Cheng,Jinqing Yang,Mengjia Wu,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双尺度保持动态（DSRD）框架，通过统一的循环状态编码时间和结构信息，利用自适应衰减核平衡短期响应与长期记忆，在14个真实基准上取得链接预测和节点分类的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态图表示学习方法采用固定的时间衰减方案或预定义的结构传播深度，难以泛化到具有不同交互频率和拓扑特征的图数据上。

Method: 提出DSRD框架，包含两个关键组件：(i) 双尺度自适应保持状态，在单一循环公式中联合建模时间动态和结构传播；(ii) 自适应衰减核，通过可学习的时间敏感参数自动平衡短期响应和长期保持。

Result: 在14个真实世界基准数据集上，DSRD在链接预测和节点分类任务中均取得最先进性能，并在直推和归纳设置下表现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: DSRD通过统一的保持状态框架有效解决了动态图上的时间和结构联合建模问题，理论分析证明了事件级并行聚合与高效循环状态更新的等价性，以及学习动态的稳定性和有界性保证。

Abstract: Representation learning on dynamic graphs requires capturing complex dependencies that evolve across both time and structure. Existing approaches typically adopt fixed temporal decay schemes or predetermined structural propagation depths, limiting their ability to generalize across graphs with diverse interaction frequencies and topological characteristics. We propose Dual-Scale Retentive Dynamics (DSRD), a unified framework that maintains a retentive representation state encoding both temporal memory and structural context. DSRD introduces two key components: (i) a retentive state with dual-scale adaptation that jointly models temporal dynamics and structural propagation within a single recurrent formulation, and (ii) adaptive decay kernels with learnable time-sensitivity parameters that automatically balance short-term responsiveness and long-term retention based on the underlying interaction patterns. We provide theoretical analysis establishing the equivalence between event-wise parallel aggregation and efficient recurrent state updates, as well as stability and boundedness guarantees for the learned dynamics. Extensive experiments on 14 real-world benchmarks demonstrate that DSRD consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance on both link prediction and node classification tasks, with strong generalization across transductive and inductive settings.

</details>


### [250] [Honest Lying: Understanding Memory Confabulation in Reflexive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29463)
*Prakhar Dixit,Sadia Kamal,Tim Oates*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了Reflexion式智能体存在“记忆虚构”故障：智能体存储自信但错误的自我反思，并在多轮尝试中持续依赖这些错误记忆，而非环境中重置的正确任务。作者提出RRR指标检测此问题，并通过程序化失败信号提取替代开放式自我诊断，有效缓解了该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Reflexion风格智能体假设其自我生成的反思性记忆是准确的，即智能体能正确诊断自身失败原因。作者通过实验证明这一假设可能系统性地失败，智能体倾向于存储错误但自信的解释，并在后续尝试中重复依赖这些错误记忆，导致任务无法解决。

Method: 提出反思重复率（RRR）作为基于日志的指标，检测智能体对错误反思内容的重复依赖；通过程序化提取轨迹级失败信号替代开放式自我诊断作为缓解策略。

Result: 在ALFWorld中识别出16个冷冻环境（0/121条反思提及正确目标对象），HumanEval中识别出4个类似案例。缓解方法将正确对象提及率从0%提升至86%，RRR从0.64降至0.10，并解冻了16个冷冻环境中的3个。

Conclusion: 反思性记忆可能会强化而非纠正错误信念，程序化失败信号提取比开放式自我诊断更可靠地指导智能体改进。

Abstract: Reflexion-style agents rely on self-generated reflections as memory, implicitly assuming that agents can accurately diagnose their own failures.We show that this assumption can fail systematically: across ALFWorld and HumanEval, agents store confident but incorrect interpretations of the task and continue acting on them across trials,even though the environment resets to the correct task each time. We call this failure mode memory confabulation and introduce the Reflection Repetition Rate (RRR), a log-based metric that detects repeated reliance on incorrect reflective content.Using RRR, we identify 16 frozen environments in ALFWorld, where 0 of 121 reflections mention the correct target object, and 4 analogous cases in HumanEval. Our mitigation replaces open-ended self-diagnosis with programmatic extraction of trajectory-level failure signals, increasing correct object mention from 0% to 86%, reducing RRR from 0.64 to 0.10, and solving 3 of 16 frozen ALFWorld environments, suggesting that reflective memory can reinforce false beliefs rather than correct them.

</details>


### [251] [Composing Non-Conjugate Factor Graphs with Closed-Form Variational Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29467)
*Mykola Lukashchuk,Kyrylo Yemets,Wouter M. Kouw,Dmitry Bagaev,İsmail Şenöz,Jeff Beck,Bert de Vries*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明由五种因子图基元（双线性因子、指数链接、Gamma先验、高斯似然、等式节点）组成的任意概率模型，在平均场分解下均可保持闭式变分消息传播，并展示了从静态集成到分裂分支路由的深度组合，最终实现通用函数逼近。在时间序列预测中，该方法可推断门控函数而非学习，提供校准的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将概率构建块堆叠成深层架构通常会破坏闭式推理，本文旨在证明闭式推理可以被保留。

Method: 识别五种因子图基元（双线性因子、指数链接、Gamma先验、高斯似然、等式节点），证明在其组合下闭式变分消息传播成立；利用高斯矩生成函数和Gamma充分统计量处理非共轭接口（指数链接）；通过静态集成、输入依赖门控、分裂分支路由展示深度组合。

Result: 堆叠路由层可编码任意决策树，建立具有闭式推理的通用函数逼近；在集成时间序列预测中，框架产生贝叶斯混合专家模型，门控函数被推断而非学习，在五个基准数据集上提供校准的不确定性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够在保持闭式推理的同时实现深度概率组合，为概率编程和贝叶斯深度学习提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: Stacking probabilistic building blocks into deeper architectures typically breaks closed-form inference. We show that closed-form inference can be preserved. We identify five factor-graph primitives: a bilinear factor, an exponential link, a Gamma prior, a Gaussian likelihood, and an equality node, and prove that any model composed from them admits closed-form variational message passing. The construction works because each primitive preserves a small set of message families: under mean-field factorization, messages on Gaussian variables remain Gaussian and messages on precision variables remain Gamma, while the only non-conjugate interface, the exponential link, remains tractable through the Gaussian moment-generating function and the sufficient statistics of the Gamma family. We demonstrate composition at increasing depth, from static ensembles through input-dependent gating to split-branch routing, and show that stacking routing layers encodes arbitrary decision trees, establishing universal function approximation with closed-form inference. Applied to ensemble time-series forecasting, the framework yields a Bayesian mixture of experts in which gating functions are inferred rather than learned, providing calibrated uncertainty over expert selection across five benchmark datasets.

</details>


### [252] [Quotient DAGs for Off-Policy Evaluation:Forward-Flow Importance Sampling and Exact Slate Propensities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29500)
*Ziwen Xie,Shaowen Xiang,Hongyu He,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于商DAG视图的前向动态规划方法（Forward-DP），为自回归板岩推荐系统计算精确的无序倾向性权重，避免阶乘枚举，从而改进离策略评估的方差与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准重要性采样在自回归板岩推荐中存在 nuisance 方差和计算鸿沟：评估目标仅关心无序板岩，但生成过程是有序的，精确的无序倾向性需要枚举所有生成顺序，这既不必要也不可行。

Method: 引入商DAG（quotient-DAG）视图，将评估等价的历史合并，并用目标策略与行为策略在合并图上的前向流比率（forward-flow ratio）分配权重。针对板岩推荐中的集充分下一项接口（set-sufficient next-item interface），提出Forward-DP，一种子集DAG动态规划算法，无需阶乘枚举即可计算精确无序倾向性。

Result: Forward-DP能够为上下文相关的自回归板岩日志器提供精确的无序倾向性原始量，使基于倾向性的评估和模型选择成为可能。

Conclusion: 商DAG框架有效弥合了生成过程与评估目标之间的结构差异，为具有对称性（如无序板岩）的离策略评估提供了通用且高效的计算范式。

Abstract: Off-policy evaluation estimates how a target policy would perform using data collected by a different behavior policy, which is crucial when online testing is costly or risky, such as in recommendation or healthcare. Standard importance sampling reweights each logged trajectory, but it can treat details of the generation process as meaningful even when the evaluation target ignores them: for example, an autoregressive slate recommender may generate an ordered sequence of items while the reward and downstream estimator depend only on the unordered slate. This creates nuisance variance and a computational gap, since exact unordered slate propensities require summing over all generation orders. We introduce a quotient-DAG view that merges histories equivalent for evaluation and assigns weights using target-to-behavior forward-flow ratios on the merged graph. For slate recommendation under a set-sufficient next-item interface, this yields Forward-DP, a subset-DAG dynamic program that computes exact unordered propensities without factorial enumeration. The resulting propensity primitive enables practical propensity-based evaluation and model selection for context-dependent autoregressive slate loggers.

</details>


### [253] [SCOPE: A Lightweight-training LLM Framework for Air Traffic Control Readback Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29543)
*Qihan Deng,Minghua Zhang,Yang Yang,Zhenyu Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOPE框架，通过将开集分类器与上下文学习机制耦合到冻结的LLM上，实现高效准确的ATC复诵异常监测，在少样本设置下达到91.05%的开集检测准确率和96.63%的异常纠正率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞行员复诵异常涉及约80%的航空事故，传统方法和现有LLM方法在泛化性、部署成本和计算效率上存在瓶颈，亟需一种兼顾准确性与低延迟的自动化复诵监测方案。

Method: 提出SCOPE框架，核心思想是在冻结的LLM之上耦合一个开集分类器插件，并结合精心设计的上下文学习机制，实现轻量级训练的高效语义推理。

Result: 在半合成通信数据集上，SCOPE在少样本条件下开集检测准确率达91.05%，异常复诵纠正率达96.63%，同时保持低延迟响应，优于现有最强基线。

Conclusion: SCOPE框架为可解释、可控的ATC复诵监测提供了一条实用路径，展示了轻量级LLM范式在航空安全领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: Pilot readback of Air Traffic Control (ATC) voice instructions is a primary safeguard against miscommunication in air transportation. However, readback anomalies remain implicated in approximately 80% of aviation incidents. This vulnerability is further exacerbated by rising traffic volume and elevated cognitive workload, thereby motivating automated readback monitoring by machine. Traditional rule-based and machine learning approaches struggle to generalize across the highly variable and evolving phraseology of air traffic controller-pilot communications. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have opened a new avenue through their strong reasoning and generalization capabilities, existing approaches still face deployment and computational barriers in practice. In this work, we propose Semantic reasoning for Communication via Open-set Plug-in with Examples (SCOPE), a novel lightweight-training LLM framework that advances both the efficiency and accuracy of machine-based ATC readback monitoring. The core idea is to couple a plug-in open-set classifier with a carefully designed in-context learning mechanism on top of a frozen LLM. Extensive experiments on the semi-synthetic communication dataset show that SCOPE attains superior accuracy while delivering the low-latency response required for operational environments. Under a few-shot setting, SCOPE achieves 91.05% accuracy in open-set detection and corrects 96.63% of anomalous readbacks, thereby outperforming the strongest available baselines while providing explanations for its decisions. These findings demonstrate the potential of our framework as a practical pathway toward interpretable and controllable ATC readback monitoring.

</details>


### [254] [Singularity-aware Optimization via Randomized Geometric Probing: Towards Stable Non-smooth Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29547)
*Ruoran Xu,Borong She,Xiaobo Jin,Qiufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Singularity-aware Adam (S-Adam) 优化器，通过局部几何不稳定性（LGI）度量动态调整步长，解决非光滑损失景观下自适应优化器的梯度抖动问题，在量化感知训练和高噪声小批量学习任务中优于 AdamW 和 Prox-SGD，最高提升 6% 准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习架构（如 ReLU 激活、量化算子）包含非光滑组件，导致损失景观不光滑，传统自适应优化器（如 Adam）在 Clarke 次微分中因梯度信号冲突产生剧烈振荡（梯度抖动），导致收敛性差和泛化能力次优。

Method: 提出局部几何不稳定性（LGI）度量，通过随机方向导数的方差高效估计 Clarke 次微分直径；基于此引入自适应阻尼机制 exp(-λρ)，在高不稳定区域减速更新，在平滑区域保持快速收敛；利用微分包含理论证明算法几乎必然收敛到 (δ,ε)-Clarke 驻点，收敛率为最优的 O(1/√T)。

Result: 在 CIFAR-100 上准确率提升 6%，在 TinyImageNet 上提升 3%，有效缓解梯度振荡，一致优于 AdamW 和 Prox-SGD。

Conclusion: S-Adam 通过感知局部几何不稳定性有效解决了非光滑优化中的梯度抖动问题，为量化感知训练等高噪声场景提供了一种更鲁棒的优化方案。

Abstract: Deep learning optimization relies heavily on the assumption of smooth loss landscapes, a condition systematically violated by modern architectures due to non-smooth components such as ReLU activations and quantization operators. In such non-smooth regimes, adaptive optimizers such as Adam suffer from gradient chattering, violent oscillations caused by conflicting signals within the Clarke subdifferential, leading to poor convergence and suboptimal generalization. To address this, we introduce Singularity-aware Adam (S-Adam), a novel optimizer that stabilizes training by dynamically modulating step sizes based on local geometric instability. Our key contribution is the Local Geometric Instability (LGI) metric, a computationally efficient estimator of the Clarke subdifferential diameter derived from the variance of randomized directional derivatives. S-Adam incorporates an adaptive damping mechanism exp(-$λ$$ρ$) that decelerates updates in high-instability regions while preserving fast convergence in smooth basins. We provide a rigorous convergence analysis using differential inclusions, proving that S-Adam converges almost surely to ($δ$,$ε$)-Clarke stationary points at the optimal O(1/$\sqrt(T)$) rate. Empirical evaluations on Quantization-Aware Training (QAT) and high-noise small-batch learning demonstrate that S-Adam consistently outperforms AdamW and Prox-SGD, achieving accuracy gains of up to 6 percent on CIFAR-100 and 3 percent on TinyImageNet while effectively mitigating gradient oscillations.

</details>


### [255] [Opir: Efficient Multi-Task Safety Classification for Toxicity, Jailbreaks, Hate Speech, and Harmful Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29659)
*Ihor Stepanov,Aleksandr Smechov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Opir，一个基于GLiClass架构的编码器护栏模型系列，用于LLM应用中的实时安全过滤。Opir包含多任务模型（二进制安全/不安全分类、多标签毒性分类、越狱分类、零样本不安全提示与响应分类）以及参数量低于1亿的边缘变体。模型在包含996个类别的三层分类体系上训练，结合了不安全提示、对抗挖掘的硬负样本、良性安全样本等多源数据。在12项安全分类任务和17项类别任务上与8个当代护栏系统对比，Opir在多数基准上达到或超越最强开源基线，且部署 footprint 显著更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全过滤方案面临两难：大型护栏模型成本高、延迟大，而轻量级方案往往精度不足；同时需要区分良性敏感文本与真正的隐蔽有害内容。亟需一种高效、精准且部署成本低的实时安全过滤器。

Method: 基于GLiClass架构的编码器护栏模型族，包含多任务模型和边缘模型；采用三层分类体系（16个顶层/126个中层/854个叶层标签共996类）；训练数据融合多源（对抗挖掘硬负样本、良性安全样本、多语言翻译、Aegis2/WildGuard子集等）；开源评估工具支持多种后端和评测维度。

Result: 在12项安全分类任务和17项类别任务上，与包括GLiNER2和生成式护栏在内的8个系统对比，Opir变体在多数基准数据集上与最强开源基线持平或领先，且部署 footprint 显著更小。

Conclusion: Opir证明了基于编码器的护栏模型能够在保持较高精度的同时实现极低的部署成本，为LLM实时安全过滤提供了高效实用的方案。

Abstract: Real-time safety filtering for large language model (LLM) applications requires classifiers that can detect unsafe prompts, toxic language, jailbreak attempts, and unsafe responses without the cost profile of large guardrail models, and that can distinguish benign sensitive text from genuinely covert harmful content. In this paper, we introduce Opir, a family of encoder-based guardrail models built on the GLiClass architecture. Opir includes multi-task models for binary safe/unsafe classification, multi-label toxicity classification, jailbreak classification, and zero-shot unsafe prompt and response categorization. We also release edge variants with fewer than 100M parameters dedicated to binary safe/unsafe categorization. The models are trained on a three-level taxonomy containing 996 categories across 16 top-level labels, 126 mid-level labels, and 854 leaf labels. Opir's training data combines taxonomy-grounded unsafe prompts, adversarially mined hard negatives, benign safety-preserving examples, generated response examples, multilingual translations, and portions of the Aegis2 and WildGuard training subsets. We also open-sourced an evaluation harness that supports GLiClass and GLiNER2 backends as well as decoder-based models, and covers binary safety classification, multi-label categorization, toxicity, jailbreak detection, prompt safety, response safety, response refusal, and prompt subcategory views across public benchmark families. Across an expanded comparison spanning 12 safety-classification tasks and 17 category tasks against eight contemporary guardrail systems -- including both GLiNER2-based and generative guardrail models -- Opir variants are competitive on or ahead of the strongest open-weight baselines on the majority of benchmark datasets while operating with a substantially smaller deployment footprint.

</details>


### [256] [The Little Book of Generative AI Foundations: An Intuitive Mathematical Primer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29713)
*Tianhua Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本书提供了对现代生成式人工智能数学基础的简洁、面向推导的导论，涵盖从PCA、变分自编码器、扩散模型到归一化流、自回归分解、GANs、Wasserstein GANs和基于能量的模型等主要生成模型家族，旨在揭示它们之间的联系和推导结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献要么过于侧重实现细节，要么缺乏对生成模型家族之间数学联系的清晰梳理，作者希望在不牺牲数学严谨性的前提下，让生成建模的结构性理解变得更容易获取。

Method: 采用推导导向的叙述方式，从PCA和概率PCA出发，依次推导变分自编码器、扩散模型、归一化流、自回归分解、GANs、Wasserstein GANs和基于能量的模型，构建一条连贯的数学路线图。

Result: 形成了一本紧凑的、建立数学基础的入门教材，使读者能够理解生成模型之间的推导关系和结构关联，而非孤立的架构清单。

Conclusion: 本书面向对数学好奇的研究者、从业者和学生，作为建立生成式AI数学基础的入门读物，有助于更深入地理解和研究现代生成模型。
